- **`frontend/src/components/EChart.tsx:22`** — setOption(option, true) uses notMerge=true on every option change. Because `option` is a prop, any parent re-render that creates a NEW option object literal (the common case) triggers a full non-merge replacement — destroying internal interaction state (legend selection, dataZoom window, tooltip) and incurring a full redraw. If callers pass inline object literals this fires far more than intended.
- **`frontend/src/components/EChart.tsx:21-23`** — Referential-identity perf trap: the effect depends on `option` by reference. Callers must memoize the option object or every render re-applies it. There is no useMemo guidance and no shallow comparison; an unmemoized inline `option={{...}}` causes setOption on every render.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:29-34`** — 'Try again' simply resets error to null and re-renders the SAME children. If the underlying error is deterministic (bad prop, null data), it will immediately re-throw and reproduce the fallback — an infinite manual-retry loop with no remount/key-reset or backoff. A `resetKeys`/onReset-with-remount pattern would actually recover; as-is the button is often a no-op for persistent errors.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:15-18`** — componentDidCatch only console.errors — there is no integration with any error-reporting/telemetry service. Production render crashes are invisible beyond the user's own devtools console. (Acceptable for a demo, but a real gap for a compliance product.)
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:51-52`** `[immersive/dead]` — Inconsistent parallax math: rotation.x is eased toward a target (`+= (pointer.y*0.25 - g.rotation.x) * 0.04`, a stable lerp), but rotation.y is INCREMENTED by `pointer.x*0.25*0.01` every frame on top of the continuous spin from line 45. Because it adds rather than easing toward a target, holding the cursor to one side continuously accelerates/biases the Y spin instead of settling at a parallax offset — likely not the intended 'parallax toward cursor' behavior (asymmetric with the X axis).
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:74-85`** `[immersive/dead]` — No reduced-motion handling: the field rotates, breathes, and parallaxes unconditionally. Unlike CustomCursor (which respects prefers-reduced-motion), this continuous animation runs for all users, conflicting with the project's WCAG AAA accessibility bar (2.3.3 Animation from Interactions / motion sensitivity).
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:74-85`** `[immersive/dead]` — No visibility/idle gating: the Canvas + useFrame render loop runs continuously even when the component is offscreen, scrolled away, or the tab is backgrounded (r3f defaults to always-on frameloop). Constant GPU/CPU cost for a purely decorative layer; an on-demand frameloop or IntersectionObserver pause would save power, especially on laptops/mobile.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/ImmersiveLanding.tsx:35`** `[immersive/dead]` — Root container sets cursor:'none' (line 35) unconditionally for the whole viewport. If UVCursor fails to mount, errors, or is disabled for reduced-motion/a11y, the user has no visible pointer at all. This is also an accessibility concern against the project's stated WCAG AAA bar — there is no fallback cursor and no prefers-reduced-motion accommodation here.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:94-105,121`** `[immersive/dead]` — WindDust BufferGeometry is created in useMemo([]) but never disposed. When phase leaves exterior the <points> unmounts, but r3f only auto-disposes geometry it created from JSX args, not a geometry passed via the `geometry` prop. This geometry (and its attribute buffers) leaks on unmount. Add a disposal effect.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:157-165`** `[immersive/dead]` — ReadySignal fires onReady on the FIRST rendered frame, which is not the same as 'all assets loaded'. Lazy FloorScene, textures (useLiveLedger), and the facade may still be streaming when sceneReady flips true, so the Preloader can dismiss before the scene is visually complete. 'Scene ready' here means 'first paint', not 'loaded'.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/context/BuildingContext.tsx:25,109`** `[immersive/dead]` — mouseNDC is exposed as a live mutable ref object but typed in the interface as a plain `{ x: number; y: number }` (line 25). Consumers cannot tell from the type that it is mutated in place and that reading it during render is unsafe/stale. The non-reactive contract is only documented in a comment (50-51), not encoded in the type. A consumer that destructures mouseNDC at render time will capture stale values.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/context/BuildingContext.tsx:76-80`** `[immersive/dead]` — floorPhase builds the phase string via template literal and casts `as BuildingPhase` (line 79). No runtime validation, so passing a FloorId with no matching floor_<id> member (or a future FloorId addition) produces an invalid phase string silently typed as valid. The union and FloorId list must be kept manually in sync; 'lobby'/'rooftop' are special-cased but any new floor relies on the cast being correct.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:62-68`** `[immersive/dead]` — LobbyFloor renders 90 individual tile meshes (6x15) as separate draw calls (lines 63-68) instead of InstancedMesh or a single texture. 90 draw calls just for the checkerboard. Wainscot (12), coffers (32), beams (14), pillars (12 meshes) etc. push the lobby into hundreds of un-instanced draw calls.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:34,38-50`** `[immersive/dead]` — useMemo dependency for tiles is [cols,rows] (line 50) but TILE_SIZE is a local const inside LobbyFloor (line 34) re-declared every render and not in deps — currently fine because it is constant, but if TILE_SIZE ever became dynamic the memo would go stale. The memo also closes over LOBBY_Z/ROOM_D (module constants, fine).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:106-120`** `[immersive/dead]` — The four gold corner brackets are positioned with x = PAGE_W + 0.02 + sx*(PAGE_W/2 - 0.12) (line 111). With PAGE_W=2.1 the base offset is ~2.12, so brackets sit at x≈1.07 and x≈3.17 — centered near the RIGHT page (at x≈1.07) but the right bracket at x≈3.17 is far outside the page edge (right page right edge ≈2.12). The math double-counts PAGE_W and likely renders one bracket floating off the book — looks like a positioning bug.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:43,59-70`** `[immersive/dead]` — useLiveLedger is called but its return is only consumed for the page map; there is no loading/error state and no Suspense — if the hook throws or the canvas/texture fails, the page silently falls back to a blank cream material with no indication. For a 'living' centerpiece, a stuck/blank ledger is a silent failure.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:24-43`** `[immersive/dead]` — Particle init uses useMemo (not useEffect) for a side-effecting assignment to dataRef.current. Works, but conceptually a side effect in render; under React 18 StrictMode double-invoke it runs twice (harmless here, just regenerates). Reading dataRef before this useMemo runs is impossible since it's same render, but the pattern is fragile.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:189-191`** `[immersive/dead]` — Entry is gated on openCountRef.current === 1, but handleDoorOpen is also called by the second door. If the user clicks both doors quickly the second open still flips scatterActive (already true) — harmless — but there is no guard preventing enterBuilding's transition from racing with a second GSAP swing. More importantly there is no way to re-arm after entry; minor since the component unmounts on phase change.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:57`** `[immersive/dead]` — Scale formula `Math.max(0, 1 - Math.max(0, -p.pos.z + DOOR_Z) * 0.1)` is almost certainly wrong. Particles start at z≈DOOR_Z and have positive z velocity (toward camera), so p.pos.z grows > DOOR_Z, making `-p.pos.z + DOOR_Z` negative, clamped to 0 → scale stays 1 (never shrinks). The intended depth-fade likely never triggers; this is dead/ineffective logic.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/HashChainSpine.tsx:67-71`** `[immersive/dead]` — Idle rotation runs forever via useFrame with no pause/visibility gating. 12 links rotating + the seal tweens keep the scene's render loop busy even when the building exterior is off-screen (after entering the building) unless the parent unmounts it. Verify the parent actually unmounts on phase change; otherwise this is wasted GPU each frame.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/HashChainSpine.tsx:29-47`** `[immersive/dead]` — Nested GSAP tweens (color/emissive tweens started inside the onStart of the emissiveIntensity tween) create implicit timing coupling and three concurrent tweens per link. The inner color tween has delay:0 inside onStart so effectively starts at the same `delay` — the nesting is unnecessary indirection and makes the timeline hard to reason about / kill.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:44`** `[immersive/dead]` — Final mix factor `reveal * uvInk.a + uvInk.r * reveal * 0.5` relies on uvInk.r as a mask, but the ink is cyan #7df9ff (r≈0.49) on black — red channel is mid-level for ink and 0 for background, which roughly works, but it's an accidental coupling: any future non-cyan ink (e.g. pure blue or green) would have low/zero red and silently fail to reveal. Mask should use luminance or alpha explicitly.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:127`** `[immersive/dead]` — drawRiskLabUVInk's probability dot grid calls Math.random() at texture-build time, so the 'forensic' dots are non-deterministic and change every time the texture is regenerated (e.g. on remount/StrictMode double-render). Decorative, but means the hidden content is not reproducible and double-renders differ.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:53-63`** `[immersive/dead]` — ShaderMaterial sets no transparent/blending/depth config and the fragment always outputs alpha 1.0. If this material is meant to overlay a base document where the un-revealed areas should show the base unchanged, that works (it samples uBaseMap), but it cannot be layered as a transparent decal over other geometry — the design intent (decal vs full surface) is ambiguous and undocumented.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx:152-177`** `[immersive/dead]` — SHAPScreen draws a 'SHAP FEATURE IMPORTANCE' chart with fully hardcoded bars/labels. The project actually ships a real SHAP-capable risk engine (per repo commit history), so this immersive readout is mock data masquerading as model output — a correctness/authenticity gap if it is ever read as real.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/DetectiveFloor.tsx:261-273`** `[immersive/dead]` — Cork-board frame loop is broken/incomplete: the offset ternaries `(fi===0 ? 0 : 0)` are always 0 (dead arithmetic) and the mapped array values are never used, so both 'frame' pieces are centered identically and only two of the four expected frame edges render. Either dead code or an unfinished frame.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/VaultFloor.tsx:232`** `[immersive/dead]` — `<cylinderGeometry args={[...]} rotation-z={Math.PI/2} />` — rotation-z is applied to the geometry element, which has no transform; it is silently ignored by r3f. The intent was to lay the connecting chain bar horizontally (it should be on the <mesh>). Result: the bar's orientation is wrong/ineffective. Concrete latent bug.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:293-312`** `[immersive/dead]` — CityLightsBelow builds the window grid with Math.random() inline in render (line 295 visibility, line 308 emissiveIntensity) and is NOT memoized. Any re-render of RooftopFloor re-randomizes which windows are lit and their brightness, causing a visible flicker / non-deterministic scene. Should be precomputed in useMemo (as StarPoints correctly does).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:294-300`** `[immersive/dead]` — Window grid uses Math.floor(h/3) rows and Math.floor(w/2.5) cols, then divides by those same floored counts for spacing (e.g. w/Math.floor(w/2.5)). If a building's w<2.5 or h<3, Math.floor yields 0 -> division by zero (Infinity/NaN positions) and Array.from({length:0}) — the current hardcoded data avoids 0 (min w=5,h=18), but it is an unguarded edge case.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx:362-380`** `[immersive/dead]` — ExhibitTab is a motion.button but the expand/collapse uses click only; there is no aria-expanded/aria-controls relationship between tab and card, no focus trap, and no Escape-to-close — an a11y gap given the project's stated WCAG AAA bar.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:167`** `[immersive/dead]` — ExitFlash renders OUTSIDE the AnimatePresence (line 167) and relies SOLELY on onAnimationComplete to navigate, with no timeout fallback. If the framer-motion animation is interrupted (tab backgrounded, reduced-motion, or a re-render unmounting it) onAnimationComplete may not fire and the navigation silently never happens, stranding the user on the flash. No guard/cleanup exists.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:150`** `[immersive/dead]` — On floor change, useFloorData synchronously resets data to EMPTY (summary 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…') then fetches (useFloorData line 151-154). Because the panel is keyed by activeFloor and animates in with delay 0.35, the user can briefly see 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…' on every floor entry even with a fast backend — a perceived-latency flaw. No skeleton distinguishes 'connecting' from 'offline'.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/PaperShredTransition.tsx:92`** `[immersive/dead]` — flyIn early-returns if activeRef is already true. If a teleport is triggered again before flyOut completes (rapid floor switching), flyIn no-ops and the existing pieces/animation persist; combined with tlRef.current?.kill() living only inside flyIn (line 108), overlapping transitions can desync the visual from the actual phase and the 600ms camera window.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/PaperShredTransition.tsx:179`** `[immersive/dead]` — Phase-edge detection fires flyIn whenever phase becomes 'teleporting'. BuildingContext.enterBuilding ALSO sets phase='teleporting' (BuildingContext line 68), so the shred plays on the very first lobby entry, not only on floor-to-floor teleports. If the entry transition is meant to differ, this conflates two journeys — worth confirming against design intent.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/Preloader.tsx:56`** `[immersive/dead]` — Two paths can schedule setTimeout(complete,420): the driver when p>=100 (line 56) and the unblock effect (line 68). complete() is idempotent (completedRef), so it won't double-run, but the orphaned setTimeout from the losing path is never cleared — a dangling timer can fire after unmount, calling clearInterval/setVisible on an unmounted component (React warns).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/Preloader.tsx:48`** `[immersive/dead]` — The driver setInterval lives in an effect with deps [sceneReady, complete]. On sceneReady false→true the cleanup clears the old interval and creates a NEW one, restarting the 155ms cadence; any in-flight setTimeout(complete) survives across the re-subscribe, so the two effects encode overlapping, order-dependent completion logic that is hard to reason about.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx:148`** `[immersive/dead]` — Effect 4 has deps [onComplete]. If the parent passes a non-memoized onComplete (a fresh function each render), the entire GSAP sequence tears down (killed=true, killTweensOf) and restarts from y:-180 on every parent re-render — the intro visibly stutters/restarts. Correctness depends on the caller memoizing onComplete; nothing here guards against that.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx:41`** `[immersive/dead]` — Canvas sized once with no resize handling (effect deps []). If the window resizes during the ~4s intro, the cream fill and ink polygon use stale dimensions and maxR (computed from old cx/cy), leaving uncovered cream edges as the ink expands.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx:57`** `[immersive/dead]` — The prefers-reduced-motion check `window.matchMedia(...).matches` is read during render and returns null, but it is NOT a reactive subscription — if the user toggles reduced-motion at runtime, the cursor won't appear/disappear until an unrelated re-render. Also evaluating matchMedia in the render body conflates a side-effecting query with render. (Hook order is safe because the early return is after all hooks.)
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx:27`** `[immersive/dead]` — Two separate passive mousemove listeners are registered on window — one in Effect 1 (handleMove, which setPos's every move) and one inside useMouseNDC (line 55). Every mouse move runs both handlers and triggers a React re-render for the exact dot, on top of the per-frame halo re-render. The two could be unified.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:101-102`** `[immersive/dead]` — useEffect deps intentionally only [phase] with exhaustive-deps disabled. camera, setPhase, mouseNDC are referenced but omitted. camera/setPhase are stable in practice, but the suppression hides that buildFloorCam allocates new THREE.Vector3 objects on every phase change (L36-41, also called twice per frame in useFrame L122) — minor GC churn each frame.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:122`** `[immersive/dead]` — Perf: buildFloorCam(floorIdx) is called inside useFrame on every animation frame for interior phases, allocating two new THREE.Vector3 every frame (~60/sec). Should hoist a memoized base per phase instead of reallocating.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:21-27`** `[immersive/dead]` — No HTTP status check before r.json() (also L43-49, L65-71): a 404/500 returns a non-JSON or error body and r.json() may throw or yield unexpected shape. studio/risk/detective have no own try/catch and rely solely on the hook-level .catch — a single bad response replaces the whole floor with the offline state even if the endpoint exists.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:152`** `[immersive/dead]` — setData(EMPTY) is called synchronously on every floorId change before the fetch resolves, so the panel flashes 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…' on each navigation even when data returns in <100ms. No debounce / stale-while-revalidate; previous floor's data discarded immediately.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:49`** `[immersive/dead]` — Type holes: ar.value.analyses (also L61,73) iterated as any — a.quality_grade, q.ok, inv.risk_level, a.dataset_id, *.created_at accessed untyped/unguarded. a.dataset_id undefined produces hash seed like 'A5undefined'; created_at missing -> fmtTime('') -> new Date('') Invalid Date -> toISOString throws -> caught -> ''.slice(0,19)+'Z' = 'Z' as the timestamp, then sorted by 'Z'. Silent data corruption rather than error.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:89`** `[immersive/dead]` — Sort uses b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp) on formatted 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SSZ' strings. Works only because the format is lexically monotonic; any entry whose timestamp degraded to 'Z' (see above) or a differing format sorts unpredictably to top/bottom.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:225-231`** `[immersive/dead]` — RAF render loop runs unconditionally for the hook's whole lifetime (~60fps redraw of a 720x490 canvas + texture upload) regardless of whether the ledger is on-screen/visible. No visibility/pause gating; constant GPU texture re-upload (needsUpdate every frame) and CPU repaint even when the floor isn't in view.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts:15-33`** `[immersive/dead]` — The material and its two 512x512 CanvasTextures created in useMemo are never disposed on unmount. No useEffect cleanup calling material.dispose()/baseMap.dispose()/uvMap.dispose(). When the Risk floor remounts (phase changes in/out) this leaks GPU texture + program memory each cycle.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:84-87`** — cursor:none on body/links/buttons for all fine-pointer devices hides the native cursor globally, relying on a JS custom cursor (.land-cursor). If that JS fails to mount, errors, or isn't present on non-landing app routes, the user has NO visible cursor — a serious usability/a11y regression. The reduced-motion block does not restore the cursor either.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:716-723`** — Reduced-motion override is incomplete relative to the project's WCAG AAA bar. The universal *{animation:none;transition:none} catches element animations/transitions, but pseudo-element transitions are only re-stated for .redact::after — .chain-connector::after's scaleX transition is not explicitly listed, and the explicit selector list omits scan-pass/.scan-beam, blink/.cursor-blink, ring-expand/.ring, char-land/.char-anim, stat-rise/.stat-reveal. Worth auditing that every motion path is genuinely suppressed.

### LOW (155)

- **`frontend/index.html:10, 14-17`** — External Google Fonts dependency (line 10) — runtime network call; offline/blocked-CDN renders fall back to system fonts, and there is no <noscript> message for a JS-disabled visitor (blank page).
- **`frontend/src/store/themeStore.ts:17-18`** — Top-level side effects at lines 17-18 (localStorage read + DOM classList mutation + apply) execute on module import, not in a React lifecycle. SSR-unsafe (references document/localStorage at import) — fine for this CSR-only app but a footgun if ever server-rendered, and makes the module impure/harder to test.
- **`frontend/src/store/themeStore.ts:11-31`** — No cross-tab sync: a theme change in one tab is persisted to localStorage but other open tabs are not updated (no window 'storage' listener).
- **`frontend/src/App.tsx:38`** — BrowserRouter has no `basename` (line 38); any deployment under a sub-path (e.g. /app) would break all routing and asset-relative links.
- **`frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx:46, 285`** — sealedNodes uses `[...prev, i]` with no dedupe (line 46). Under StrictMode's double-mount the uncaptured leaked timers from the first run can push duplicate indices, making `sealedNodes.length === 4` (line 285) and connector logic potentially fire early/oddly in dev.
- **`frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx:93-94`** — Logo NavLink points to '/' (line 94) which exits the AppShell layout entirely back to the marketing Landing page; there is no in-app 'home'/dashboard distinction, which can feel like an accidental logout-of-app.
- **`frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx:317-343, 418-422`** — Inline <style> injected on every render (lines 418-422) re-creates a <style> element each render of AppShell; harmless functionally but a minor anti-pattern vs a static CSS rule, and the breadcrumb relies on `current` so a deep unmatched path (line 317) silently renders an empty breadcrumb.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx:24`** — ago() does no validation of the ISO string; an invalid/empty created_at yields new Date(...).getTime()===NaN, making d NaN and falling through every comparison to return 'Nd ago' with NaN. No guard.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx:294`** — iv.chain_head.slice(0,12) assumes chain_head is always a non-null string; if the API omits it, .slice throws. No optional-chaining/fallback (contrast Overview's defensive {} fetch handling).
- **`frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx:56`** — Swallow-all .catch(()=>({})) means backend-down is indistinguishable from genuinely-empty history — user sees the 'No analyses yet' empty state even on a network error, with no error affordance (unlike Risk/NLP/Settings which surface 'Backend unavailable').
- **`frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx:2`** — ShieldAlert imported from lucide-react but never used — dead import.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx:49`** — useRef<number>() initialized with no value (undefined); window.clearTimeout(undefined) on first run is harmless but the ref type is number while the uninitialized value is undefined — minor type looseness.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx:281`** — ECharts tooltip backgroundColor hardcoded to dark rgba(19,17,16,0.96) with light text #e8dfc8 (281/294/307); in light/porcelain theme the tooltip stays dark, inconsistent with the rest of the theme-aware coloring (muted/grid adapt but tooltip does not). a11y/contrast consideration.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx:203`** — TIER_COLOR[a.risk_tier] lookups (203,214) have no fallback; an unexpected tier string yields undefined color (transparent dot/bar) — contrast Overview which uses '|| --text-muted'.
- **`frontend/src/pages/NLP.tsx:83`** — setResult(await res.json()) is not validated against the Result interface; a malformed/partial body lacking signals/entities/regulatory_matches will cause downstream .map/.length to throw (e.g. result.signals.map at 241, result.regulatory_matches.length at 248).
- **`frontend/src/pages/NLP.tsx:184`** — Inline <style>@keyframes spin</style> rendered inside the component tree and re-created on every render; minor — should live in a global stylesheet, and a 'spin' keyframe likely already exists elsewhere in the app.
- **`frontend/src/pages/NLP.tsx:205`** — LEVEL_COLOR[result.risk_level] has no fallback (used at 194/205/223/224); an unexpected level string yields undefined color for the big label/border/badge. Entities legend (283) always renders all 6 ENTITY_COLOR labels even if none appear in the result — slightly misleading as a 'legend' for the actual highlights.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx:137`** — Type hole: (r as any).accent casts away the heterogeneous-array type. Only the GenAI row defines accent (40); the cast suppresses TS and would silently allow typos. The rows array could be a typed union or each row given an optional accent field.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx:36-44`** — While health is loading (and no error yet), rows===[] so the SYSTEM STATUS panel renders just its label with empty body and no loading indicator — a brief blank/janky state. No skeleton/spinner.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx:33`** — fetch(...).then(r=>r.json()) does not check r.ok; a non-2xx health response with a JSON body resolves into setHealth and is rendered as if healthy (per-row ok flags computed from possibly-absent fields), rather than tripping setErr. Only network failures set err.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx:40`** — health.genai.startsWith('llm') assumes genai is always a string; if the API returns null/omits it, .startsWith throws during rows derivation and the whole page fails to render. No guard (contrast database?. optional chaining used one line below).
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:138`** — On forecast fetch failure the catch only clears loading; no user-visible error. Button silently does nothing on failure (body.detail thrown but swallowed).
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:169`** — res.json().catch(()=>({})) then `body.detail` — if JSON parse fails body is {} and detail is undefined, falling back to generic message; if the server returns ok:false with non-JSON the error is opaque.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:145-157`** — pinFromSql column heuristic inspects only rows[0] to infer string/number types; a column null in the first row but numeric elsewhere is mis-typed. valueCol must differ from labelCol or pin silently no-ops with no user feedback.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:21, 28`** — SPAN map and heatmap height 6 are magic numbers; 'kpi' charts are filtered out before buildLayout but SPAN has no 'kpi' key (handled by the ||6 default, harmless).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:160-161`** — Result table headers derive from Object.keys(s.result[0]) and body cells from Object.values(row); heterogeneous key order across rows can misalign columns and values. Assumes s.result[0] exists (guarded by s.result?.length>0).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:30, 87, 140`** — SEV map is keyed by exact 'Critical'|'High'|'Medium'|'Low'. An unexpected risk_level/severity yields undefined → invalid CSS color and `${undefined}1f` background. No fallback.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:128`** — Timeline list uses key={i} (array index). Stable for static trace, fragile if it ever reorders/animates.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:79`** — Raw server error string (body.detail) rendered into the UI. React escapes text so it's XSS-safe, but it leaks raw backend error detail to end users.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:116`** — Pin confirmation uses setTimeout(2500) with no cleanup; unmount within 2.5s fires setState on an unmounted component. Rapid repeated pins stack timers, each resetting the label.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:55`** — translate() sets sql from body.sql without validating it exists/non-empty; a malformed translate response sets the editor to undefined and runs undefined as SQL.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:134-141`** — Result rows use key={i} (index); acceptable for static results but mis-reconciles if the same table re-renders shorter (residual rows). Numeric detection typeof row[c]==='number' fails for numeric strings returned from the DB.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:69-75, 91-102`** — Accessibility: neither the NL input nor the SQL textarea has an aria-label/label; placeholder is the only affordance. Enter handling differs between the two inputs (Enter translates vs Ctrl+Enter runs) with no guard for IME composition / Shift+Enter.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:40, 56-58, 68`** — Cards use key={i} (array index) and `open` tracks an index; if insights ever reorder/filter, the expanded card and animations bind to the wrong item. Stable only because the list is static.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:68-80`** — Expand/collapse asymmetry: the evidence block is mounted via `open === i &&` with an enter animation but no AnimatePresence/exit, so closing snaps instantly while opening animates height 0→auto.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:56-67`** — Accessibility: the expand toggle is a <button> with no aria-expanded / aria-controls, and the revealed evidence has no programmatic association — fails the project's stated WCAG AAA bar for disclosure widgets.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:49`** — Severity→color mapping is binary ('warning'→danger red, anything else→teal). An unexpected severity value silently maps to the 'info' teal styling; no visual distinction for other levels.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:41-43`** — Quality ring grade <text> hardcodes fontFamily='JetBrains Mono' instead of var(--font-mono); if the design token font changes or the font isn't loaded this diverges from the rest of the UI.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:25, 84`** — Color thresholds (gradeColor) and ROLE_COLORS are hardcoded hex rather than design-system tokens, so these specific colors don't follow theme changes (ring/grade and role badges are fixed regardless of light/dark theme).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:24, 65-67, 83-100`** — profile is typed `any`; every field access (row_count, memory_kb, columns[].missing_pct/unique_count/mean/semantic_type) is unchecked. A missing/renamed field throws at render (e.g. profile.memory_kb.toLocaleString() on undefined) with no guard or fallback.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:95`** — Completeness bar width = `${100 - col.missing_pct}%`; if missing_pct exceeds 100 or is negative (bad data) the bar width becomes negative/over-100% with no clamp. The >20 danger threshold is a magic number.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx:57-58`** — .filter((d)=>d.name) is effectively dead/no-op: name is `ISO2[...] || d.label`, which is always a truthy non-empty string for any normal label, so unrecognized country codes are NOT filtered out — they are passed to ECharts as unknown map names and silently ignored by the renderer (no honest 'unmapped' feedback to the user).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx:13`** — ISO2['KP'] = 'Korea' is incorrect/ambiguous. KP is North Korea; ECharts world.json uses 'Dem. Rep. Korea'. Value will silently not render. South Korea 'KR' is also missing entirely from the table.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx:18,33`** — Module-level mutable singleton `_worldLoaded` is shared global state. In dev StrictMode double-invoke or across tests it can mask re-registration logic; also if the first fetch fails (state 'fallback') the flag stays false so every subsequent mount re-attempts the fetch.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:51`** — symbolSize computes node degree with edges.filter(...).length inside nodes.map — O(N×E). Fine for the small graphs implied by the strict |r|/η² thresholds, but a latent perf trap if thresholds loosen. Precompute a degree map once.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:57-68`** — links.map spreads `...e` AND then overwrites `label` (string from edge) with a structured ECharts label object, and re-sets source/target to the same values. The spread carries arbitrary raw props (weight/sign/kind plus the original string `label`) into the ECharts link, relying on the trailing structured `label` to win — fragile and the reason for the `as any` cast.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:68`** — `as any` cast defeats type checking on the links array; a malformed edge (e.g. missing weight) would silently produce NaN width (1 + undefined*5) with no compile-time guard.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx:34-43`** — Autoplay interval restarts whenever `playing` or `frames.length` changes. When playback reaches the end it calls setPlaying(false) from inside the setIdx updater (line 38) — calling a sibling state setter inside another setter's callback works because React batches, but is slightly surprising. No loop-back to start when finished.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx:38`** — When playback is at the last frame and the user presses Play, the interval fires once, i+1 >= frames.length is immediately true, so it sets playing false and never advances — pressing Play at the end is a no-op with a brief 900ms flicker of the pause icon. Should reset idx to 0 on play-from-end.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx:35`** — window.clearInterval(timer.current) runs with timer.current === undefined on the first non-playing effect run; harmless (clearInterval tolerates undefined) but relies on that behavior. timer ref typed useRef<number>() initialized undefined.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx:37`** — Delta color/arrow uses v >= 0 => success/up, so a 0% change renders green with an up-arrow (and '0%'), which misrepresents 'no change' as a positive. Should treat 0 as neutral.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx:35`** — Delta component is declared inside the WhatChanged render body, so it's a new component identity each render; React unmounts/remounts its subtree on every parent re-render (minor, but avoidable — hoist it out of the component).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx:69`** — Mover list uses array index `i` as React key. Stable here because movers is replaced wholesale per fetch, but index keys are an anti-pattern if the list ever reorders/animates.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:607-610`** — HowItWorks uses setTimeout(i*140) to reveal step cards but never stores/clears the timeout ids; unmounting within the ~420ms stagger window runs callbacks against detached DOM nodes.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:688-700`** — LedgerCTA creates the setInterval BEFORE the `if (!el) return` guard; on the null-chainRef path the cleanup that clears the interval is never returned, so the interval leaks. Edge case.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:265, 306, 432`** — Inconsistent magic transaction count: hero/StudioMock use '284,507' while RiskMock (306) and SectionRisk (434) use '284,807'. 284,807 is the real Kaggle creditcard dataset size; the other is a typo — undermines the 'no fabricated metrics' brand claim.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:191, 709, 726`** — 'Wolter Kluwer' misspelled — the real compliance vendor is 'Wolters Kluwer' (with an s). Appears in three user-visible places.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:341`** — Dead code: MOCKS registry object defined but never referenced anywhere.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:50-86`** — a11y/perf: global window 'scroll' (Nav) and 'mousemove' (Cursor) listeners plus the perpetual RAF run regardless of prefers-reduced-motion. Unlike Hero.tsx/LedgerSections.tsx, Landing.tsx never checks prefers-reduced-motion, so scanner animation, pinned scroll-jacking, marquees and custom cursor all run for reduced-motion users (WCAG 2.3.3 / the AAA bar in PRODUCT.md).
- **`frontend/src/sections/Hero.tsx:39`** `[immersive/dead]` — Non-null assertion ref.current! in onMouseMove — type hole; fine at runtime but unsafe pattern.
- **`frontend/src/sections/Hero.tsx:111`** `[immersive/dead]` — var(--cyan, #22d3ee) fallback hardcoded inline in two spots (caret + scroll arrow) rather than a guaranteed token.
- **`frontend/src/sections/LedgerSections.tsx:174`** `[immersive/dead]` — Non-null assertion imgWrap.current! in ExhibitPhoto onMouseMove — type hole; relies on the handler only firing while mounted.
- **`frontend/src/sections/LedgerSections.tsx:109, 140`** `[immersive/dead]` — Math.random()-based hash/typing is cosmetic but HashTicker rolls a new 64-char string every 90ms indefinitely while mounted (Rehearsal every 28ms) — continuous re-render churn. Both correctly gated on prefers-reduced-motion and cleaned up; noted as constant background work.
- **`frontend/src/sections/LedgerSections.tsx:178`** `[immersive/dead]` — img has loading='lazy' and alt={ex.title} (good), but the loupe magnifier is mouse-only with no keyboard/touch affordance — 'HOVER TO INSPECT' is inaccessible to keyboard/touch users (AAA bar).
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx:117-119`** — groupRef.current.rotation accessed in useFrame without a null check (the ref is typed null! and never guarded, unlike the Node refs). Safe under R3F mount ordering but a latent null-deref if the scene is ever used outside that guarantee.
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx:92`** — FraudEdge ref typed as useRef<any>(null) — type hole; loses type safety on .material.dashOffset. Should type as the drei Line/Line2 ref.
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx:70`** — Color inconsistency: fraud red here is #ff4d5e but Landing.tsx's brand RED is #c2331f, and the FraudNetSection legend swatch uses Landing's RED — so the legend's 'Fraud path' swatch (#c2331f) does not match the actual rendered edges (#ff4d5e).
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/ParticleField.tsx:98-101`** — useFrame runs an unconditional 7200-particle GPU update every frame with no prefers-reduced-motion / visibility gating. Moot while unused, but if revived it animates for reduced-motion users and when offscreen.
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/ParticleField.tsx:68-84`** — POSITIONS/RANDOMS are module-singletons computed at import time, so all ParticleField instances share one immutable geometry — cannot vary or re-seed per instance. Intentional but a hidden coupling.
- **`frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx:29`** — Per-mousemove DOM traversal: every mousemove fires el.closest(...) on the event target. On deep DOM trees this runs at full pointer-event frequency with no throttle/rAF batching — minor perf cost, and setHovering will re-render the component on every hover-state transition.
- **`frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx:31`** — Listener attached to `window` without `{ passive: true }`. Not a scroll/touch listener so impact is negligible, but passive is the safer default for high-frequency pointer listeners.
- **`frontend/src/components/EChart.tsx:11`** — Hardcoded renderer 'canvas' with no SVG option and no `theme` (undefined). Fine as a default, but not configurable via props — callers cannot opt into SVG rendering or a theme without editing this wrapper.
- **`frontend/src/components/EChart.tsx:9-19`** — No global window 'resize' fallback. The ResizeObserver covers container-size changes (the right approach), but device-pixel-ratio changes (e.g. dragging the window between monitors with different DPR) are not observed and won't trigger a crisp re-render.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:27`** — The raw error.message is rendered directly into the UI. For an internal tool this is fine, but surfacing raw exception text to end users can leak implementation details / stack-adjacent info and is not localized.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:23-35`** — Accessibility: the error card has no role="alert" / aria-live region, so screen readers are not notified when a view crashes and the fallback appears. The decorative AlertTriangle icon also lacks aria-hidden. Falls short of the project's WCAG AAA bar.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:29-34`** — The 'Try again' button uses inline styles only and lacks any :focus-visible / hover styling defined here, and there is no keyboard-focus management when the boundary trips (focus is not moved to the alert), reducing keyboard/AT usability.
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:76-83`** `[immersive/dead]` — No aria/role on the Canvas and no aria-hidden; the decorative WebGL canvas is exposed to the accessibility tree with no label. Should be aria-hidden='true' since it is purely ornamental.
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:76`** `[immersive/dead]` — No WebGL-context-loss handling and no fallback if WebGL is unavailable/blocked. If context creation fails the background silently disappears (and could throw inside r3f); a static gradient fallback would be more robust. Pairs well with wrapping this in the ErrorBoundary primitive.
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:10`** `[immersive/dead]` — COUNT=2600 points with sizeAttenuation + additive blending + dpr up to 2 is a fixed cost regardless of device; there is no quality/COUNT scaling for low-end or mobile GPUs, so the same particle budget runs everywhere.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/ImmersiveLanding.tsx:45-52`** `[immersive/dead]` — Intro components are gated purely on local boolean state (preloaderDone/stampDone) that only ever moves forward. If phase were ever reset to 'preloading'/'stamp_intro' elsewhere, the overlays would not re-appear, so the context phase and these local flags can desynchronize. The single source of truth is split between local state and context.phase.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/ImmersiveLanding.tsx:40-42`** `[immersive/dead]` — No error boundary around BuildingScene. A throw inside the r3f tree (e.g. a failed lazy FloorScene chunk) would unmount the whole landing with no fallback UI; the cursor:none page would be left blank.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:133-139`** `[immersive/dead]` — FloorArrivalPulse arrival detection depends on prevRef being exactly 'teleporting' immediately before the floor phase. enterBuilding() in the context also goes teleporting -> lobby, so entering the lobby triggers the flash too (likely intended). But goToFloor()/exitFloor() transition WITHOUT going through 'teleporting', so direct floor switches that bypass teleportTo never flash — inconsistent arrival feedback depending on which navigation path was used.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:24-30`** `[immersive/dead]` — Sun directional light at [10,8,18] has its shadow ortho frustum fixed at ±30 with far=150, but the building/lobby interior extends to z≈-47 and the ground is 400 wide; objects outside the ±30 frustum (city silhouettes, distant ground) cast no shadows and may show abrupt shadow-map cutoff. Magic frustum not matched to scene extent.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:17-31`** `[immersive/dead]` — Two 2048x2048 shadow maps plus ~13 point lights downstream (chandelier 8, sconces 4, pedestal, arrival, lobby fill) — none of the point lights cast shadows (default false) so cost is bounded, but the standalone-material + per-pendant pointLight pattern in the lobby combined with this rig is heavy for low-end GPUs. dpr is capped at 2 which helps but there is no quality tier / reduced setting.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/context/BuildingContext.tsx:82-101`** `[immersive/dead]` — Navigation paths are inconsistent: enterBuilding and teleportTo animate through 'teleporting' (600ms), but goToFloor and exitFloor switch phase instantly with no transition and no isTransitioning flag. Callers must know which to use to get the shred animation; exitFloor jumps to lobby with no visual transition, which looks abrupt vs teleportTo.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/context/BuildingContext.tsx:73,100`** `[immersive/dead]` — The 600ms transition duration is a magic number duplicated in enterBuilding and teleportTo and must match the PaperShredTransition animation timing (300+300 per the comment) defined in a different file. No shared constant, so the timer and the CSS/animation can drift out of sync.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:105-117,151-167`** `[immersive/dead]` — Nested Array.from(...).map(...) returning arrays of meshes inside JSX (e.g. wainscot 105-117) relies on React flattening nested arrays; keys are unique but the pattern is fragile/hard to read. CofferedCeiling wraps each coffer in an extra <group> (153) with a key but no transform — 32 unnecessary empty group nodes.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:8`** `[immersive/dead]` — LOBBY_X constant (line 8) is declared but never referenced — dead constant.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:268-272`** `[immersive/dead]` — The 'VERITA' branding on the reception desk (268-272) is a solid box, not text geometry, so it renders as a black rectangle, not the brand wordmark. Real Text/TextGeometry or a texture is needed — current state is a placeholder.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:47-51`** `[immersive/dead]` — Idle-float useFrame sets bookRef.current.position.y to an absolute sin value (line 49) rather than relative to a base position. Fine now because the book group's base y is 0, but any future base-y offset on the inner group would be overwritten every frame — the animation owns the absolute y, which is brittle.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:53`** `[immersive/dead]` — Misleading stale comment at line 53 claims coverMat is the texture-not-ready fallback; it is actually the right-page cover material used unconditionally.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:15-18,47-51`** `[immersive/dead]` — No prefers-reduced-motion handling for the continuous idle float (book bob/sway) or the pulsing pedestal light. Against the project's WCAG AAA goal, perpetual decorative motion with no reduced-motion opt-out is an accessibility concern.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/BuildingFacade.tsx:140-142`** `[immersive/dead]` — VeritaSignMesh creates a THREE.CanvasTexture in useMemo but never disposes it (no useEffect cleanup calling tex.dispose()). If BuildingFacade unmounts/remounts (e.g. phase changes in/out of exterior), GPU texture memory leaks one texture per mount. Same applies to the WINDOW_GLOW/STONE module-level materials, though those are intentionally app-lifetime singletons.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/BuildingFacade.tsx:5-11`** `[immersive/dead]` — Seven module-level shared MeshStandardMaterial singletons are mutable and shared across all instances. Fine while static, but any future per-mesh tweak (e.g. hover tint) mutating one of these would affect every mesh using it. Also they are created at module-eval time even if the facade never renders.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:102-106`** `[immersive/dead]` — GSAP tween on groupRef.current.rotation is not killed on unmount. If the component unmounts mid-swing (phase transition to teleporting at 320ms, well before 850ms swing completes) GSAP keeps animating a detached object — minor leak/no crash, but no gsap.killTweensOf cleanup in any effect.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:67`** `[immersive/dead]` — instancedMesh args={[undefined, undefined, PARTICLE_COUNT]} relies on r3f to inject geometry/material from children; passing undefined for geometry/material is the documented pattern but TS-loose. Fine functionally.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:157`** `[immersive/dead]` — DoorFrame creates a new MeshStandardMaterial on every render outside any memo. Rarely re-renders so cheap, but inconsistent with DoorPanel's memoized material and not disposed.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/HashChainSpine.tsx:37-39`** `[immersive/dead]` — Gold color is hardcoded as raw normalized RGB floats (0.658, 0.518, 0.173) with a comment '#a8842c'. Brittle and duplicated from the GOLD material in BuildingFacade; if the brand gold changes these floats silently drift. Should derive from a shared THREE.Color('#a8842c').toArray() or token.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/HashChainSpine.tsx:75`** `[immersive/dead]` — torusGeometry uses low tessellation (radialSegments 8) so links look faceted; intentional perhaps, but each of 12 links creates its own geometry instance (no sharing) — could be instanced/shared for perf.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:108,110`** `[immersive/dead]` — Fonts reference 'JetBrains Mono' / Georgia by name with no font-load guarantee. If the webfont isn't loaded when the canvas is rasterized, the browser silently falls back to a default mono/serif, changing the hidden-ink appearance. No document.fonts.ready await before drawing.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:78-80`** `[immersive/dead]` — makeUVInkTex sets RepeatWrapping but never sets needsUpdate (CanvasTexture sets it on construct, OK) and does not set t.colorSpace. For authored color content THREE r152+ defaults textures to no/linear handling depending on usage; cyan may render slightly off without SRGBColorSpace. Also the returned texture is never disposed by this helper (caller's responsibility, undocumented).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:84-144,108-114`** `[immersive/dead]` — Hardcoded mock forensic data ('RISK SCORE: 0.847', 'FRAUD PROBABILITY: 94.2%', 'ANOMALY CLUSTER: K=7', 'CHAIN HEAD: 0xA8F2C3D1'). These look like real model/chain outputs but are fixed strings — a reviewer/screenshot could mistake them for live data. Worth a comment marking them as decorative, or wiring to real values.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/FloorScene.tsx:23`** `[immersive/dead]` — Suspense fallback is null — while a floor chunk downloads the room is empty (no loading mesh/spinner). Acceptable for small chunks but a visible gap on slow networks.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/StudioFloor.tsx:1-2`** `[immersive/dead]` — useRef and useFrame are imported but never used — dead imports (lint should flag). StudioFloor is fully static with no animation.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/StudioFloor.tsx:179-184`** `[immersive/dead]` — Floor-border loop maps over `[[0,RW,0.14],[0,RW,0.14]]` but never reads the tuple values — only index i is used. The array contents are dead; intent unclear (looks like leftover from a refactor).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/StudioFloor.tsx:56`** `[immersive/dead]` — shapes.slice(0, 2 + seed): for any DraftingTable with schematicIdx>2 this would request more than the 3 available shapes (slice clamps, so no crash, but the visual stops varying). Magic coupling between seed and shape count is fragile.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx:39-44`** `[immersive/dead]` — makeEvidenceTex markers array mixes number and string element types in a single tuple, forcing Number(x)/Number(y)/String(lbl) coercions. A typed tuple ([number,number,string]) would avoid the runtime casts and the implicit any[] inference.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx:152`** `[immersive/dead]` — SHAPScreen's texture useMemo has [] deps and ignores `position`; fine, but two SHAPScreens each build their own identical 256x160 canvas instead of sharing one texture — minor redundant canvas allocation.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx:180`** `[immersive/dead]` — SHAPScreen <mesh position={position}> passes the [number,number,number] tuple directly as the position prop (works in r3f) while sibling components destructure into a <group> — inconsistent style, harmless.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/DetectiveFloor.tsx:261`** `[immersive/dead]` — The mapped array is typed `as number[][]` and its element contents are entirely ignored — dead data plus a loose cast that hides the no-op.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/DetectiveFloor.tsx:201`** `[immersive/dead]` — PendantLamp pointLight has castShadow plus a second castShadow pointLight per lamp (two PendantLamps) on top of the central light — multiple shadow-casting point lights are expensive on the GPU; combined with other floors' shadow lights this can be a perf trap on weaker devices.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/VaultFloor.tsx:39-43`** `[immersive/dead]` — Each ArchiveShelf builds 20 CanvasTextures in useMemo, and there are four shelves => 80 offscreen 128x64 canvases generated on every mount of the Vault floor. With FloorScene unmount/remount on phase change these are recreated each visit (no caching/dispose), a memory/GC churn trap.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/VaultFloor.tsx:202-206`** `[immersive/dead]` — linksRef.current is a plain array populated via ref callbacks but never reset/cleared on unmount; combined with remounts this is mostly harmless (GC'd with component) but the stale-index pattern is fragile if LINKS ever changes between renders.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/VaultFloor.tsx:159-165, 195`** `[immersive/dead]` — Four TorchSconces each run a per-frame useFrame mutating a pointLight position+intensity (4 dynamic lights) plus other floor point lights — several simultaneous dynamic point lights with decay is a known r3f/three perf cost, notable on a deliberately dark scene.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:47, 195, 137`** `[immersive/dead]` — SkyDome sphere radius 195 vs StarPoints r up to 180 vs CityLightsBelow z down to -100 and distant light distance 100 — several large hardcoded world-scale magic numbers that must stay mutually consistent with the building's 12-unit floor stacking; no shared constant documents this relationship.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:39-41`** `[immersive/dead]` — makeSkyTex sets wrapS=RepeatWrapping but never sets repeat or wrapT, and the texture is applied to a sphere with default UVs — the RepeatWrapping has no visible effect (dead configuration).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:1`** `[immersive/dead]` — Like other floors, useRef is used but the file also follows the shared pattern; here it IS used (glowRef), so fine — but note SkyDome/StarPoints/CityLightsBelow each allocate large geometry/textures regenerated on every floor remount with no dispose().
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx:239-252`** `[immersive/dead]` — The visible/return-null pattern unmounts the component entirely outside lobby/floor phases. Because the outer slide-in animation has delay:0.6 (line 265), every time the user returns to a qualifying phase the 280px slide replays with a 0.6s delay; on rapid floor switching the nav repeatedly re-slides in.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx:401`** `[immersive/dead]` — Case number 'V-{year}-001' is hardcoded to '-001' and recomputes new Date() on every render. Cosmetic, but the static suffix undercuts the 'alive/forensic' authenticity intent.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx:162`** `[immersive/dead]` — Identifier typo: `activeFlorId` (should be activeFloorId). Cosmetic but propagates through lines 162, 235, 236.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx:167`** `[immersive/dead]` — phase.replace('floor_','') as FloorId is an unchecked cast — a type hole. If a new phase string slips through the visible guard without a matching ternary branch, this silently produces a bad FloorId.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx:165`** `[immersive/dead]` — During teleporting, all buttons are disabled by isTransitioning, but activeFlorId falls back to currentFloor ?? 'lobby' (lines 165-166), so mid-teleport the highlighted stop momentarily points at the OLD floor rather than the destination (currentFloor is only set after the 600ms timer in teleportTo).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx:184`** `[immersive/dead]` — Panel width 170 vs FloorContentOverlay's right:175 reservation (FloorContentOverlay line 181) — a 5px inconsistency in the two files' shared layout contract; a latent coupling that will drift.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ExteriorHint.tsx:52`** `[immersive/dead]` — Infinite pulse animation (repeat:Infinity) does not respect prefers-reduced-motion. Unlike UVCursor (which bails entirely on reduced-motion), this looping y/opacity animation runs unconditionally — an accessibility gap against the AAA bar.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:98`** `[immersive/dead]` — Score bar right:`${(1-item.score)*100}%` has no clamp. FloorItem.score is documented 0–1 but useFloorData passes raw quality_score (useFloorData line 36) which could exceed 1; out-of-range score overflows or inverts the bar.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:48`** `[immersive/dead]` — StatusBadge destructures `accent` (line 48) and DataRow passes it (line 109), but StatusBadge never uses accent — dead prop / vestigial threading.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:181`** `[immersive/dead]` — Hardcoded left:228 / right:175 to 'clear' the sibling overlays is a fragile layout contract; right:175 does not even match ElevatorHUD's actual width 170. On narrow viewports the 228+175 reserved gutters can crush the panel — no responsive breakpoint.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx:154`** `[immersive/dead]` — exitColor special-case keys off the literal hex '#7df9ff' (risk's cyan). This couples FloorContentOverlay to an exact hex in FLOOR_META; changing risk's accent would silently break the flash-color override.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/PaperShredTransition.tsx:72`** `[immersive/dead]` — No prefers-reduced-motion handling: the full 72-piece confetti + RAF loop plays for all users, unlike UVCursor which bails. AAA accessibility gap for a large-area motion effect.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/Preloader.tsx:52`** `[immersive/dead]` — Math.random() in the progress delta makes load time non-deterministic and untestable; combined with the 83 stall and no failsafe, there is no reproducible boot timing.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx:150`** `[immersive/dead]` — No prefers-reduced-motion handling and no skip-on-click affordance: the full ~4-second cinematic plays for everyone. Against the AAA bar this should offer a reduced/skip path.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx:67`** `[immersive/dead]` — maxR multiplier 1.28 is tuned for typical aspect ratios; on extreme aspect ratios sqrt(cx²+cy²)*1.28 may under/over-shoot, and combined with the no-resize issue can leave uncovered corners.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx:22`** `[immersive/dead]` — hovering detection uses (e.target as HTMLElement).closest(...). If e.target is ever a non-Element, .closest is undefined and would throw. Practically mousemove targets are Elements, but the unchecked cast is a latent type/runtime hole.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx:57`** `[immersive/dead]` — When UVCursor returns null under reduced-motion, the OS cursor must be restored elsewhere; StampIntro and presumably the app shell set cursor:'none'. If any ancestor keeps cursor:none while UVCursor is null, reduced-motion users could be left with NO visible cursor — a cross-file coupling worth verifying.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:50-53`** `[immersive/dead]` — kill() nulls activeTween.current, but the GSAP tween created for lookTarget (L91) is never tracked in a ref and never killed on phase change — only the camera.position tween is killed. A rapid phase switch can leave a stale lookTarget tween running concurrently with the new one, briefly fighting over lookTarget.current.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:43`** `[immersive/dead]` — Hook returns void and side-effects the shared camera; there is no unmount cleanup that kills the active tween (kill() is only called inside the effect body on the next phase change). If the component unmounts mid-tween, the GSAP tween keeps running against a possibly-disposed camera until it completes.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:29`** `[immersive/dead]` — avgQ assumes quality_score is a 0–1 fraction (*100), but the per-item score (L36) uses quality_score directly as a 0–1 progress value (fallback 0.5). If backend ever returns 0–100 scores, summary shows e.g. 8800% — no clamping/validation.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:49`** `[immersive/dead]` — fetchRisk summary 'clear' = LOW/MINIMAL only; MEDIUM cases counted in neither flagged nor clear, so flagged+clear can be < list.length, which may confuse displayed totals.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:91`** `[immersive/dead]` — ar.value.analyses access after allSettled assumes fulfilled value is an object with .analyses; if endpoint returns valid JSON of a different shape (array/error) .analyses is undefined and silently coerces to [] — masks backend contract drift.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:31`** `[immersive/dead]` — tickHash buckets on Date.now()/1800 — 1800 is milliseconds, not a clean second boundary; intent unclear and the trailing '…' implies truncation that isn't real. Cosmetic but misleading as an audit artifact in a compliance product.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:96-100`** `[immersive/dead]` — LABELS has 9 entries indexed by i%9 while placeholder builds 14 rows — labels repeat (rows 9–13 reuse labels 0–4) but TYPES (i%3) and STATUSES (i%4) cycle on different periods, so label/type pairing is arbitrary (e.g. a 'csv' label can get type QUERY).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:42-46`** `[immersive/dead]` — fetchEntries wraps Promise.allSettled in try/catch; allSettled never rejects, so the catch only guards parse errors before settling and rarely fires. A bad single endpoint just yields fewer results (acceptable), but the try/catch gives a false sense of comprehensive handling.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useMouseNDC.ts:4-11`** `[immersive/dead]` — If the caller passes an inline arrow as onMove (not memoized), useCallback's [onMove] dep changes every render, recreating handler and tearing down/re-adding the window listener on every parent render. Correctness is fine but it puts a memoization burden on every caller; an internal ref-to-latest-callback pattern would make it bind-once.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts:35-40`** `[immersive/dead]` — useFrame runs for the hook's entire lifetime updating uMouse/uTime even when the Risk floor isn't the active phase/visible; no gating on phase. Minor, but it keeps the shader animating off-screen.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts:37-38`** `[immersive/dead]` — mouseRef.set then copy is an extra indirection; material.uniforms.uMouse.value.set(mouseNDC.x, mouseNDC.y) would be simpler and equally allocation-free. Minor dead-ish indirection.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts:16-25`** `[immersive/dead]` — Grid loop uses w for both axes with `i <= w` step 32 on a hardcoded 512 texture; if makeUVInkTex were ever called non-square this draws an incorrect grid. Tightly coupled to the 512x512 literals passed two lines above.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts:13-15`** — ChartSpec.data is typed any[] and labels string[]?, so all p.x/p.y/p.label/p.value/p.bin/p.count/c.x/c.y/c.value accesses are unchecked any member accesses — type holes that defeat the strict TS config. A discriminated union per chart_type would catch shape mismatches at compile time.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts:113`** — Heatmap series uses labels.indexOf(c.x)/labels.indexOf(c.y) inside a .map, O(n) per cell → O(n^3) for an n×n matrix; a precomputed index Map would make it O(n^2). If a cell's x/y is not in labels, indexOf returns -1 and ECharts silently misplaces/drops it (no validation).
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts:18, 49, 70, 97, 104, 110, 146`** — Colors and tooltip bg/text are hardcoded hex/rgba duplicating index.css tokens (#4d7cff==--blue, #a855f7==--violet, #ff4d5e==--danger, rgba(11,14,26,..) is a legacy dark value). They will NOT follow html.light theme — charts stay dark-themed in light mode, and any token change must be edited in two places. Only muted/grid are themable via props.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:46-49`** — Four web fonts (Fraunces, Space Grotesk, Inter, JetBrains Mono) are referenced only by family name; no @font-face/@import here and no font-display strategy. If index.html doesn't load them the UI silently falls back to Georgia/system-ui/monospace, changing the whole forensic-ledger identity, with no FOUT/FOIT control expressed in CSS.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:90-95`** — Scrollbar styling is webkit-only (::-webkit-scrollbar); Firefox gets the default — no scrollbar-width/scrollbar-color fallback, a cross-browser inconsistency.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:36-43, 60-70`** — Comment says semantics are 'kept for chart/alert compat', but html.light only remaps --danger and --success, leaving --blue/--violet/--cyan/--indigo/--signature at dark-world values. Combined with chartOptions.ts hardcoding the same hexes, theming is split-brain: some semantics adapt to light mode, others (and all chart colors) do not.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:480-487`** — Print rule uses body *{visibility:hidden} then reveals #verita-report; visibility:hidden still occupies layout so hidden ancestors can leave blank space and any position:fixed element outside the report tree can affect pagination. Also depends on an element with id verita-report existing — silent no-op if the report view isn't mounted.
- **`frontend/src/test/setup.ts:3-13`** — Comments reference jsdom ('jsdom doesn't implement matchMedia'), but vitest.config.ts uses environment:'happy-dom', which already implements both matchMedia and ResizeObserver — so the ||-guarded stubs never execute in the current config (dead/aspirational code or stale comments). jsdom is installed but unused by the vitest config; reconcile the comment vs. the actual environment.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.test.tsx:2`** — fireEvent imported but never used — dead import. The 'Try again' recovery branch (onClick → setState({error:null}) re-rendering children) is left uncovered, which is the most behaviorally interesting part of the boundary.

### NIT (89)

- **`frontend/index.html:3-13`** — No meta description, theme-color, or Open Graph tags — SEO/social-share metadata absent for a product landing page.
- **`frontend/src/vite-env.d.ts:1`** — No custom ImportMetaEnv interface declared — project-specific env vars like VITE_API_URL are not strongly typed (rely on Vite's permissive default index signature).
- **`frontend/src/store/themeStore.ts:14, 17`** — localStorage value is cast `as Theme` (line 17) without validation; a corrupted stored value bypasses the type and is silently treated as non-light. Also no try/catch around localStorage — throws in privacy modes / disabled storage would crash the module import.
- **`frontend/src/App.tsx:25-29`** — Route declaration order (line 25-29) does not match the sidebar NAV order in AppShell — cosmetic/maintenance inconsistency only.
- **`frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx:57-414`** — Pervasive magic numbers for layout (sidebar 220, header 52, stamp 28, avatar 30, gaps, the 0.48–0.95rem font sizes) and theme-conditional ternaries duplicated inline rather than tokenized — high inline-style verbosity makes the component hard to scan/maintain.
- **`frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx:8, 19`** — imported `Settings` from lucide-react (icon, line 8) shares its name with the routed `Settings` page component conceptually; not a collision here since the page isn't imported in this file, but the NAV uses the icon while the route label is also 'Settings' — easy to confuse when editing.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx:249-251 / 302-306`** — Dead-ish conditional fallbacks: analyses.length===0 / investigations.length===0 blocks are unreachable when both are empty (empty branch wins) and only render in the mixed case; mostly redundant.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx:57`** — Magic numbers: limit=8 hardcoded into both history URLs with no constant.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx:68`** — Magic numbers: 180ms debounce, limit=20, slider bounds 0.05/0.95/0.01 all inline with no named constants.
- **`frontend/src/pages/NLP.tsx:241`** — Signals list items keyed by array index (key={i}); fragile if list ordering/content changes. Same index-key pattern in highlight() marks (key={i}, line 50).
- **`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx:74`** — Hardcoded hex strings #131110 / #f3eee2 in the theme-description copy duplicate values that presumably live in the CSS token system; could drift from the actual --bg tokens.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:290, 410`** — Report button calls window.print() directly; #verita-report has inline display:none, so print visibility depends on a print stylesheet elsewhere overriding it — coupling not visible in this file.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx:41-46`** — Result interface types profile/quality/dashboard as `any`, losing type safety across the whole page (charts, kpis, geoChart all `any`).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:1-3`** — Uses the legacy glass/aurora visual system and hardcoded gradient hex, conflicting with CLAUDE.md/DESIGN.md guidance that the paper/ink Forensic Ledger system is canonical and glass should be maintained-not-extended.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx:18-28`** — Result interface duplicates the name 'Result' used in Studio.tsx (different shape); module-scoped so not a conflict but easy to confuse.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:27-43`** — run() has no client guard beyond the disabled button; pressing Ctrl+Enter repeatedly can fire overlapping requests — in-flight requests aren't aborted.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx:1-3`** — Legacy glass styling and hardcoded gradient hex, same DESIGN.md drift as Investigator.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:53`** — ins.icon is rendered as raw text (assumed emoji); a non-emoji/empty string leaves a blank leading column with the gap intact.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx:1-3`** — Legacy glass styling, same DESIGN.md drift as the other children.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:53, 83`** — Deduction rows use key={i} (index); column cards correctly use key={col.name}. Data is static so low impact.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx:29`** — maxHeight calc(100vh - 140px) hardcodes a 140px header offset; if the Studio header height changes the rail's scroll viewport misaligns.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx:64`** — tooltip formatter uses `p.value` truthiness — a legitimate value of 0 renders as 'no activity', conflating 'zero activity' with 'no data'.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx:63-69`** — Colors hardcoded as hex/rgba inside the ECharts option rather than CSS tokens, so the map will not follow theme changes and diverges from the design-system token approach used elsewhere.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:50,54`** — 'geo' node type is special-cased to 'dimension' in two places (category and shadow color) but is not a declared category; any other unknown type falls through to category=type (which won't match a legend category) and shadow fallback #4d7cff — unknown types render uncolored/uncategorized without warning.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:7,63`** — Palette and edge colors hardcoded hex, not theme tokens — won't respond to theming and duplicates color definitions also present in sibling panels (GeoMap).
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx:47,61`** — fontFamily 'JetBrains Mono' is a hardcoded string literal rather than the var(--font-mono) token used in the wrapper chrome — inconsistent and breaks if the mono font is renamed.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx:72,76`** — Range input and Play/Pause button have no aria-label / associated <label>; the button is icon-only. Against the project's stated WCAG AAA bar the scrubber and play control are not screen-reader labeled.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx:84`** — f.total.toLocaleString() and f.mean.toLocaleString() assume numbers; if the backend ever returns null/undefined for an empty period this throws. No defensive coercion.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx:14,68`** — Mover.before / Mover.after are part of the fetched type but never rendered — dead data in the UI (only change_pct shown). Either show before→after or drop them from the consumed shape.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx:33`** — No loading skeleton: between mount and first response the panel renders null, so it pops in abruptly (the framer-motion entrance only plays once headline arrives).
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:16`** — fakeHash magic constants (9301, 49297, 233, 829.7382, %16) are opaque pseudo-random hex generation; cosmetic only.
- **`frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx:315`** — Purple accent #a855f7 (and rgba variants) hardcoded inline in multiple places (NLP section/mock) instead of hoisted to a constant alongside AMBER/RED/GREEN/GOLD.
- **`frontend/src/sections/Hero.tsx:1`** `[immersive/dead]` — Dead legacy module retained per DESIGN.md 'maintain, don't extend' — candidate for deletion if the aurora system is fully retired.
- **`frontend/src/sections/LedgerSections.tsx:1`** `[immersive/dead]` — Dead legacy module duplicating the canonical ledger story now inlined in Landing.tsx. Candidate for deletion.
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx:59`** — Per-node useFrame closures (20 nodes) each run trig every frame; fine at 20 nodes but one-useFrame-per-mesh doesn't scale — larger graphs would need an instanced approach.
- **`frontend/src/components/landing/ParticleField.tsx:1`** — Dead legacy module; aurora background superseded by the dark-paper Forensic Ledger. Candidate for deletion. Shader magic numbers undocumented.
- **`frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx:28`** — `e.target as HTMLElement` is an unchecked cast. If the event target is a non-Element node (e.g. a text node or the document in edge cases), `.closest` would throw; in practice mousemove targets are Elements so this is low-risk, but it's an unguarded type assertion.
- **`frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx:8,22`** — Comment says it is 'Disabled on coarse pointers (touch)' but the logic keys off '(pointer: fine)' rather than '(pointer: coarse)'. Functionally equivalent for typical devices, but a hybrid device reporting fine would still enable the cursor — minor doc/intent mismatch.
- **`frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx:47,75`** — Both layers hardcode zIndex 9999 with no documented stacking contract; if any modal/overlay uses a comparable z-index the decorative cursor could paint over it (pointerEvents:none mitigates interaction issues but not visual layering).
- **`frontend/src/components/EChart.tsx:5`** — No accessibility affordance: the chart canvas has no role, aria-label, or text fallback. ECharts can emit an aria description via option.aria, but the wrapper neither sets nor exposes it — a11y gap against the project's stated WCAG AAA bar.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx:15`** — `info` is typed as `unknown` rather than React's ErrorInfo type; harmless since it is only forwarded to console, but loses type information (e.g. info.componentStack).
- **`frontend/src/components/SignalField.tsx:13`** `[immersive/dead]` — `useRef<THREE.Points>(null!)` uses the non-null assertion init pattern; conventional for r3f refs but technically lies about the initial null value (mitigated by the `if (!g) return` guard in useFrame).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/ImmersiveLanding.tsx:36`** `[immersive/dead]` — Hardcoded paper-cream hex '#f3eee2' is duplicated here and in BuildingScene (clear color + background). Not pulled from a shared token, so the two can drift.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:98-114`** `[immersive/dead]` — WindDust wrap logic is asymmetric: X spawns in ±27.5 but wraps at <-27 to +27, and Y spawns -9..11 but wraps >12 to -9. The 0.5-unit and 1-unit mismatches between spawn range and wrap thresholds are harmless but make the bounds look unintentional.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/BuildingScene.tsx:79-84`** `[immersive/dead]` — CityBackground meshes use index `i` as React key (line 80); fine because the array is static, but they are flat boxes that never animate and could be merged/instanced for fewer draw calls.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/context/BuildingContext.tsx:54,69-72,96-98`** `[immersive/dead]` — setPhase is exported but is just setPhaseState renamed; meanwhile higher-level actions (enterBuilding etc.) bypass it and call setPhaseState directly. Mixed usage makes it harder to add cross-cutting logging/guards in one place.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:227-232,336-340`** `[immersive/dead]` — High emissiveIntensity values (chandelier pendants 2.5, sconces 3) combined with ACES + 14 point lights may clip; no bloom postprocessing is wired here so the emissive reads as bright dots without glow — the 'lights' have no postFX.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LobbyRoom.tsx:34,80,147,209,219`** `[immersive/dead]` — Magic numbers throughout (tile 1.6, wainscot 1.4, panel counts 6, coffer 4x8, pillar taper 0.28/0.32, chandelier radius 1.4, atan2(0.1,r) droop) with no named constants beyond room dimensions — intentional for hand-authored scene but tuning-error-prone.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:124-134`** `[immersive/dead]` — VERITA embossed label on spine (124-134) and corner brackets are again plain boxes, not actual lettering — consistent with LobbyRoom's placeholder approach but not literal branding text.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/lobby/LivingLedger.tsx:131`** `[immersive/dead]` — Inline new THREE.Color in render-path material props (e.g. line 131) allocates per render of that mesh — negligible here but a pattern to watch; the ones inside useMemo are fine.
