- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/BuildingFacade.tsx:147`** `[immersive/dead]` — VERITA sign plane material is marked transparent but the canvas is fully opaque (paper background filled). transparent:true with no alpha forces an unnecessary alpha-blend pass / can introduce z-sorting artifacts.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/BuildingFacade.tsx:73,170-175`** `[immersive/dead]` — Heavy use of unexplained magic numbers for floor-band ys ([-5.8,-1.6,2.6,6.8]) and window grid positions that are not derived from BH/floor count — brittle if BH changes. Comment says '4 columns × 4 rows' which matches but coordinates are hand-tuned literals.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/BuildingFacade.tsx:147`** `[immersive/dead]` — Inline meshStandardMaterial here (not a shared singleton) is recreated on every render of VeritaSignMesh, unlike the rest of the file which uses singletons — minor inconsistency; cheap since the component rarely re-renders.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/EntranceDoors.tsx:147`** `[immersive/dead]` — Handle horizontal position uses isLeft ? +0.38 : -0.38 of DOOR_W from panelOffsetX — handles end up toward the meeting edge; correct, but the ternary plus panelOffsetX (already side-dependent) is hard to read.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/exterior/HashChainSpine.tsx:89-96`** `[immersive/dead]` — LinkConnector material and torus materials are all created inline per render; geometries/materials are not disposed. Acceptable for a small static set but inconsistent with disposal hygiene.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/materials/uvReveal.ts:49-64`** `[immersive/dead]` — createUVRevealMaterial provides no way to update uMouse/uTime and no glslVersion; consumers must reach into material.uniforms directly each frame. A thin helper or typed uniforms object would reduce stringly-typed access. Also UV_VERT/UV_FRAG are exported but the whole module is not imported by the three exterior TSX files reviewed — appears unused by this subsystem (potential dead code or used elsewhere).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/FloorScene.tsx:13-19`** `[immersive/dead]` — Phase->component mapping is a hand-written ternary chain; a lookup object (Record<BuildingPhase, LazyComponent>) would be more maintainable and type-checked exhaustively against the union.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/StudioFloor.tsx:169`** `[immersive/dead]` — makeBlueprintTex texture is created in useMemo with [] deps so it is stable per mount, but because FloorScene unmounts/remounts floors on phase change the canvas texture is regenerated every entry (no shared cache across floors).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx:229-232`** `[immersive/dead]` — Floor UV grid lines use 7 thin emissive planes laid ON the shader floor at y+0.005; combined with the shader-revealed floor this is double-drawing grid lines (the shader baseMap already draws a grid per useUVReveal.ts), a slight visual/perf redundancy.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/DetectiveFloor.tsx:12`** `[immersive/dead]` — ctx.getContext('2d')! uses non-null assertion (every canvas tex factory does this across all five floors); if a browser returns null (rare, e.g. context-lost) this throws. No guard/fallback anywhere in the subsystem.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/VaultFloor.tsx:23`** `[immersive/dead]` — Label text embeds a literal '✓' (U+2713) drawn via canvas fillText — depends on the default canvas font having the glyph; on a fallback font it may render as tofu. Minor cross-platform risk.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx:316`** `[immersive/dead]` — CityLightsBelow distant pointLight intensity 2.5 distance 100 decay 1 is a very large-radius light; combined with per-floor lights this is a notable but bounded fragment-shading cost.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx:12`** `[immersive/dead]` — FLOORS status union is 'ACTIVE'|'SEALED'|'RESTRICTED' yet only ACTIVE and SEALED are ever assigned in the manifest (no RESTRICTED entry), so the red statusColor branch (line 70) is dead in practice.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx:89`** `[immersive/dead]` — borderLeft is set conditionally AND a CSS transition:'background 0.2s' (line 89) coexists with framer-motion's whileHover x animation; mixing CSS transitions and motion transforms on the same element is harmless but inconsistent with the rest of the file's pure-motion approach.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx:107`** `[immersive/dead]` — The active ◀ arrow uses initial/animate without AnimatePresence, so it never animates out when the current floor changes (it disappears with the row re-render).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ExteriorHint.tsx:31`** `[immersive/dead]` — Decorative SVG has no aria-hidden; with pointerEvents:none and a text label present it is low-impact, but the empty <svg> may be announced by some screen readers.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/ExteriorHint.tsx:47`** `[immersive/dead]` — Copy 'Click the doors to enter' is mouse-centric; no keyboard/tap equivalent guidance. Minor UX/i18n consideration.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/PaperShredTransition.tsx:146`** `[immersive/dead]` — flyIn builds proxyArr from p.startX/startY while flyOut builds it from current p.x/p.y; the asymmetry is intentional (fly-out resumes wherever pieces are) but undocumented and easy to break.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/Preloader.tsx:82`** `[immersive/dead]` — zIndex 10000 vs StampIntro 9998 vs UVCursor 9999 — three different magic z-indices in three files with no shared constant; easy to collide as overlays grow.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx:227`** `[immersive/dead]` — stampWrapRef is attached (line 227) but never read — dead ref (only stampInnerRef is animated).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx:13`** `[immersive/dead]` — Both laggedRef AND laggedPos hold the halo position (ref as lerp source, state to trigger render). Standard pattern, but the dual source invites a stale-state bug if future edits read laggedPos instead of laggedRef in the tick.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:79`** `[immersive/dead]` — Magic thresholds 18 (z-jump, L79) and 8 (y-jump, L86) are unexplained constants implicitly tied to FLOOR_STEP=12 geometry; brittle if floor spacing changes.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts:118`** `[immersive/dead]` — FLOOR_INDEX[phase] returns 0 for 'lobby'; the interior parallax branch correctly uses `floorIdx !== undefined` (handles index 0) rather than a falsy check — deliberate and correct, worth noting.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:35`** `[immersive/dead]` — Status mapping logic ('A'/'B' grade, CRITICAL/HIGH risk) is duplicated near-verbatim in useLiveLedger.ts (L54,78) — two sources of truth for the same VERIFIED/FLAGGED rules; should be shared.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts:19`** `[immersive/dead]` — EMPTY is a module-level object reused as initial, loading, and (shape of) offline fallback; FloorData.items is an array so accidental mutation of EMPTY.items would leak across consumers (no mutation today, but it's a shared reference).
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts:112`** `[immersive/dead]` — Layout magic numbers (column x-offsets 36,54,132,342,452,516; stamp widths 58/52; font sizes 7/7.5/8/8.5/10px) are scattered literals in render() with no named constants — fragile to tweak.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useMouseNDC.ts:5`** `[immersive/dead]` — Typed against MouseEvent only; touch/pointer input is not handled, so parallax is mouse-only (no touchmove/pointermove). Acceptable for a desktop immersive scene but a coverage gap on touch devices.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useMouseNDC.ts:3`** `[immersive/dead]` — Returns void with no SSR guard on window; fine in a Vite client-only app but would throw if ever imported in an SSR/test environment without a window.
- **`frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts:11`** `[immersive/dead]` — Return type is inferred from createUVRevealMaterial; no explicit return annotation, so consumers depend on cross-module inference — slight type-stability risk if uvReveal's signature changes.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts:31, 104, 138`** — Tooltip backgroundColor rgba(11,14,26,0.92) is a legacy machine-world dark value with no token; in Porcelain (light) mode this tooltip is near-black — minor contrast/visual inconsistency.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts:95`** — Histogram hides x-axis labels entirely (axisLabel.show:false) so bins are unlabeled on the axis; readers rely solely on the tooltip to identify a bin — a legibility/a11y tradeoff.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.test.ts:10`** — Heavy use of : any casts on option objects forfeits type-level regression safety; a structural typo in the produced option would not fail the test as long as the asserted leaf still exists.
- **`frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.test.ts:44-52`** — Coverage gaps: bar/histogram branches are untested, and the ci-lo stacking / (hi-lo) math is not numerically verified — only the presence of the ci-band series name is checked.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:393-396`** — .uv-toggle anchors with top:calc(100vh-72px) and margin-left:calc(100%-158px) — magic offsets assuming a 158×72px button region; changing padding/text breaks the bottom-right anchoring. A position:fixed right/bottom would be more robust.
- **`frontend/src/index.css:119-139`** — Machine-world overlays (.crt-lines/.crt-vignette/.scan-beam) use position:absolute inset:0 and rely on a positioned ancestor; dropped into a static container they escape to the nearest positioned ancestor or viewport — a latent containing-block footgun.
- **`frontend/src/test/setup.ts:4`** — matchMedia stub always returns matches:false, so tests cannot exercise the matches:true branch (reduced-motion / dark-mode). A coverage ceiling for any media-query-dependent logic.
- **`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.test.tsx:19-24`** — Does not assert componentDidCatch logged with the '[Verita] error in Studio:' prefix, nor that re-rendering after a successful 'Try again' restores children — both untested.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.test.tsx:18-23`** — Only the open/reveal direction is tested; the toggle-closed path (clicking the same button again → open===i?null) and the single-open invariant (opening row B closes row A) are not asserted, leaving part of the accordion state machine uncovered.
- **`frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.test.tsx:6`** — Fixture's info insight (severity:'info') silently exercises an undefined-token fallback in the component: InsightsPanel.tsx borderLeft for non-warning uses var(--accent,#12b5a3), but --accent is NOT defined in index.css :root, so info findings always render the hardcoded #12b5a3 fallback. (Reported against the component but surfaced via this test's fixture.)

---

## File-by-file detail

## Frontend App Shell, Routing, Entry Point & Theme (Verita React/Vite)

> This subsystem is the application's bootstrap and chrome: main.tsx mounts React in StrictMode into #root; App.tsx defines the react-router-dom v7 route tree (a public Landing at "/" plus five authenticated pages nested under a shared AppShell layout via Outlet), wrapping each page element in an ErrorBoundary and conditionally rendering a CustomCursor everywhere except the landing. AppShell renders the "Forensic Ledger" sidebar + header chrome with two decorative live effects (a random hex hash ticker and a four-node integrity chain that seals on mount). themeStore is a tiny zustand store that toggles a "light" class on documentElement and persists to localStorage; config.ts resolves the backend API base URL from VITE_API_URL with a localhost fallback. There is no live data here — all dynamics are cosmetic (Math.random hash, animated chain), styling is almost entirely inline styles keyed to CSS custom properties defined in index.css.

### `frontend/src/main.tsx`  _(11 lines)_

**Purpose:** Application entry point — creates the React 18 root and renders <App/> in StrictMode; imports global index.css.
**Props/inputs:** none
**State/refs:** none
**Dependencies:** `react`, `react-dom/client`, `./App`, `./index.css`
**Data flow:** No data. Pure mount: ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(...).
**Styling:** Imports ./index.css globally (the source of all --bg, --text, --stamp-red, --foil-gold, --font-* CSS variables consumed downstream).

**Details:** Line 6 uses a non-null assertion `document.getElementById("root")!` to satisfy TypeScript; #root exists in index.html line 15 so this is safe in practice. React.StrictMode (lines 7-9) double-invokes effects/renders in development — this matters for AppShell's timer effects (see AppShell issues): the chain-sealing setTimeout chain and the hash setInterval are mounted, cleaned up, and remounted, so in dev the chain animation effectively runs twice and any leaked timer would compound. No createRoot error handling, no hydration (CSR only).

### `frontend/index.html`  _(18 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vite HTML entry — document shell, fonts preconnect/stylesheet, #root mount node, module script tag for main.tsx.
**Props/inputs:** n/a
**State/refs:** n/a
**Dependencies:** `/src/main.tsx (module script)`, `Google Fonts: Space Grotesk, Inter, JetBrains Mono, Fraunces`
**Data flow:** Static. Loads fonts from fonts.googleapis.com / fonts.gstatic.com.
**Styling:** Loads four font families (line 10) consumed via --font-display/--font-body/--font-mono/--font-serif tokens. Uses display=swap to avoid FOIT.

**Details:** lang="en" (line 2). Viewport meta is standard responsive (line 5). Title (line 6) is the brand tagline. Preconnect hints to both googleapis and gstatic with crossorigin on gstatic (lines 7-8) — correct font-loading optimization. Single root div (line 15), module script type with /src/main.tsx (line 16). No favicon link, no meta description, no Open Graph/theme-color, no <noscript> fallback.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 10, 14-17): External Google Fonts dependency (line 10) — runtime network call; offline/blocked-CDN renders fall back to system fonts, and there is no <noscript> message for a JS-disabled visitor (blank page).
- _nit_ (line 3-13): No meta description, theme-color, or Open Graph tags — SEO/social-share metadata absent for a product landing page.

### `frontend/src/config.ts`  _(1 lines)_

**Purpose:** Single source of truth for the backend API base URL.
**Exports:** `API_BASE (const string)`
**Props/inputs:** n/a
**State/refs:** n/a
**Dependencies:** `import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL (Vite env, typed via vite-env.d.ts)`
**Data flow:** Reads Vite build-time env VITE_API_URL; falls back to the hardcoded string "http://localhost:8000". Consumed by page/service modules that fetch the FastAPI backend.
**Styling:** n/a

**Details:** `export const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000";`. Because import.meta.env values are inlined at build time, the fallback is baked into the production bundle if VITE_API_URL is unset. Confirmed there is NO .env / .env.production file in frontend/, so by default every build ships the localhost:8000 fallback — fine for dev, broken for any deployed environment unless the env var is provided at build time. The `||` (rather than `??`) also means an empty-string VITE_API_URL falls through to localhost, which is the usually-desired behavior.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 1): Hardcoded http://localhost:8000 fallback with no .env file present in the repo — a production build with VITE_API_URL unset will silently point the frontend at localhost (mixed-content/CORS failures in prod). No runtime warning.

### `frontend/src/vite-env.d.ts`  _(1 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vite client type reference so import.meta.env and ?url/?raw asset imports are typed.
**Props/inputs:** n/a
**State/refs:** n/a
**Dependencies:** `vite/client (triple-slash reference)`
**Data flow:** n/a (ambient types only).
**Styling:** n/a

**Details:** `/// <reference types="vite/client" />`. Provides the generic ImportMetaEnv type. Note: it does NOT declare a custom ImportMetaEnv interface, so VITE_API_URL (used in config.ts) is typed as the loose `string | boolean | undefined` from Vite's default ImportMetaEnv index signature rather than an explicit `readonly VITE_API_URL: string`. Adding an `interface ImportMetaEnv { readonly VITE_API_URL: string }` would give stronger typing and autocomplete.

**Issues:**
- _nit_ (line 1): No custom ImportMetaEnv interface declared — project-specific env vars like VITE_API_URL are not strongly typed (rely on Vite's permissive default index signature).

### `frontend/src/store/themeStore.ts`  _(31 lines)_

**Purpose:** Zustand store for dark/light theme with side-effect (DOM class toggle) + localStorage persistence.
**Exports:** `useTheme (zustand hook)`
**Props/inputs:** n/a
**State/refs:** ThemeState: { theme: 'dark'|'light'; toggle(); set(t) }
**Hooks:** `create (zustand)`
**Dependencies:** `zustand (create)`
**Data flow:** Reads/writes localStorage key 'verita-theme'; mutates document.documentElement classList. No API. Consumed by AppShell (theme + toggle) and presumably other pages.
**Styling:** Drives theming by toggling the 'light' class on <html> (line 13); all CSS variables in index.css are presumably re-scoped under html.light.

**Details:** Type Theme = 'dark'|'light' (line 3). apply() (lines 11-15) toggles the 'light' class based on equality and writes localStorage. CRITICAL ORDERING: lines 17-18 run apply(initial) at MODULE-IMPORT TIME (top-level side effect) — this is what prevents a flash of wrong theme before React mounts, but it means importing this module has a DOM side effect (touches document). `initial` (line 17) casts localStorage value to Theme with `|| 'dark'` default — note an invalid/corrupted stored value (e.g. 'blue') would NOT be caught by the cast (TS cast is compile-time only) and would be passed through; apply() would then just not add the 'light' class, defaulting to dark visuals, so it degrades gracefully. toggle() (lines 22-26) reads current via get(), flips, applies, sets. set() (lines 27-30) applies + sets directly. Store state and DOM are kept in sync only through apply(); if another tab changes localStorage, this tab is NOT notified (no 'storage' event listener) so cross-tab theme is not synced.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 17-18): Top-level side effects at lines 17-18 (localStorage read + DOM classList mutation + apply) execute on module import, not in a React lifecycle. SSR-unsafe (references document/localStorage at import) — fine for this CSR-only app but a footgun if ever server-rendered, and makes the module impure/harder to test.
- _low_ (line 11-31): No cross-tab sync: a theme change in one tab is persisted to localStorage but other open tabs are not updated (no window 'storage' listener).
- _nit_ (line 14, 17): localStorage value is cast `as Theme` (line 17) without validation; a corrupted stored value bypasses the type and is silently treated as non-light. Also no try/catch around localStorage — throws in privacy modes / disabled storage would crash the module import.

### `frontend/src/App.tsx`  _(43 lines)_

**Purpose:** Root component — sets up BrowserRouter and the full route tree, wraps pages in ErrorBoundary, and conditionally mounts the CustomCursor.
**Exports:** `default App()`, `(internal) AppRoutes()`
**Props/inputs:** none
**State/refs:** none (reads route via useLocation in AppRoutes)
**Hooks:** `useLocation (react-router-dom)`
**Dependencies:** `react-router-dom (BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, useLocation)`, `./components/CustomCursor`, `./components/AppShell`, `./components/ErrorBoundary`, `./pages/{Landing,Studio,Risk,NLP,Overview,Settings}`
**Data flow:** No data fetching. Pure routing/composition. Passes route elements (props.node) into ErrorBoundary via the wrap() helper.
**Styling:** None directly (delegates to AppShell + page components).

**Details:** wrap() helper (lines 12-14) is a factory that returns each page wrapped in <ErrorBoundary label={...}>, giving per-route error isolation with a human label ('Landing','Studio','Risk Engine','NLP Insight','Overview','Settings'). AppRoutes() (lines 17-34): destructures pathname from useLocation; renders <CustomCursor/> ONLY when pathname !== '/' (line 21) — the documented intent (line 16 comment) is that the Landing page manages its own cursor/scroll effects. Route structure (lines 22-31): '/' -> Landing (NOT inside AppShell, so the landing is full-bleed with no sidebar/header); a LAYOUT route `<Route element={<AppShell/>}>` (line 24) with no path nests the five app pages (/studio, /overview, /risk, /nlp, /settings) — AppShell's <Outlet/> renders them inside the chrome. NOTE the route order in JSX (studio, overview, risk, nlp, settings) differs from the sidebar NAV order (overview, studio, risk, nlp, settings) — harmless since matching is by path, but a latent inconsistency. No catch-all/404 route: navigating to an unknown path renders nothing (blank) with a console warning from the router. No lazy-loading/Suspense — all six pages (including the heavy react-three-fiber Studio/Landing) are imported eagerly at the top, so the initial bundle includes the 3D layer even when the user lands on a 2D page. App() (lines 36-42) just wraps AppRoutes in BrowserRouter (no basename, so deploying under a subpath would break routing).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 22-31): No catch-all route (path="*"). Unknown URLs render a blank screen (router logs 'No routes matched location') instead of a 404/redirect — poor UX and a dead-end for mistyped/stale links.
- _medium_ (line 5-10): All page components are imported eagerly (lines 5-10) with no React.lazy/Suspense code-splitting. The immersive react-three-fiber pages (Landing/Studio) are bundled into the initial load, inflating first-paint cost for users who only need a 2D page.
- _low_ (line 38): BrowserRouter has no `basename` (line 38); any deployment under a sub-path (e.g. /app) would break all routing and asset-relative links.
- _nit_ (line 25-29): Route declaration order (line 25-29) does not match the sidebar NAV order in AppShell — cosmetic/maintenance inconsistency only.

### `frontend/src/components/AppShell.tsx`  _(425 lines)_

**Purpose:** Layout chrome for the five authenticated pages — 'Forensic Ledger' sidebar (logo, nav, animated integrity chain, live hash) + top header (breadcrumb, hash ticker, theme toggle, avatar) wrapping an <Outlet/>.
**Exports:** `default AppShell()`, `(internal) useHashTick()`
**Props/inputs:** none (consumed as a react-router layout route element)
**State/refs:** useTheme() -> {theme, toggle}; useLocation() -> loc; useHashTick() -> hash string; sealedNodes: number[] (which chain nodes have sealed)
**Hooks:** `useState`, `useEffect`, `useTheme (zustand)`, `useLocation (react-router)`, `custom useHashTick`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useState)`, `react-router-dom (NavLink, Outlet, useLocation)`, `lucide-react (LayoutGrid, Sparkles, ShieldAlert, MessageSquareText, Settings, Sun, Moon)`, `../store/themeStore (useTheme)`
**Data flow:** No real data. NAV array (lines 14-20) is a hardcoded static config. hash (line 27) is Math.random-generated cosmetic hex. sealedNodes is purely client-driven animation state. Avatar initials 'RM' (line 406) are hardcoded (matches git user rajmodi262). No API, no props.
**Styling:** Almost entirely inline style objects keyed to CSS variables (--bg,--bg-2,--border,--text,--text-muted,--stamp-red,--foil-gold,--seal-green,--paper,--font-display/body/mono/serif). Two CSS classes used: 'chain-node'/'chain-node--dark'/'sealed' (defined index.css 261-283) and '.hash-ticker-header'. A single injected <style> block (lines 418-422) holds one media query. Heavy use of magic-number sizing (sidebar 220px, header 52px, stamp 28px, avatar 30px, etc.).

**Details:** useHashTick() (lines 23-32): useState seeded with a fixed hex 'a3f2b1c4e8d7a0f9'; a setInterval every 2200ms regenerates a 16-char random hex via Array.from({length:16}, () => Math.floor(Math.random()*16).toString(16)); cleanup clears the interval. This timer runs forever while AppShell is mounted regardless of tab visibility or prefers-reduced-motion. current (line 39) = NAV.find(n => loc.pathname.startsWith(n.to)) — startsWith means a deeper path like /risk/123 still resolves to the Risk entry; correct here. The chain-seal effect (lines 43-52): a self-recursive setTimeout — initial 800ms delay, then tick() appends index i to sealedNodes and re-schedules itself every 600ms while i<4, sealing nodes 0..3. CLEANUP BUG: the returned cleanup only clearTimeout(t) for the FIRST timer; the recursive setTimeout handles created inside tick() are never captured or cleared, so if AppShell unmounts between 800ms and ~2600ms, pending tick() timers still fire and call setSealedNodes on an unmounted component (React 18 no longer warns, but it is a leaked timer / wasted setState). Under StrictMode (main.tsx) the effect mounts-unmounts-remounts in dev, so the first mount's uncaptured timers leak and a second chain animation starts — sealedNodes can briefly accumulate duplicates from two overlapping runs (the array uses `[...prev, i]` with no dedupe, and `.includes(i)` masks visual duplicates but length-based logic on line 285 `sealedNodes.length === 4` could overshoot to >4 in dev). Render: outer flex container min-height 100vh (lines 57-64). Sidebar (lines 68-296): fixed 220px, sticky top:0 height 100vh overflow hidden; 3px gold gradient band (theme-conditional, lines 83-90); logo NavLink to '/' (escapes the shell back to landing) with a 28px 'V' stamp box and VERITA/INTELLIGENCE lockup; 'CASE FILE' section label; nav (lines 168-229) maps NAV to NavLinks using the function-style `style={({isActive})=>...}` and function-child render to show active state (bold weight, --text color, subtle bg, 2px left border in --stamp-red, a red icon, and an 'OPEN' mono tag). Each NavLink has data-cursor (line 173) hooking the CustomCursor. Integrity block (lines 231-295): 'INTEGRITY' label; four chain-node divs with connectors — each node gets an inline transition with a staggered delay `${i*0.6}s` AND has the matching class toggled when sealedNodes.includes(i); connector lines (lines 262-274) turn --foil-gold only when both adjacent nodes are sealed; the live hash text (lines 280-294) turns --seal-green once sealedNodes.length===4. Header (lines 304-409): 52px tall; left breadcrumb 'CASE FILE / {current.short}' shown only if current is truthy (so on an unmatched nested path the breadcrumb silently disappears); right cluster has the hash ticker (display:none inline, revealed >=900px via the injected media query lines 418-422 toggling .hash-ticker-header to display:block !important), the theme-toggle button, and a hardcoded 'RM' avatar. Theme toggle button (lines 362-388): onClick=toggle; shows Sun when dark / Moon when light (line 387); hover handlers imperatively mutate borderColor/color to --foil-gold via e.currentTarget.style and revert on leave — this is JS-driven hover instead of CSS :hover, and there is no focus-visible styling so keyboard focus gives no visual border/color feedback. Main (lines 412-414) is the scroll container (overflow:auto, padding 24/28) wrapping <Outlet/>.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 43-52): Timer leak in the chain-seal effect: the cleanup (lines 50-51) only clears the initial setTimeout `t`. The recursive setTimeout handles created inside tick() (line 48) are never stored or cleared, so unmounting AppShell during the ~800–2600ms animation window leaves pending callbacks that fire setSealedNodes on an unmounted component. Compounded by React.StrictMode double-mount in dev (overlapping animation runs).
- _medium_ (line 362-388): Theme-toggle button has no :focus / focus-visible styling — hover feedback is applied imperatively via onMouseEnter/onMouseLeave (lines 378-385) only, so keyboard users get no visible focus indicator. Violates the project's stated WCAG AAA bar (CLAUDE.md / PRODUCT.md).
- _medium_ (line 362-388, 252-294): Nav links and the theme toggle rely on color/border + an 'OPEN' text tag for active state but the toggle button's accessible name is only the title attribute 'Toggle theme'; it has no aria-pressed/aria-label reflecting current theme, and the decorative hash/chain elements lack aria-hidden, adding noise for screen readers.
- _medium_ (line 23-32): useHashTick setInterval (lines 26-29) runs unconditionally every 2200ms for the life of the shell, ignoring prefers-reduced-motion. The index.css reduced-motion query (lines 717-723) only kills CSS animations/transitions; the JS-driven hash flicker continues, so motion-sensitive users still see the value change. Also keeps timers alive in background tabs.
- _low_ (line 46, 285): sealedNodes uses `[...prev, i]` with no dedupe (line 46). Under StrictMode's double-mount the uncaptured leaked timers from the first run can push duplicate indices, making `sealedNodes.length === 4` (line 285) and connector logic potentially fire early/oddly in dev.
- _low_ (line 93-94): Logo NavLink points to '/' (line 94) which exits the AppShell layout entirely back to the marketing Landing page; there is no in-app 'home'/dashboard distinction, which can feel like an accidental logout-of-app.
- _low_ (line 317-343, 418-422): Inline <style> injected on every render (lines 418-422) re-creates a <style> element each render of AppShell; harmless functionally but a minor anti-pattern vs a static CSS rule, and the breadcrumb relies on `current` so a deep unmatched path (line 317) silently renders an empty breadcrumb.
- _nit_ (line 57-414): Pervasive magic numbers for layout (sidebar 220, header 52, stamp 28, avatar 30, gaps, the 0.48–0.95rem font sizes) and theme-conditional ternaries duplicated inline rather than tokenized — high inline-style verbosity makes the component hard to scan/maintain.
- _nit_ (line 8, 19): imported `Settings` from lucide-react (icon, line 8) shares its name with the routed `Settings` page component conceptually; not a collision here since the page isn't imported in this file, but the NAV uses the icon while the route label is also 'Settings' — easy to confuse when editing.

**Dead code / TODOs:** The header hash-ticker is hidden (display:none inline, lines 354) and only revealed >=900px; below that breakpoint it is permanently dead/invisible. No TODO/FIXME comments. No commented-out code. No literal mock-data placeholders beyond the cosmetic random hash and hardcoded 'RM' avatar initials.

---

## Data Dashboard Pages (Overview, Risk, NLP, Settings)

> Four route-level page components under frontend/src/pages/ that form Verita's data dashboards. All four are default-exported function components styled inline with the "Forensic Ledger" design tokens (CSS vars like --surface, --foil-gold, --stamp-red, hard 4px4px box-shadows, JetBrains Mono / serif fonts). Overview shows analysis/investigation history from /api/history; Risk is the fraud ML dashboard (ECharts ROC/PR/feature curves, live threshold slider, confusion matrix, alert queue from /api/risk); NLP is a compliance-text analyzer POSTing to /api/nlp/analyze with entity highlighting; Settings shows live /api/health status plus a theme toggle. All data is live API (relative to API_BASE from ../config) except NLP's hardcoded SAMPLE narrative and the entity/color legend maps. Risk and Settings consume the shared themeStore (Zustand); Risk is the only file using a chart library (EChart wrapper over echarts) and the only one with a debounce ref/timer.

### `frontend/src/pages/Overview.tsx`  _(314 lines)_

**Purpose:** Command Center / history landing page. Fetches summary counters plus recent analyses and investigations and renders three stat tiles, two history columns, and an empty-state CTA.
**Exports:** `default Overview()`
**Props/inputs:** None (route component).
**State/refs:** useState summary {analyses,investigations,queries} (line 49); analyses: Analysis[] (50); investigations: Investigation[] (51); loaded: boolean (52). No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState x4`, `useEffect (one-time fetch, line 54)`, `useNavigate (react-router, line 48)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useState)`, `react-router-dom (useNavigate)`, `lucide-react (Sparkles, ScanSearch, FileSpreadsheet, Database)`, `../config (API_BASE)`
**Data flow:** Live API only. useEffect fires once on mount (empty dep array, line 63) and runs Promise.all over three fetches: /api/history/summary, /api/history/analyses?limit=8, /api/history/investigations?limit=8 (line 57). Helper j() (55-56) does fetch(...).then(r=>r.ok?r.json():{}).catch(()=>({})) so any failed request resolves to {} and the page silently renders zeros/empties rather than an error. Results flow into the four state setters; loaded flips true (61). No props, no mock data — but GRADE_COLOR (16-18) and RISK_COLOR (19-21) are hardcoded lookup maps keyed by grade letter / risk level.
**Styling:** Inline styles via shared panelStyle (31-36, --surface bg, 1px --border, hard 4px4px shadow) and labelStyle (38-45, mono uppercase 0.56rem, 0.24em tracking). Serif 900-weight h1 with clamp() sizing. Stat tiles use grid auto-fit minmax(170px,1fr) (127). History uses auto-fit minmax(320px,1fr) (196). All colors are CSS vars; no className usage.

**Details:** ago() helper (23-29) computes relative time from an ISO string via (Date.now()-new Date(iso))/1000 with thresholds 60 / 3600 / 86400 returning 'just now','Nm ago','Nh ago','Nd ago'. empty flag (65) = loaded && both arrays empty — gates the empty-state panel (167) vs the two-column history grid (195). NEW ANALYSIS button (100) and empty-state CTA (176) both navigate('/studio'). Hover handlers on the header button (119-120) imperatively mutate boxShadow/transform to a 'press in' effect (shadow 3px3px->2px2px, transform translate(1px,1px)); restored onMouseLeave. Analyses rows (201-248) show title (ellipsis truncation 219-221), a mono meta line with row_count.toLocaleString(), column_count, insights_count, ago(created_at) (228), and a square grade badge whose border+text color = GRADE_COLOR[grade] || --text-muted (236,242). Investigations rows (259-301) have a left border = RISK_COLOR[risk_level] (269), header line risk_level+finding_count, a chain line iv.chain_head.slice(0,12)+'…' with full hash in title attr (292-294), memo_mode and ago(); right side a static '✓ sealed' badge in --seal-green (297-299). Inline per-row 'None yet' fallbacks (249-251, 302-306) are effectively dead because empty already hides this whole grid when both are empty — they'd only show if exactly one array is empty.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 54-63): useEffect has no AbortController and no isMounted guard; if the component unmounts before Promise.all resolves, the four setState calls fire on an unmounted component (React 18 dev warning / potential update-after-unmount). No cleanup returned.
- _low_ (line 24): ago() does no validation of the ISO string; an invalid/empty created_at yields new Date(...).getTime()===NaN, making d NaN and falling through every comparison to return 'Nd ago' with NaN. No guard.
- _low_ (line 294): iv.chain_head.slice(0,12) assumes chain_head is always a non-null string; if the API omits it, .slice throws. No optional-chaining/fallback (contrast Overview's defensive {} fetch handling).
- _low_ (line 56): Swallow-all .catch(()=>({})) means backend-down is indistinguishable from genuinely-empty history — user sees the 'No analyses yet' empty state even on a network error, with no error affordance (unlike Risk/NLP/Settings which surface 'Backend unavailable').
- _nit_ (line 249-251 / 302-306): Dead-ish conditional fallbacks: analyses.length===0 / investigations.length===0 blocks are unreachable when both are empty (empty branch wins) and only render in the mixed case; mostly redundant.
- _nit_ (line 57): Magic numbers: limit=8 hardcoded into both history URLs with no constant.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODO/FIXME. GRADE_COLOR/RISK_COLOR are hardcoded presentational maps (16-21). The per-column 'None yet' fallbacks (249-251, 302-306) are largely unreachable given the top-level empty gate. No mock data.

### `frontend/src/pages/Risk.tsx`  _(313 lines)_

**Purpose:** Fraud Risk Engine dashboard. Live decision-threshold slider recomputes model metrics and an AML alert queue; renders KPI tiles, ROC and PR ECharts, a confusion matrix, a feature-importance bar chart, and a flagged-alert table.
**Exports:** `default Risk()`
**Props/inputs:** Risk: none. Module-local helpers: Panel({title,children}) line 235; Cell({label,value,good?,bad?}) line 255 (not exported).
**State/refs:** useState threshold:number=0.5 (44); metrics: Metrics|null (45); alerts: Alert[] (46); loading:boolean=true (47); error:string (48). useRef debounce: number (49) holds the setTimeout id.
**Hooks:** `useState x4`, `useRef (debounce timer, 49)`, `useEffect (threshold-driven debounced fetch, 54-70)`, `useTheme (Zustand store, 43)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useRef,useState)`, `lucide-react (ShieldAlert imported but UNUSED, Database)`, `echarts type EChartsOption`, `../config (API_BASE)`, `../store/themeStore (useTheme)`, `../components/EChart (chart wrapper)`
**Data flow:** Live API only. useEffect keyed on [threshold] (70): clears prior debounce timer (55), sets a 180ms setTimeout (56,68) that Promise.all-fetches /api/risk/metrics?threshold=X and /api/risk/alerts?threshold=X&limit=20 (59-60), then setMetrics(m)/setAlerts(a.alerts||[])/clears error (62); catch sets 'Backend unavailable — start the API on :8000' (64); finally setLoading(false) (66). Cleanup clears the timer (69). isReal flag derived from metrics.data_source==='ulb_creditcard' (91) drives the REAL/SYNTHETIC banner. TIER_COLOR (21-23) hardcoded. No mock data.
**Styling:** Inline panelStyle/labelStyle (25-38) plus th/td padding consts (39-40). Theme-aware muted/grid colors computed from theme (51-52: dark vs light hex/rgba). KPI grid auto-fit minmax(130px,1fr) (122). Charts in fixed 1fr 1fr grid (164) and minmax(260px,0.8fr) 1.2fr grid (174). Risk-tier dot uses inline boxShadow glow keyed by TIER_COLOR (203). Alert risk bar width=`${risk_score}%` (214).

**Details:** Three guard returns before main render: loading&&!metrics shows 'Training fraud model on first load…' (72-80); error&&!metrics shows the error string in --danger (81-87); !metrics returns null (88) — so once metrics exists, transient errors on later threshold changes are swallowed silently (error state set but never shown because metrics is truthy). Debounce of 180ms (68) throttles rapid slider drags. Slider input range 0.05–0.95 step 0.01 (154-157); threshold.toFixed(2) shown in --foil-gold (149) and echoed into the confusion-matrix and alert-queue panel titles (175,189). KPIs (123-128) call .toFixed(3) on roc_auc/pr_auc/precision/recall/f1 — will throw if any field is null/undefined. Alert-queue title computes alerts.filter(a=>a.flagged).length live (189). Table rows keyed by transaction_id (200); amount via a.amount.toLocaleString() (208); is_fraud_actual truthy renders red 'FRAUD' else em-dash (220-223). EChart options are pure functions taking (data,muted,grid): rocOption (278) draws a dashed diagonal baseline series [[0,0],[1,1]] plus the smooth curve in #4d7cff with translucent area; prOption (291) single purple #a855f7 curve; importanceOption (303) sorts ascending and renders a horizontal bar (category yAxis) in --foil-gold with right-rounded corners borderRadius [0,3,3,0] and barMaxWidth 16. axis() helper (268) hardcodes nameGap 26, min 0, max default 1, JetBrains Mono fonts. Tooltip bg rgba(19,17,16,0.96) hardcoded — does not adapt to light theme.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 124-128): KPI render calls metrics.roc_auc.toFixed(3) etc. with no null-guard. If the backend returns a Metrics object missing any numeric field (or null), this throws a runtime TypeError and the page white-screens. Same risk at 165/168 (toFixed in chart titles) and 90 (cm fields).
- _medium_ (line 62-66): On threshold changes after first load, a fetch failure sets error but the render path never shows it (all three error/loading guards require !metrics). User silently sees stale metrics with no indication the live recompute failed.
- _medium_ (line 59-60): fetch().then(r=>r.json()) does not check r.ok; a 4xx/5xx returning a JSON error body resolves 'successfully' and is fed into setMetrics/setAlerts as if valid, bypassing the catch. Only network-level failures reach the error handler.
- _low_ (line 2): ShieldAlert imported from lucide-react but never used — dead import.
- _low_ (line 49): useRef<number>() initialized with no value (undefined); window.clearTimeout(undefined) on first run is harmless but the ref type is number while the uninitialized value is undefined — minor type looseness.
- _low_ (line 281): ECharts tooltip backgroundColor hardcoded to dark rgba(19,17,16,0.96) with light text #e8dfc8 (281/294/307); in light/porcelain theme the tooltip stays dark, inconsistent with the rest of the theme-aware coloring (muted/grid adapt but tooltip does not). a11y/contrast consideration.
- _low_ (line 203): TIER_COLOR[a.risk_tier] lookups (203,214) have no fallback; an unexpected tier string yields undefined color (transparent dot/bar) — contrast Overview which uses '|| --text-muted'.
- _nit_ (line 68): Magic numbers: 180ms debounce, limit=20, slider bounds 0.05/0.95/0.01 all inline with no named constants.

**Dead code / TODOs:** ShieldAlert import unused (line 2). No TODO/FIXME. TIER_COLOR (21-23) and chart line colors (#4d7cff, #a855f7) hardcoded. No mock data — fully live API.

### `frontend/src/pages/NLP.tsx`  _(295 lines)_

**Purpose:** Compliance Text Analysis page. User pastes/edits a narrative, POSTs it to the NLP analyzer, and the result (risk score/level, recommended action, signals, regulatory framework matches, and span-highlighted entities) renders in a two-column layout.
**Exports:** `default NLP()`
**Props/inputs:** None (route component). Module-local helper highlight(text, entities) (not exported).
**State/refs:** useState text:string=SAMPLE (70); result: Result|null (71); loading:boolean=false (72); error:string (73). No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState x4 (no useEffect — purely event-driven)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useState)`, `lucide-react (Loader2, FileSearch)`, `../config (API_BASE)`
**Data flow:** Live API on demand. analyze() (75-89) POSTs JSON {text} to /api/nlp/analyze with Content-Type header (78-81); if !res.ok throws Error(`Server ${status}`) (82); else setResult(await res.json()) (83); catch sets generic 'Backend unavailable — start the API on :8000' (85, which masks the Server NNN message); finally setLoading(false) (87). Hardcoded data: SAMPLE narrative string (24) used as initial textarea value and LOAD SAMPLE reset; LEVEL_COLOR (13-15), ENTITY_COLOR (16-19), FRAMEWORK_COLOR (20-22) are hardcoded color maps. No props.
**Styling:** Inline panelStyle/labelStyle (26-39). Layout grid switches from single column to '1fr 1fr' once result exists (114). Textarea is resize:vertical with inset hard shadow (137-155). Analyze button bordered in --foil-gold, dims to opacity 0.5 when disabled (175). Entities rendered via <mark> with translucent bg (color+'22' hex-alpha), 2px solid underline, square corners (49-58). Legend swatches map over ENTITY_COLOR entries (283).

**Details:** highlight(text, entities) (41-67) is the core logic: clones+sorts entities by start (42), walks a cursor, pushes plain text slices between entities and a <mark> for each entity span (48-62); skips any entity whose start < cursor (44 — overlap guard, drops overlapping/out-of-order spans); appends trailing text (65). Entity <mark> bg uses ENTITY_COLOR[label]+'22' (8-bit alpha hex) with matching 2px underline; title attr = label for hover tooltip (51). Crucially highlight() re-slices the CURRENT textarea `text` state (line 280 passes `text`, not a server-returned source), so if the user edits the textarea after analyzing without re-running, the cached result.entities offsets no longer align with the edited text and highlights drift/corrupt — a real edge case. Analyze button disabled when loading||!text.trim() (159) and shows spinning Loader2 (animation:'spin 1s linear infinite') with an inline <style> injecting @keyframes spin (184) — re-injected on every render while present. Risk verdict panel (191) left-border + big number colored by LEVEL_COLOR[result.risk_level] (194,205); score shown out of /100 (214-216). Signals list (240-244) keyed by index. Regulatory matches only render if length>0 (248), pill color by FRAMEWORK_COLOR (260-262). Entities section only renders if result.entities.length>0 (276); shows count, highlighted paragraph, and the full ENTITY_COLOR legend (always all 6 labels regardless of what was actually found).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 280): highlight() is called with the live `text` state, not a snapshot of the analyzed text. Editing the textarea after a successful analyze (without re-running) leaves stale result.entities start/end offsets pointing into mismatched text, producing misaligned/garbled highlights or dropped spans. The analyzed text should be captured alongside result.
- _medium_ (line 84-85): catch (e:any) discards the real error (including the precise `Server ${status}` thrown at 82) and always shows 'Backend unavailable'. A 500/422 from a reachable backend is misreported as the backend being down. e:any also defeats type-checking.
- _low_ (line 83): setResult(await res.json()) is not validated against the Result interface; a malformed/partial body lacking signals/entities/regulatory_matches will cause downstream .map/.length to throw (e.g. result.signals.map at 241, result.regulatory_matches.length at 248).
- _low_ (line 184): Inline <style>@keyframes spin</style> rendered inside the component tree and re-created on every render; minor — should live in a global stylesheet, and a 'spin' keyframe likely already exists elsewhere in the app.
- _low_ (line 205): LEVEL_COLOR[result.risk_level] has no fallback (used at 194/205/223/224); an unexpected level string yields undefined color for the big label/border/badge. Entities legend (283) always renders all 6 ENTITY_COLOR labels even if none appear in the result — slightly misleading as a 'legend' for the actual highlights.
- _nit_ (line 241): Signals list items keyed by array index (key={i}); fragile if list ordering/content changes. Same index-key pattern in highlight() marks (key={i}, line 50).

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODO/FIXME. SAMPLE (line 24) is hardcoded demo text (also the default textarea value). LEVEL_COLOR/ENTITY_COLOR/FRAMEWORK_COLOR are hardcoded color maps. Analysis itself is live API. e:any (84) is a type hole.

### `frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx`  _(207 lines)_

**Purpose:** Configuration page. Theme toggle (dark/light) plus a live system-status readout from /api/health (service, audit DB, GenAI mode, risk model, API auth) and a config note with external links to docs/health/metrics.
**Exports:** `default Settings()`
**Props/inputs:** None (route component).
**State/refs:** useState health: Health|null (29); err:boolean (30). No refs. Theme comes from the Zustand themeStore (28), not local state.
**Hooks:** `useState x2`, `useEffect (one-time health fetch, 32-34)`, `useTheme (Zustand, 28)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useState)`, `lucide-react (Sun, Moon, Database, Cpu, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, ExternalLink, Server)`, `../config (API_BASE)`, `../store/themeStore (useTheme — provides theme + toggle)`
**Data flow:** Live API only. useEffect on mount (empty deps, 34) fetches /api/health, .then(r=>r.json()).then(setHealth), .catch(()=>setErr(true)) (33). rows[] (36-44) is derived (not state) from health: Service (status==='healthy' => ok), Audit database (dialect + ready/unavailable, ok=!!database?.ready), GenAI mode (accent --violet if genai.startsWith('llm')), Risk model, API auth. No mock data; theme strings/colors are presentational constants.
**Styling:** Inline panelStyle (11-17, includes marginBottom:14) and labelStyle (18-25). Page capped at maxWidth:720 (47). Status rows are flex space-between with a bottom border except the last (123). Status dot color = ok? --seal-green : --danger with matching glow boxShadow (148-149). Theme toggle and link buttons use imperative onMouseEnter/Leave to animate shadow/transform/color. Theme description line shows literal hex (#131110 / #f3eee2) per theme (74).

**Details:** useTheme() supplies {theme, toggle} (28); toggle wired to the Appearance button (79) which swaps Sun/Moon icon and 'SWITCH TO LIGHT/DARK' label based on theme (99-100). Hover handlers (96-97) press the button in (shadow 2px2px->1px1px, translate 1px,1px). System-status block shows an err banner '⚠ Backend unreachable' (108-112) only when err is true; rows.map (114) renders nothing if health is null AND err is false (rows is [] until health loads — so during the brief load window the SYSTEM STATUS panel is empty with no spinner). database row uses optional chaining health.database?.ready and conditional dialect string (39). GenAI accent applied via (r as any).accent (137) — type hole, because accent only exists on one row object and the array is heterogeneous. Config note (159) embeds DATABASE_URL/GEMINI_API_KEY/VERITA_API_KEY as colored <code> (163-167). Links array (171-174) maps [label,href] tuples to target=_blank rel=noreferrer anchors pointing at API_BASE/docs, /api/health, /metrics with hover color/border animation to --foil-gold (197-198).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 32-34): Health useEffect has no cleanup/AbortController; if Settings unmounts before /api/health resolves, setHealth/setErr fire post-unmount. Same class of issue as Overview.
- _low_ (line 137): Type hole: (r as any).accent casts away the heterogeneous-array type. Only the GenAI row defines accent (40); the cast suppresses TS and would silently allow typos. The rows array could be a typed union or each row given an optional accent field.
- _low_ (line 36-44): While health is loading (and no error yet), rows===[] so the SYSTEM STATUS panel renders just its label with empty body and no loading indicator — a brief blank/janky state. No skeleton/spinner.
- _low_ (line 33): fetch(...).then(r=>r.json()) does not check r.ok; a non-2xx health response with a JSON body resolves into setHealth and is rendered as if healthy (per-row ok flags computed from possibly-absent fields), rather than tripping setErr. Only network failures set err.
- _low_ (line 40): health.genai.startsWith('llm') assumes genai is always a string; if the API returns null/omits it, .startsWith throws during rows derivation and the whole page fails to render. No guard (contrast database?. optional chaining used one line below).
- _nit_ (line 74): Hardcoded hex strings #131110 / #f3eee2 in the theme-description copy duplicate values that presumably live in the CSS token system; could drift from the actual --bg tokens.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODO/FIXME and no mock data — fully live /api/health. (r as any).accent (137) is a deliberate type-hole cast. Hardcoded theme hex strings in copy (74) duplicate token values.
