## Studio — investigation workspace (Verita frontend)

> Studio is the data-investigation workspace: a user drops a CSV/Excel file, the backend profiles it and returns a Result bundle (profile, dashboard specs, insights, relationships, quality), and the page renders a tabbed workspace over that single payload. Studio.tsx is the orchestrator — it owns the upload lifecycle (idle → scanning → reveal → ready), a draggable react-grid-layout dashboard of ECharts panels with per-panel forecast overlays, KPI row, a print-only report, and six tabs. The four child components are leaf views fed entirely by props derived from that one Result (ProfileRail, InsightsPanel) or that re-fetch their own data by datasetId (Investigator, SqlPlayground). SqlPlayground can "pin" a query result back up into Studio's dashboard grid as a synthetic bar chart, the only child→parent data flow in the set.

### `frontend/src/pages/Studio.tsx`  _(451 lines)_

**Purpose:** Top-level investigation workspace page. Manages the upload/scan/reveal/ready lifecycle, fetches the dashboard payload from POST /api/dashboard/generate, and renders a tabbed UI (Dashboard, Investigator, Key Findings, Relationships, Map, SQL). Owns the react-grid-layout draggable/resizable chart grid, KPI cards, per-chart forecast overlay, panel hide/remove, SQL-pin reconciliation, and a hidden print-only report.
**Exports:** `default Studio (React component)`
**Props/inputs:** None — route-level page, takes no props.
**State/refs:** phase ('idle'|'scanning'|'reveal'|'ready'|'error'), result (Result|null), findings (string[]), error (string), dragOver (bool), hidden (Set<string> of removed panel ids), tab (Tab union), pinned (any[] of SQL-pinned chart specs), gridLayout (RGL Layout[]), forecast ({data, loading, activeId}), gridWidth (number). Refs: inputRef (hidden file input), gridContainerRef (grid width measurement). Nested ScanOverlay has its own `tick` state.
**Hooks:** `useState (x10 in Studio + tick in ScanOverlay)`, `useRef (x2)`, `useCallback (toggleForecast, pinFromSql, upload)`, `useMemo (kpis, charts, visibleCharts, geoChart, availableTabs)`, `useEffect (ScanOverlay tick interval; gridLayout reconciliation; ResizeObserver width measurement)`, `useTheme (themeStore)`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `lucide-react (12 icons)`, `react-grid-layout (RGL + Layout type) + its css + react-resizable css`, `../config (API_BASE)`, `../store/themeStore (useTheme)`, `../components/EChart`, `../lib/chartOptions (specToOption, forecastOption, ForecastData)`, `child studio components: InsightsPanel(+Insight type), ProfileRail, RelationshipMap, SqlPlayground, Investigator, GeoMap, WhatChanged, TimeMachine`
**Data flow:** API-driven. upload() POSTs FormData to /api/dashboard/generate and stores the whole Result. toggleForecast() POSTs to /api/dashboard/forecast (periods:14). KPIs/charts/geoChart all derive from result via useMemo. pinFromSql receives a SqlPlayground result via the onPin prop and synthesizes a local 'bar' chart spec into `pinned` (the only inbound child data). Theme-derived `muted`/`grid` colors are passed down to EChart option builders, TimeMachine, and RelationshipMap.
**Styling:** Almost entirely inline styles using CSS custom properties from the paper/ink design system (var(--surface), --foil-gold, --stamp-red, --font-serif/mono/body/display, hard box-shadows like '4px 4px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.4)'). One shared `ghostBtn` CSSProperties object. Two injected <style> blocks define @keyframes spin (duplicated). react-grid-layout/react-resizable CSS imported globally.

**Details:** LIFECYCLE: phase machine idle→scanning→reveal→ready→error. upload() sets 'scanning', awaits fetch, builds 5 REAL findings strings from the response profile/quality/insights, sets 'reveal', then a setTimeout(1600ms) commits result and flips to 'ready'. ScanOverlay (lines 72-109): self-incrementing tick via setInterval(450ms) (cleaned up); when findings is empty it slices GENERIC_STEPS to tick+1 (capped at 7) for a fake-progress effect, last line shows ▸ in gold, prior lines ✓ in green; once real findings arrive all show ✓ green. Sweeping scan-line is a framer-motion loop top:0%→100%→0% over 2.4s infinite. GRID: buildLayout() (24-34) left-packs charts into a 12-col grid using SPAN map {line:12,heatmap:6,bar:6,pie:4,histogram:6} default 6; heatmap height 6 rows else 5; minW:3 minH:4; wraps row when x+w>12. The reconciliation effect (200-205) merges previous dragged positions by id so existing panels keep their position and only new (e.g. pinned) panels get fresh slots — explicitly fixing a 'rearrange forgets' bug. WIDTH: deliberately NOT using RGL WidthProvider — a manual ResizeObserver (216-229) measures gridContainerRef and only renders <RGL> when gridWidth>0 (338); comment says WidthProvider latched onto near-zero width during the scan transition and never recovered. RGL config: cols=12, rowHeight=64, margin=[16,16], draggableHandle='.drag-handle', isBounded. FORECAST: toggleForecast (128-143) is a 3-way toggle — if already active for this chart, deactivate; if data already cached, just switch activeId (does NOT refetch — cache shared across all line charts); else fetch once. Only line charts get the Forecast button; showForecast requires isLine && activeId===c.id && data. Forecast title badge shows winning method, backtest MAPE, '(beat N others)', and a tooltip listing tournament model MAPEs. PANELS: each panel has drag-handle (GripVertical + ellipsized title), optional forecast button, and an X remove button that adds id to `hidden` Set. KPIs rendered separately (filtered chart_type==='kpi') in an auto-fit minmax(170px,1fr) grid with staggered entrance (delay i*0.06); danger accent colors the value red. 'New file' button resets result/pinned/gridLayout/forecast and returns to idle. window.print() triggers the hidden #verita-report (display:none) which uses pt units and serif/monospace web-safe fonts for clean PDF. TABS: availableTabs hides 'map' unless geoChart exists (geoChart = first dashboard chart whose dimension is in profile.geos). EDGE CASES/NOTES: result! and r=result! non-null assertions after phase==='ready' guard. pinFromSql picks first string column as label and first numeric (≠label) as value, bails if no numeric, slices to 12 rows, priority:99, id=`pin_${Date.now()}`. body.detail used as error text on non-ok. Magic numbers: 450ms tick, 1600ms reveal delay, 2.4s scan loop, periods:14 forecast, 12-row pin cap, rowHeight 64.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 128-143, 339-378): Forecast data is cached globally (single forecast.data) but keyed to one activeId. Toggling forecast on a different line chart reuses the FIRST chart's forecast series rather than fetching that chart's own forecast — every line chart shows the same forecast overlay. If multiple line charts exist this is incorrect data.
- _medium_ (line 187): setTimeout(1600ms) that commits result/phase has no cleanup. If the component unmounts (route change) during the reveal window, the timer fires setState on an unmounted component — React warning / potential leak. No ref/clearTimeout guard.
- _medium_ (line 143): toggleForecast useCallback depends on the whole `forecast` object, so it is recreated on every forecast state change (loading, data, activeId), defeating memoization and re-creating the handler passed to each panel button repeatedly.
- _low_ (line 138): On forecast fetch failure the catch only clears loading; no user-visible error. Button silently does nothing on failure (body.detail thrown but swallowed).
- _low_ (line 169): res.json().catch(()=>({})) then `body.detail` — if JSON parse fails body is {} and detail is undefined, falling back to generic message; if the server returns ok:false with non-JSON the error is opaque.
- _low_ (line 145-157): pinFromSql column heuristic inspects only rows[0] to infer string/number types; a column null in the first row but numeric elsewhere is mis-typed. valueCol must differ from labelCol or pin silently no-ops with no user feedback.
- _low_ (line 21, 28): SPAN map and heatmap height 6 are magic numbers; 'kpi' charts are filtered out before buildLayout but SPAN has no 'kpi' key (handled by the ||6 default, harmless).
- _nit_ (line 290, 410): Report button calls window.print() directly; #verita-report has inline display:none, so print visibility depends on a print stylesheet elsewhere overriding it — coupling not visible in this file.
- _nit_ (line 41-46): Result interface types profile/quality/dashboard as `any`, losing type safety across the whole page (charts, kpis, geoChart all `any`).

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODO/FIXME. @keyframes spin is defined twice (lines 90/105 region) inside one component and also redefined in child components. `theme` from useTheme is used only to compute muted/grid colors. The `Insight` type is imported only to type the Result.insights field.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/Investigator.tsx`  _(190 lines)_

**Purpose:** Auditable Compliance Investigator tab. Runs an autonomous AML/financial-crime agent over the dataset via POST /api/agent/investigate, then renders the verdict (risk level + recommended action), a SHA-256 hash-chain verification badge, an expandable reasoning timeline where each step shows its SQL query / result table / hash link, and a compliance memo.
**Exports:** `default Investigator (React component)`
**Props/inputs:** { datasetId: string }
**State/refs:** phase ('idle'|'running'|'done'|'error'), result (Result|null), error (string), open (number|null — index of the expanded timeline step).
**Hooks:** `useState (x4)`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `lucide-react (ScanSearch, Loader2, ShieldCheck, ChevronDown, Link2, FileText, CircleCheck, CircleSlash, CircleDot)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`
**Data flow:** API-driven. run() POSTs {dataset_id} to /api/agent/investigate; the entire Result (verdict, trace steps, chain, memo) is stored and rendered. No mock data. datasetId comes from Studio prop.
**Styling:** Inline styles. Uses the LEGACY dark/glass design system ('glass' className, gradient buttons linear-gradient(135deg,#6366f1,#a855f7), rounded corners borderRadius 8-18, soft glows like '0 10px 40px -8px rgba(168,85,247,0.6)') — contrary to the canonical paper/ink Forensic Ledger system. SEV color map for severities. Hardcoded hex colors throughout (#16c784 green, #ff2d55 red, #a855f7 purple).

**Details:** run() sets 'running', POSTs, throws on non-ok using body.detail, then await new Promise(setTimeout 700ms) as a deliberate 'dramatic pause so the agent feels like it's working' before setting result+'done'. The idle/running/error states share one centered glass card with a gradient icon tile (ScanSearch, or spinning Loader2 when running) and a Run button; error appends ⚠ message. DONE view: (1) verdict grid 1.4fr/1fr — left card borderLeft colored by SEV[risk_level] showing '{level} risk', '{confirmed_count} confirmed / {tests_run} tests', and recommended_action in a tinted box (`${color}1f` = ~12% alpha hex suffix); right card shows chain.verified (green ShieldCheck or red 'Chain broken'), chain.length steps, and head hash truncated to 32 chars + '…'. (2) Reasoning timeline maps r.trace: a step isTest unless id==='baseline'||'synthesis'; non-test steps use blue CircleDot, test steps use CircleCheck (confirmed, colored by severity) or CircleSlash (cleared, muted); a vertical connector line is drawn between nodes except the last (i < length-1). Each card staggers in with delay 0.05*i. Test steps show a badge '{severity} · confirmed' or 'cleared'. If s.query exists, a toggle button (ChevronDown rotates 180° when open) expands an AnimatePresence height:0→auto block containing the SQL <pre>, a result table (first row's keys as headers, slice(0,5) rows, null rendered as ∅), and a hash link 'prev_hash[:10]… → hash[:10]…'. open is a single index so only one step expands at a time. (3) Memo card: header with memo_mode pill (purple if mode startsWith 'llm', else muted), the memo in a pre-wrapped <pre>, and a 'Re-run investigation' button calling run() again. EDGE CASES: open compares strictly to i; re-click sets null. result! non-null assertion after the early-return guard. Magic numbers: 700ms pause, slice(0,5) result rows, [:10]/[:32] hash truncations, delay 0.05*i.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 55-84): The idle/running/error early-return only covers those three phases. A transient error during a RE-RUN replaces the whole done view (verdict/trace/memo) with the empty intro card, losing the visible prior result even though it's still in state.
- _medium_ (line 47-48): Artificial await setTimeout(700) on every run adds purely cosmetic latency to a compliance tool, and has no cleanup if unmounted mid-pause (setState-after-unmount risk).
- _low_ (line 160-161): Result table headers derive from Object.keys(s.result[0]) and body cells from Object.values(row); heterogeneous key order across rows can misalign columns and values. Assumes s.result[0] exists (guarded by s.result?.length>0).
- _low_ (line 30, 87, 140): SEV map is keyed by exact 'Critical'|'High'|'Medium'|'Low'. An unexpected risk_level/severity yields undefined → invalid CSS color and `${undefined}1f` background. No fallback.
- _low_ (line 128): Timeline list uses key={i} (array index). Stable for static trace, fragile if it ever reorders/animates.
- _low_ (line 79): Raw server error string (body.detail) rendered into the UI. React escapes text so it's XSS-safe, but it leaks raw backend error detail to end users.
- _nit_ (line 1-3): Uses the legacy glass/aurora visual system and hardcoded gradient hex, conflicting with CLAUDE.md/DESIGN.md guidance that the paper/ink Forensic Ledger system is canonical and glass should be maintained-not-extended.
- _nit_ (line 18-28): Result interface duplicates the name 'Result' used in Studio.tsx (different shape); module-scoped so not a conflict but easy to confuse.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. The component injects @keyframes spin via <style> (line 80) duplicating the same keyframe defined in sibling components — should be global. memo_mode/severity strings are stringly-typed.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/SqlPlayground.tsx`  _(154 lines)_

**Purpose:** SQL Playground tab — a real SQL console (DuckDB, read-only, table registered as `data`) against the uploaded dataset. Provides a plain-English→SQL translation bar, a SQL editor with run, a paginated/scrollable result table, and an optional 'Pin to dashboard' action that lifts the result up to Studio as a chart.
**Exports:** `default SqlPlayground (React component)`
**Props/inputs:** { datasetId: string; onPin?: (result: QueryResult, sql: string) => void }
**State/refs:** pinnedMsg (string — transient confirmation text), sql (string — editor contents, seeded with a default GROUP BY channel query), question (string — NL input), result (QueryResult|null), error (string), running (bool), translating (bool).
**Hooks:** `useState (x7)`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion)`, `lucide-react (Play, Sparkles, Loader2, Database, Pin)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`
**Data flow:** API-driven. run(overrideSql?) POSTs {dataset_id, sql} to /api/sql/query → {columns, rows, row_count, truncated, elapsed_ms}. translate() POSTs {dataset_id, question} to /api/sql/translate → {sql}, sets the editor, then auto-runs the returned SQL. onPin (prop callback to Studio) sends the current result+sql upward to create a pinned dashboard chart. datasetId from Studio prop.
**Styling:** Inline styles, legacy glass system ('glass' className, borderRadius 8-10, gradient AI button linear-gradient(120deg,#6366f1,#a855f7), var(--signature) run button). Two extracted CSSProperties consts (aiBtn, runBtn). Monospace font for SQL editor and numeric cells; sticky table headers (position:sticky top:0 background var(--bg-2)). Injected @keyframes spin.

**Details:** EDITOR: textarea seeded with a 3-line default query (SELECT channel, COUNT(*), ROUND(AVG(amount),2) … GROUP BY channel ORDER BY avg_amount DESC), spellCheck false, resize vertical, rows=6. Ctrl/Cmd+Enter in the textarea runs the current sql (94). The 'Run' button calls run() (no arg → uses state sql). NL BAR: input with Enter key handler calling translate(); translate bails if question is blank (trim), sets translating, fetches SQL, sets it into the editor, then awaits run(body.sql) so it shows AND executes. RESULTS: rendered only when result truthy; header shows row_count, '(truncated)' if truncated, and elapsed_ms. Pin button shown only if onPin && row_count>0; on click it calls onPin(result, sql), sets pinnedMsg='Pinned to dashboard ✓', and setTimeout(2500ms) clears the message (button label uses pinnedMsg || 'Pin to dashboard'). TABLE: scroll container maxHeight 420 with sticky headers; cells render null as ∅ in muted color, numbers in mono font (typeof row[c]==='number'); column iteration uses result.columns (consistent ordering, unlike Investigator's Object.values approach). ERROR handling: run/translate throw on non-ok (body.detail) and display ⚠ error; run clears result on error, translate leaves prior result. Magic numbers: rows=6, 500-row cap (only stated in helper text, enforced server-side), maxHeight 420, 2500ms message timeout.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 14-17, 45-62): Doc comment claims the NL translation is 'shown for review, never auto-run blindly', but translate() calls `await run(body.sql)` immediately after setSql — it DOES auto-run the model-generated SQL. Behavior contradicts the stated safety promise.
- _low_ (line 116): Pin confirmation uses setTimeout(2500) with no cleanup; unmount within 2.5s fires setState on an unmounted component. Rapid repeated pins stack timers, each resetting the label.
- _low_ (line 55): translate() sets sql from body.sql without validating it exists/non-empty; a malformed translate response sets the editor to undefined and runs undefined as SQL.
- _low_ (line 134-141): Result rows use key={i} (index); acceptable for static results but mis-reconciles if the same table re-renders shorter (residual rows). Numeric detection typeof row[c]==='number' fails for numeric strings returned from the DB.
- _low_ (line 69-75, 91-102): Accessibility: neither the NL input nor the SQL textarea has an aria-label/label; placeholder is the only affordance. Enter handling differs between the two inputs (Enter translates vs Ctrl+Enter runs) with no guard for IME composition / Shift+Enter.
- _nit_ (line 27-43): run() has no client guard beyond the disabled button; pressing Ctrl+Enter repeatedly can fire overlapping requests — in-flight requests aren't aborted.
- _nit_ (line 1-3): Legacy glass styling and hardcoded gradient hex, same DESIGN.md drift as Investigator.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. Helper text 'results capped at 500 rows' is hardcoded UI copy assuming a server-side cap; stale if the backend cap changes. @keyframes spin duplicated again.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.tsx`  _(86 lines)_

**Purpose:** Key Findings tab content — renders the auto-generated executive summary plus a grid of insight cards, each expandable to reveal the exact computation/evidence that produced it ('show the work' auditability).
**Exports:** `default InsightsPanel (React component)`, `Insight (interface, re-used by Studio.tsx)`
**Props/inputs:** { insights: Insight[]; summary: string }
**State/refs:** open (number|null — index of the currently expanded insight card; single-open accordion).
**Hooks:** `useState (x1)`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion)`, `lucide-react (ChevronDown, Lightbulb)`
**Data flow:** Pure props. Receives insights[] and summary from Studio (which got them from /api/dashboard/generate). No fetching, no mock data. Insight = {kind, icon, severity:'info'|'warning', text, evidence}.
**Styling:** Inline styles, legacy glass ('glass' className). Executive summary card has borderLeft var(--blue); insight cards borderLeft red (var(--danger)) when severity==='warning' else var(--accent,#12b5a3) teal fallback. Findings grid is responsive: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(320px, 1fr)). Evidence block is monospace, pre-wrap.

**Details:** Executive summary card animates in (opacity/y) once. Findings rendered in an auto-fill grid; each card staggers entrance with transition delay 0.06*i. Each card shows the insight icon (emoji string ins.icon rendered as text at 1.1rem) + text, and a 'how was this computed?' toggle button (ChevronDown rotates 180° when open). When open===i, a motion.code block expands (initial height:0 → auto) showing ins.evidence in monospace pre-wrap. Single-open accordion: clicking an open card closes it (open===i ? null : i). EDGE CASES: empty insights[] renders just the summary + an empty grid (no empty-state message). The expand animation has initial+animate but NO exit, and is conditionally mounted (open===i &&) rather than wrapped in AnimatePresence, so the collapse is instant (no exit animation) — asymmetric with the open animation. Magic numbers: minmax 320px, delay 0.06*i, rotate 180deg, transition .2s.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 40, 56-58, 68): Cards use key={i} (array index) and `open` tracks an index; if insights ever reorder/filter, the expanded card and animations bind to the wrong item. Stable only because the list is static.
- _low_ (line 68-80): Expand/collapse asymmetry: the evidence block is mounted via `open === i &&` with an enter animation but no AnimatePresence/exit, so closing snaps instantly while opening animates height 0→auto.
- _low_ (line 56-67): Accessibility: the expand toggle is a <button> with no aria-expanded / aria-controls, and the revealed evidence has no programmatic association — fails the project's stated WCAG AAA bar for disclosure widgets.
- _low_ (line 49): Severity→color mapping is binary ('warning'→danger red, anything else→teal). An unexpected severity value silently maps to the 'info' teal styling; no visual distinction for other levels.
- _nit_ (line 53): ins.icon is rendered as raw text (assumed emoji); a non-emoji/empty string leaves a blank leading column with the gap intact.
- _nit_ (line 1-3): Legacy glass styling, same DESIGN.md drift as the other children.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs, no dead code. The `kind` field of Insight is never used in rendering (only icon/severity/text/evidence are).

### `frontend/src/components/studio/ProfileRail.tsx`  _(109 lines)_

**Purpose:** The Data Profile rail (Dashboard tab sidebar) — an instant data-quality audit: an animated SVG quality-score ring with letter grade, score deductions list, a 2x2 dataset-stats grid, and per-column mini-cards showing role badge, completeness bar, unique count, null %, and mean for measures.
**Exports:** `default ProfileRail (React component)`
**Props/inputs:** { profile: any; quality: Quality } where Quality = {score, grade, duplicate_rows, deductions:{reason,points}[]}
**State/refs:** None (stateless / purely presentational).
**Hooks:** `none (only framer-motion declarative animation)`
**Dependencies:** `framer-motion (motion)`
**Data flow:** Pure props from Studio (profile, quality originate from /api/dashboard/generate). No fetching, no local state. profile is typed `any` (accesses row_count, column_count, memory_kb, columns[] with name/semantic_type/missing_pct/unique_count/mean).
**Styling:** Inline styles, legacy glass ('glass' className) with maxHeight calc(100vh - 140px) and overflow:auto (scrolls independently). ROLE_COLORS map drives per-column badge colors (measure teal, dimension/geo blue, temporal purple, identifier slate, boolean amber, text indigo). gradeColor computed by score thresholds (>=90 green, >=75 blue, >=60 amber, else red). Color-with-alpha pattern `${color}1f` for tinted badge backgrounds.

**Details:** QUALITY RING: SVG 64x64, background circle r=26 stroke var(--surface-2), foreground motion.circle with strokeDasharray = ring (2π·26 ≈ 163.36) and animate strokeDashoffset from ring → ring*(1 - score/100), rotate(-90 32 32) so it sweeps from top, duration 1.1s easeOut. Grade letter centered as SVG <text> hardcoded fontFamily 'JetBrains Mono'. DEDUCTIONS: rendered only if deductions.length>0, each row reason + '−{points}' in danger color. STATS GRID: 2x2 array literal [Rows, Columns, Memory KB, Duplicates] mapped to cards; row_count/memory_kb use toLocaleString(). COLUMN CARDS: profile.columns.map — badge color from ROLE_COLORS[semantic_type] default slate #8c97b5; completeness bar is a 3px track with inner bar width=`${100 - missing_pct}%`, colored danger if missing_pct>20 else role color at opacity 0.8; footer row shows '{unique_count} uniq', '{missing_pct}% null' or 'complete', and for measures with mean!=null a 'μ {mean}' formatted with maximumFractionDigits:0. Magic numbers: r=26 (ring radius), strokeWidth 6, score thresholds 90/75/60, missing_pct>20 danger threshold, 3px bar height, maxHeight offset 140px.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 41-43): Quality ring grade <text> hardcodes fontFamily='JetBrains Mono' instead of var(--font-mono); if the design token font changes or the font isn't loaded this diverges from the rest of the UI.
- _low_ (line 25, 84): Color thresholds (gradeColor) and ROLE_COLORS are hardcoded hex rather than design-system tokens, so these specific colors don't follow theme changes (ring/grade and role badges are fixed regardless of light/dark theme).
- _low_ (line 24, 65-67, 83-100): profile is typed `any`; every field access (row_count, memory_kb, columns[].missing_pct/unique_count/mean/semantic_type) is unchecked. A missing/renamed field throws at render (e.g. profile.memory_kb.toLocaleString() on undefined) with no guard or fallback.
- _low_ (line 95): Completeness bar width = `${100 - col.missing_pct}%`; if missing_pct exceeds 100 or is negative (bad data) the bar width becomes negative/over-100% with no clamp. The >20 danger threshold is a magic number.
- _nit_ (line 53, 83): Deduction rows use key={i} (index); column cards correctly use key={col.name}. Data is static so low impact.
- _nit_ (line 29): maxHeight calc(100vh - 140px) hardcodes a 140px header offset; if the Studio header height changes the rail's scroll viewport misaligns.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs, no dead code.

---

## Studio visualization/analysis panels (GeoMap, RelationshipMap, TimeMachine, WhatChanged)

> Four presentation components in the Studio dashboard layer that turn auto-detected dataset structure into interactive visualizations. GeoMap and RelationshipMap are stateless/near-stateless ECharts wrappers driven entirely by props (choropleth world map and force-directed column-relationship graph). TimeMachine and WhatChanged are self-fetching panels that POST a datasetId to the FastAPI `/api/dashboard/*` endpoints and render the responses (a scrub/play time series of KPIs + racing bar chart, and a first-half-vs-second-half movers comparison). All four render via the shared `../EChart` wrapper (echarts) except WhatChanged which is pure styled divs + framer-motion + lucide icons. The design uses the legacy dark "glass"/aurora system (CSS vars + hardcoded hex), not the canonical paper/ink Forensic Ledger identity.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/GeoMap.tsx`  _(91 lines)_

**Purpose:** Auto-generated world choropleth that appears when Verita detects a geo column in a dataset. Maps ISO-2 country codes to ECharts world-map country names and shades countries by an aggregated value, with a graceful bar-list fallback when the world GeoJSON cannot be loaded (offline).
**Exports:** `default GeoMap`
**Props/inputs:** { data: { label: string; value: number }[]; title: string } — `data` is pre-aggregated label/value pairs where label is expected to be an ISO-2 code; `title` is the panel heading.
**State/refs:** const [state, setState] = useState<'loading'|'ready'|'fallback'>(...) — single state-machine string. Initial value derived from module-level `_worldLoaded` (line 25): if the GeoJSON was already registered by a previous mount it starts 'ready', else 'loading'. No refs. Transitions: loading->ready on fetch success; loading->fallback on fetch/parse error.
**Hooks:** `useState`, `useEffect`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useState)`, `echarts (* as echarts, for echarts.registerMap)`, `../EChart (shared chart wrapper)`, `echarts type EChartsOption`
**Data flow:** Pure props in (`data`/`title`). Side-fetch of static GeoJSON from a CDN (jsDelivr echarts@4.9.0 world.json) on first mount; registered globally via echarts.registerMap('world', geo). No backend API. Module-level `_worldLoaded` flag (line 18) caches registration across all instances/mounts for the page lifetime.
**Styling:** Inline styles + className 'glass' (legacy dark aurora system). Heavy use of hardcoded hex (#1b2440, #4d7cff, #22d3ee, #a855f7, rgba(11,14,26,0.92), #f2f5ff, #8c97b5) for the ECharts option, mixed with CSS vars (var(--text-muted), var(--font-display), var(--font-mono), var(--border)) for the surrounding chrome.

**Details:** Effect (lines 27-37): early-returns if `_worldLoaded` is already true (so it does NOT re-register or re-set state). Otherwise fetch(WORLD_GEOJSON_URL).then(r=>r.json()).then registers the map, sets the module flag, and transitions to 'ready'; any failure (.catch line 36) transitions to 'fallback'. Empty dependency array — runs once per mount. Three render branches: 'loading' returns a centered 'Loading world map…' glass card (lines 39-41); 'fallback' renders the raw data as a label/value bar list with value.toLocaleString() and an honest '(map unavailable offline)' title (lines 43-54); 'ready' builds the choropleth. Data mapping (lines 56-58): each label is uppercased and looked up in the ISO2 dictionary, falling back to the raw label, then `.filter((d)=>d.name)` — note ISO2 lookup always yields a truthy string (or the original label) so the filter effectively never drops anything except empty-string labels. `max` (line 59) is Math.max over values with a floor of 1 to avoid a zero-range visualMap. ECharts option: tooltip with custom formatter showing localized value or 'no activity' when value is falsy (line 64); visualMap min 0..max with a 3-stop gradient #1b2440->#4d7cff->#22d3ee, calculable, anchored bottom-left (lines 66-70); single 'map' series type 'world' with roam:true (drag/zoom), faint area/border fill, emphasis area color #a855f7, select disabled. EChart fixed height 440. Magic numbers: GeoJSON pinned to echarts@4.9.0 (CDN version may mismatch the installed echarts), height 440, visualMap left/bottom 10. The ISO2 table has a wrong/ambiguous mapping: KP ('Korea') — line 13 maps North Korea's ISO-2 'KP' to the generic name 'Korea', which will not match the ECharts world.json country name and silently shades nothing; South Korea 'KR' is missing entirely.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 29-37): fetch() has no AbortController / no cleanup. If GeoMap unmounts before the CDN GeoJSON resolves, the .then calls setState on an unmounted component (React warns) and registers the map post-unmount. Add an abort controller or `cancelled` guard.
- _medium_ (line 17,32): World GeoJSON is loaded from a hardcoded third-party CDN (cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/echarts@4.9.0/map/json/world.json). External network dependency: blocked CDN, CSP, or offline => silent fallback to a bar list. Version 4.9.0 is also decoupled from the project's installed echarts version. Consider bundling the geojson locally.
- _low_ (line 57-58): .filter((d)=>d.name) is effectively dead/no-op: name is `ISO2[...] || d.label`, which is always a truthy non-empty string for any normal label, so unrecognized country codes are NOT filtered out — they are passed to ECharts as unknown map names and silently ignored by the renderer (no honest 'unmapped' feedback to the user).
- _low_ (line 13): ISO2['KP'] = 'Korea' is incorrect/ambiguous. KP is North Korea; ECharts world.json uses 'Dem. Rep. Korea'. Value will silently not render. South Korea 'KR' is also missing entirely from the table.
- _low_ (line 18,33): Module-level mutable singleton `_worldLoaded` is shared global state. In dev StrictMode double-invoke or across tests it can mask re-registration logic; also if the first fetch fails (state 'fallback') the flag stays false so every subsequent mount re-attempts the fetch.
- _nit_ (line 64): tooltip formatter uses `p.value` truthiness — a legitimate value of 0 renders as 'no activity', conflating 'zero activity' with 'no data'.
- _nit_ (line 63-69): Colors hardcoded as hex/rgba inside the ECharts option rather than CSS tokens, so the map will not follow theme changes and diverges from the design-system token approach used elsewhere.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/RelationshipMap.tsx`  _(84 lines)_

**Purpose:** Force-directed graph visualizing statistical relationships between dataset columns: numeric↔numeric edges as Pearson r, category→measure edges as eta² (variance explained). Nodes are colored by column type (measure/dimension/temporal); edge color/width encode sign and strength.
**Exports:** `default RelationshipMap`
**Props/inputs:** { nodes: GraphNode[]; edges: GraphEdge[]; muted: string } where GraphNode = { id: string; type: string } and GraphEdge = { source: string; target: string; weight: number; sign: string; kind: string; label: string }. `muted` is a passed-down CSS color string for legend/edge-label text.
**State/refs:** None. Stateless functional component — purely derives an ECharts option from props on every render.
**Dependencies:** `../EChart`, `echarts type EChartsOption`
**Data flow:** 100% props-driven (nodes/edges/muted). No fetch, no state, no module globals. The statistics (Pearson/eta²) are computed upstream and passed in.
**Styling:** Inline styles + 'glass' className. Node category palette TYPE_COLOR (line 7) hardcoded hex: measure #12b5a3, dimension #4d7cff, temporal #a855f7. Edge colors hardcoded (#ff4d5e negative, #4d7cff eta², #12b5a3 pearson). Surrounding chrome uses CSS vars (var(--font-display/mono), var(--text-muted)).

**Details:** Empty-state guard (lines 14-21): if `!nodes.length`, renders an honest 'No statistically meaningful relationships found (|r| ≥ 0.25 or η² ≥ 0.05)' card — thresholds are documented in copy here, implying upstream filtering. ECharts graph series (lines 35-70): layout 'force', roam + draggable enabled, force tuning repulsion 320 / edgeLength [110,200] / gravity 0.12 (magic numbers). Three categories map to the type palette. Node mapping (lines 48-56): name=id; category coerces type 'geo' to 'dimension' (so geo nodes reuse dimension color — 'geo' is not in TYPE_COLOR, hence the coercion); symbolSize = 16 + 10*min(3, degree) where degree = count of edges touching the node — this is an O(nodes × edges) recompute inside .map (line 51); itemStyle shadowBlur 18 colored by the (coerced) type with fallback #4d7cff. Link mapping (lines 57-68): spreads the raw edge (so weight/sign/kind/label remain on p.data for the tooltip), sets a per-edge label (formatter = e.label, color = muted), lineStyle width = 1 + weight*5, color chosen by sign/kind, opacity 0.55, curveness 0.12. Cast `as any` at line 68 to satisfy types because the link object carries extra raw fields plus a structured `label`. Tooltip formatter (lines 26-29) branches on p.dataType 'edge' vs node and prints '↔' between source/target with kind translated to human text. Legend bottom-anchored, circle icons, data order ['measure','dimension','temporal'] (line 33). EChart fixed height 420.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 51): symbolSize computes node degree with edges.filter(...).length inside nodes.map — O(N×E). Fine for the small graphs implied by the strict |r|/η² thresholds, but a latent perf trap if thresholds loosen. Precompute a degree map once.
- _low_ (line 57-68): links.map spreads `...e` AND then overwrites `label` (string from edge) with a structured ECharts label object, and re-sets source/target to the same values. The spread carries arbitrary raw props (weight/sign/kind plus the original string `label`) into the ECharts link, relying on the trailing structured `label` to win — fragile and the reason for the `as any` cast.
- _low_ (line 68): `as any` cast defeats type checking on the links array; a malformed edge (e.g. missing weight) would silently produce NaN width (1 + undefined*5) with no compile-time guard.
- _nit_ (line 50,54): 'geo' node type is special-cased to 'dimension' in two places (category and shadow color) but is not a declared category; any other unknown type falls through to category=type (which won't match a legend category) and shadow fallback #4d7cff — unknown types render uncolored/uncategorized without warning.
- _nit_ (line 7,63): Palette and edge colors hardcoded hex, not theme tokens — won't respond to theming and duplicates color definitions also present in sibling panels (GeoMap).
- _nit_ (line 47,61): fontFamily 'JetBrains Mono' is a hardcoded string literal rather than the var(--font-mono) token used in the wrapper chrome — inconsistent and breaks if the mono font is renamed.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/TimeMachine.tsx`  _(95 lines)_

**Purpose:** Interactive 'Time Machine' — fetches per-period (monthly) aggregate frames for a dataset and lets the user scrub a range slider or auto-play through time. Shows three KPI tiles (records, total, mean) and an animated horizontal 'racing bars' breakdown by a dimension that re-aggregates per frame.
**Exports:** `default TimeMachine`
**Props/inputs:** { datasetId: string; muted: string; grid: string } — datasetId drives the fetch; muted and grid are CSS color strings for axis labels / split lines passed into the ECharts option.
**State/refs:** frames: Frame[] (fetched series), idx: number (current frame index), playing: boolean (autoplay toggle), measure: string (label of the aggregated measure). Ref: timer = useRef<number>() holding the setInterval id.
**Hooks:** `useState x4`, `useRef (timer)`, `useEffect (fetch on datasetId)`, `useEffect (autoplay interval on playing/frames.length)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useRef, useState)`, `framer-motion (motion)`, `lucide-react (Play, Pause, History)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`, `../EChart`, `echarts type EChartsOption`
**Data flow:** Real backend fetch: POST {API_BASE}/api/dashboard/frames with body { dataset_id }. Response shape { frames: Frame[], measure: string }. On error sets frames to [] (which then renders nothing). No mock data. Frame = { period, rows, total, mean, by_dimension?: { dimension, data: {label,value}[] } }.
**Styling:** framer-motion `motion.div` with className 'glass' and an entrance animation (opacity/y). Inline styles throughout; CSS vars for surfaces/text (var(--surface), var(--surface-2), var(--text), var(--text-muted), var(--blue), var(--border), var(--font-display/mono)) mixed with hardcoded hex (#22d3ee accents, #4d7cff bars). Range input accentColor #22d3ee.

**Details:** Fetch effect (lines 24-32): POSTs dataset_id, awaits json, throws b.detail on !r.ok; on success setFrames(b.frames), setMeasure(b.measure), and crucially setIdx(b.frames.length - 1) so it OPENS on the most recent period. On any error .catch sets frames to [] (silent — combined with the `frames.length < 2` guard at line 45 the whole component renders null, hiding errors quietly by design). Autoplay effect (lines 34-43): if !playing, clears the interval and returns; else starts a 900ms setInterval that advances idx, and when i+1 >= frames.length it calls setPlaying(false) and holds (stops at the end, no loop). Cleanup clears the interval. Dependency array [playing, frames.length] — depends on frames.length not frames, so a frames replacement of equal length would not restart the timer. f = frames[idx] (line 47). maxVal (line 48) = Math.max over every frame's by_dimension values (flatMap with `|| [0]`) so the bar x-axis max is FIXED across all frames — this is what makes the 'racing bars' comparable frame-to-frame. barOption (lines 50-62) only built if f.by_dimension exists, else {}: horizontal bar (xAxis value capped at maxVal, yAxis category inverse with labels), animationDurationUpdate 700 + cubicOut easing for the racing-bar morph between frames, bars color #4d7cff with right-rounded radius [0,4,4,0], barMaxWidth 16, right-positioned localized value labels. Render: header with History icon (#22d3ee) + current f.period in blue mono; play/pause button toggling `playing`; range input min0..max frames.length-1 that on change FORCES setPlaying(false) (scrubbing stops playback) and setIdx(parseInt(value)). KPI column (lines 83-90) maps a literal array of [label,value] pairs (Records / Total {measure} / Avg {measure}) with toLocaleString(). Bar chart rendered only when f.by_dimension truthy. Magic numbers: 900ms play interval, 700ms morph, barMaxWidth 16, EChart height 170/grid left 90.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 24-31): fetch effect has no AbortController/cleanup and no loading or error UI. Switching datasetId rapidly or unmounting mid-flight can setFrames on an unmounted component (React state-on-unmount warning) and create a race where a stale response overwrites a newer one. On failure frames stays [] and the component silently renders null (catch at line 31) — a genuine backend/network error is indistinguishable from 'dataset has <2 periods', with no logging.
- _low_ (line 34-43): Autoplay interval restarts whenever `playing` or `frames.length` changes. When playback reaches the end it calls setPlaying(false) from inside the setIdx updater (line 38) — calling a sibling state setter inside another setter's callback works because React batches, but is slightly surprising. No loop-back to start when finished.
- _low_ (line 38): When playback is at the last frame and the user presses Play, the interval fires once, i+1 >= frames.length is immediately true, so it sets playing false and never advances — pressing Play at the end is a no-op with a brief 900ms flicker of the pause icon. Should reset idx to 0 on play-from-end.
- _low_ (line 35): window.clearInterval(timer.current) runs with timer.current === undefined on the first non-playing effect run; harmless (clearInterval tolerates undefined) but relies on that behavior. timer ref typed useRef<number>() initialized undefined.
- _nit_ (line 72,76): Range input and Play/Pause button have no aria-label / associated <label>; the button is icon-only. Against the project's stated WCAG AAA bar the scrubber and play control are not screen-reader labeled.
- _nit_ (line 84): f.total.toLocaleString() and f.mean.toLocaleString() assume numbers; if the backend ever returns null/undefined for an empty period this throws. No defensive coercion.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/WhatChanged.tsx`  _(81 lines)_

**Purpose:** 'What changed?' panel — fetches a first-half-vs-second-half comparison of the dataset's time range and renders three headline delta KPIs (total/avg/volume % change) plus the top-5 biggest category movers (≥15% shift, ranked). Hides itself entirely when the dataset has no usable time axis.
**Exports:** `default WhatChanged`
**Props/inputs:** { datasetId: string } — sole prop, drives the fetch.
**State/refs:** headline: Headline | null, movers: Mover[], error: string. No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState x3`, `useEffect (fetch on datasetId)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useState)`, `framer-motion (motion)`, `lucide-react (GitCompareArrows, TrendingUp, TrendingDown)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`
**Data flow:** Real backend fetch: POST {API_BASE}/api/dashboard/compare with body { dataset_id }. Response { headline: Headline, movers: Mover[] }. Headline carries period_a/period_b ({from,to,rows}), three nullable %-change fields, and measure. Mover = { dimension, category, before, after, change_pct }. No mock data. No ECharts — pure styled divs.
**Styling:** framer-motion motion.div className 'glass' with entrance animation and a violet left border (borderLeft 3px solid var(--violet, #a855f7)). CSS vars for surfaces/text/success/danger; lucide TrendingUp/Down icons. Auto-fit KPI grid minmax(140px,1fr).

**Details:** Fetch effect (lines 22-30): POST dataset_id, await json, throw b.detail on !r.ok; on success setHeadline + setMovers; on error setError(e.message). Two null-render guards: `if (error) return null` (line 32 — quietly hides when there's no usable time axis, with an explicit comment) and `if (!headline) return null` (line 33 — pre-data / loading shows nothing, no skeleton). Inline `Delta` subcomponent (lines 35-40) defined INSIDE render (re-created every render): null => em-dash; else colored success/danger with Trending icon and sign — formatting quirk: `{v > 0 ? '+' : ''}{v}%` so positive shows +, zero shows no sign, negative relies on the number's own minus; AND the COLOR test is `v >= 0` (green) so exactly 0 is rendered green/up-arrow, misleading for 'no change'. Headline KPI grid (lines 51-62) maps a literal array of [label, value] for Total/Avg/Record-volume using the nullable change pcts through Delta. Movers section (lines 64-78) only if movers.length>0: a '(≥15% shift, ranked)' caption then movers.slice(0,5) rendered as dimension/category + Delta; row key is the array index `i` (line 69). Each mover row truncates the label with ellipsis/nowrap. before/after fields on Mover are fetched but never displayed (only change_pct). Magic numbers: slice(0,5) top movers, 15% threshold only in copy, violet border 3px, motion y:12.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 22-30): fetch has no AbortController/cleanup; rapid datasetId changes or unmount mid-flight can cause setState-on-unmounted and a stale-response race (older response overwriting newer). Same pattern as TimeMachine.
- _medium_ (line 32): `if (error) return null` swallows ALL fetch failures — a real network/500 error is treated identically to the intended 'no usable time axis' case, with no logging and no user feedback. A backend outage makes the panel silently vanish.
- _low_ (line 37): Delta color/arrow uses v >= 0 => success/up, so a 0% change renders green with an up-arrow (and '0%'), which misrepresents 'no change' as a positive. Should treat 0 as neutral.
- _low_ (line 35): Delta component is declared inside the WhatChanged render body, so it's a new component identity each render; React unmounts/remounts its subtree on every parent re-render (minor, but avoidable — hoist it out of the component).
- _low_ (line 69): Mover list uses array index `i` as React key. Stable here because movers is replaced wholesale per fetch, but index keys are an anti-pattern if the list ever reorders/animates.
- _nit_ (line 14,68): Mover.before / Mover.after are part of the fetched type but never rendered — dead data in the UI (only change_pct shown). Either show before→after or drop them from the consumed shape.
- _nit_ (line 33): No loading skeleton: between mount and first response the panel renders null, so it pops in abruptly (the framer-motion entrance only plays once headline arrives).

---

## Verita Landing / Brand Surface (frontend/src — pages/Landing.tsx, sections/Hero.tsx, sections/LedgerSections.tsx, components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx, components/landing/ParticleField.tsx)

> This subsystem is Verita's public marketing/landing experience. The live, in-use surface is `pages/Landing.tsx` (838 lines, a self-contained "Forensic Ledger" scroll story driven by Lenis smooth-scroll + GSAP ScrollTrigger, with three inline product mockups, an impact word-reveal, a pinned horizontal stats track, a how-it-works grid, and an audit-chain CTA). It embeds the only live 3D module, `components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx` (a react-three-fiber animated money-laundering graph), via a lazily-mounted Canvas. CRITICAL FINDING: `sections/Hero.tsx`, `sections/LedgerSections.tsx`, and `components/landing/ParticleField.tsx` are ALL dead code — they have zero importers anywhere in `src/` (verified by grep). They are the legacy "luminous aurora / glass" brand system (framer-motion Hero, framer-motion ledger story, GLSL particle field) that the current `Landing.tsx` rebuild replaced inline; DESIGN.md confirms the aurora/glass system is legacy and the paper/ink/stamp identity is canonical. All data across every file is hardcoded/mock — there are no API calls; every metric, hash, and table row is a literal. Styling is overwhelmingly inline-style objects with module-local color constants in Landing/FraudNetwork, and CSS-variable + external-class theming (var(--ink), .stamp, .ledger, .chain-node) in the legacy ledger/Hero files.

### `frontend/src/pages/Landing.tsx`  _(838 lines)_

**Purpose:** The live landing page — the entire brand surface. A single-file 'Forensic Ledger' scroll narrative composed of a custom cursor, fixed nav, asymmetric hero with a live fake risk-scanner, three product showcase sections (Studio/Risk/NLP) each with an inline mockup, a pinned word-by-word impact reveal, a pinned horizontal-scroll stats track, a how-it-works grid, the embedded 3D FraudNetwork, and an audit-chain CTA + footer. Drives all scroll/animation with Lenis + GSAP ScrollTrigger.
**Exports:** `default Landing (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** None. Root Landing takes no props. Internal sub-components Cursor/Nav/HeroSection/ModuleShowcase/ImpactText/HorizontalStats are memoized no-prop components. StudioMock/RiskMock/NLPMock are no-prop function components. SectionStudio/SectionRisk/SectionNLP/HowItWorks/FraudNetSection/LedgerCTA are no-prop.
**State/refs:** HeroSection: score:number (0), phase:'idle'|'scanning'|'done'. Nav: solid:boolean (scroll>80). HorizontalStats: active:number (current panel index). FraudNetSection: visible:boolean (lazy-mount gate). LedgerCTA: hashSeed:number (77, ticks +1 every 2600ms). Refs: heroRef, secRef, trackRef, chainRef, ref (per section), Cursor dot/ring refs.
**Hooks:** `useEffect`, `useRef`, `useState`, `memo`, `useNavigate (react-router-dom)`
**Dependencies:** `react (memo,useEffect,useRef,useState)`, `@react-three/fiber (Canvas)`, `gsap`, `gsap/ScrollTrigger`, `lenis`, `react-router-dom (useNavigate)`, `../components/landing/FraudNetwork`
**Data flow:** 100% mock/hardcoded. No fetch/axios/service calls. MODULES array (line 38) defines nav/strip routing targets (/studio,/risk,/nlp,/overview). All stats ('284,507 TXN','94.7% RECALL','$2.9T', etc.), mockup table rows, SHAP waterfall values, and hashes are literals. fakeHash() (line 13) deterministically generates a 48-char hex string from a seed via Math.sin — purely cosmetic. Navigation flows OUT via navigate(path) and scrollIntoView/scrollTo. No props flow in; sub-components read module-scoped constants.
**Styling:** Inline style objects throughout, keyed off module-local color constants: DARK #0c0a07, CREAM #f3eee2, CREAM_DIM, AMBER #ffb700, RED #c2331f, GREEN #1c6e4a, GOLD #a8842c, BORDER rgba(243,238,226,0.09). HowItWorks/LedgerCTA switch to the paper/ink design-system via CSS classes (.ledger, .torn-edge, .stamp, .stamp--green, .chain-node, .chain-connector, .wax-seal, .guilloche, .marquee-track) and CSS vars (var(--ink), var(--paper), var(--foil-gold), var(--seal-green), var(--stamp-red), var(--font-serif/mono/body)). LEDGER_LINES const builds a repeating-linear-gradient ruled-paper bg. Heavy use of clamp() for fluid type and the 'Hard Shadow' offset box-shadow on buttons (e.g. 5px 5px 0 RED) animating to 3px on hover.

**Details:** CURSOR (47-75): two fixed divs (dot + lagging ring). mousemove writes dot transform synchronously (passive listener); a never-cancelled requestAnimationFrame(tick) loop lerps the ring at factor 0.1 — the RAF is NOT stored or cancelled on unmount (cleanup only removes the mousemove listener), so the loop runs forever. NAV (79-115): scroll listener toggles solid at scrollY>80; background/blur/border transition .35s. Inline onMouseEnter/Leave mutate style for hover (no CSS hover). HERO (119-251): GSAP context timeline staggers .hv-left/.hv-div/.hv-right/.hv-strip with expo.out, delay 0.1. A setTimeout(1200ms) starts a fake scan: phase->'scanning', then a manual RAF easing loop (cubic ease-out 1-(1-p)^3) over D=2400ms toward target T=0.94, writing score each frame via setScore(parseFloat(...toFixed(2))); on p>=1 -> phase 'done'. `killed` flag guards both the timeout and RAF on unmount; clearTimeout(t1) and ctx.revert() run in cleanup. danger = score>=0.8 flips color amber->red and progress bar width = (score/0.94)*100%. A scrollTrigger fades/translates heroRef (opacity 0, y -60) scrub 1.4 over end '+=55%'. Targeting reticle (203-209) built from 4 corner brackets via [[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]] map. Stats 2x2 and module strip hardcoded. MOCKS registry (341) maps names to StudioMock/RiskMock/NLPMock but is DEAD — never referenced after definition (sections call the components directly). StudioMock/RiskMock/NLPMock (256-339): static mock dashboards; RiskMock claims '284,807 REAL TRANSACTIONS' while the hero stat says '284,507' (inconsistent magic numbers; 284,807 is the real Kaggle/ULB count). SectionStudio/Risk/NLP (346-513): each uses a one-shot IntersectionObserver (threshold .15/.12/.1) that fires a gsap.from entrance then obs.disconnect(); cleanup also disconnects. These gsap.from tweens are NOT wrapped in gsap.context, so on unmount mid-tween they are not reverted (minor leak/animation-orphan risk vs the ctx-wrapped ones). Each section has a giant ghost watermark number (01/02/03) and a colored top/side accent bar; NLP uses purple #a855f7 hardcoded inline (not a constant). ImpactText (528-552): pins the section and reveals words EVERY/TRANSACTION/LEAVES/A/TRACE. via gsap.fromTo with blur(12px)->0, scrub 2, pin true, end '+=150%'; willChange set; ctx.revert cleanup. HorizontalStats (563-596): pins section and translates trackRef by -(offsetWidth-innerWidth) with scrub 0.8, invalidateOnRefresh true; onUpdate computes active = floor(progress*len) clamped; bottom progress bar width=((active+1)/len)*100%. 4 STAT_PANELS hardcoded. HowItWorks (600-642): IntersectionObserver(threshold .2) then setTimeout stagger (i*140ms) sets opacity/transform directly on .step-card DOM nodes — these timeouts are NOT tracked/cleared. Uses .ledger .torn-edge + .stamp. FraudNetSection (646-680): IntersectionObserver(threshold .1) flips visible=true once then disconnects; Canvas only mounts when visible (perf-conscious lazy mount). Canvas: camera fov 50 pos [0,0,12], antialias, alpha, dpr [1,2]; ambientLight 0.4 + <FraudNetwork/>. Height 78vh. LedgerCTA (684-809): setInterval 2600ms increments hashSeed -> recomputes fakeHash for the 'chain:' display; IntersectionObserver(threshold .3) seals chain-nodes and activates connectors on a 480ms*i+200 stagger via classList; cleanup clears interval and disconnects observer (good). Marquee, audit-chain widget, 2x2 reg/evidence/hash/compat grid, wax-seal verdict, footer with MODULES nav. Root Landing (813-837): mounts Lenis (lerp 0.09, smoothWheel), wires lenis.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update), drives lenis.raf via gsap.ticker with a `killed` guard, sets gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0); cleanup sets killed, lenis.destroy(), kills all ScrollTriggers. Composition order: Cursor, Nav, HeroSection, 25vh dark spacer, ModuleShowcase, ImpactText, HorizontalStats, HowItWorks, FraudNetSection, LedgerCTA. gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger) at module top (line 9).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 56-62): Cursor RAF loop (requestAnimationFrame(tick)) is never cancelled — cleanup only removes the mousemove listener, not the RAF. On unmount the loop keeps running; ref-guards are the only thing preventing errors. Memory/CPU leak across route changes.
- _medium_ (line 353-358, 410-415, 466-471): SectionStudio/Risk/NLP fire gsap.from() entrance tweens NOT wrapped in gsap.context; only the IntersectionObserver is disconnected on cleanup. Unmounting mid-tween orphans the tween (inline transforms may be left applied). Inconsistent with the ctx-wrapped tweens elsewhere.
- _low_ (line 607-610): HowItWorks uses setTimeout(i*140) to reveal step cards but never stores/clears the timeout ids; unmounting within the ~420ms stagger window runs callbacks against detached DOM nodes.
- _low_ (line 688-700): LedgerCTA creates the setInterval BEFORE the `if (!el) return` guard; on the null-chainRef path the cleanup that clears the interval is never returned, so the interval leaks. Edge case.
- _low_ (line 265, 306, 432): Inconsistent magic transaction count: hero/StudioMock use '284,507' while RiskMock (306) and SectionRisk (434) use '284,807'. 284,807 is the real Kaggle creditcard dataset size; the other is a typo — undermines the 'no fabricated metrics' brand claim.
- _low_ (line 191, 709, 726): 'Wolter Kluwer' misspelled — the real compliance vendor is 'Wolters Kluwer' (with an s). Appears in three user-visible places.
- _low_ (line 341): Dead code: MOCKS registry object defined but never referenced anywhere.
- _low_ (line 50-86): a11y/perf: global window 'scroll' (Nav) and 'mousemove' (Cursor) listeners plus the perpetual RAF run regardless of prefers-reduced-motion. Unlike Hero.tsx/LedgerSections.tsx, Landing.tsx never checks prefers-reduced-motion, so scanner animation, pinned scroll-jacking, marquees and custom cursor all run for reduced-motion users (WCAG 2.3.3 / the AAA bar in PRODUCT.md).
- _nit_ (line 16): fakeHash magic constants (9301, 49297, 233, 829.7382, %16) are opaque pseudo-random hex generation; cosmetic only.
- _nit_ (line 315): Purple accent #a855f7 (and rgba variants) hardcoded inline in multiple places (NLP section/mock) instead of hoisted to a constant alongside AMBER/RED/GREEN/GOLD.

**Dead code / TODOs:** MOCKS const (line 341) is defined but never used. 'Wolter Kluwer' misspelled (should be 'Wolters Kluwer') in hero badge (191), LedgerCTA copy (726), and marquee (709). No TODO/FIXME comments. All data is mock/hardcoded.

### `frontend/src/sections/Hero.tsx`  _(313 lines)_

**Purpose:** Legacy hero section for the superseded 'luminous aurora/glass' brand system. A centered headline hero with a glass nav pill, magnetic buttons, four floating glass stat chips, a typewriter subtitle, a scroll cue, and an infinite trust-strip marquee. DEAD CODE — not imported anywhere in src/.
**Exports:** `default Hero`
**Props/inputs:** Hero: none. Magnetic(internal): {children:ReactNode, primary?:boolean, onClick?:()=>void}. FloatingChip: {label:string, value:string, delay:number, style:CSSProperties}. Typewriter: {text:string}.
**State/refs:** Typewriter: n:number (chars revealed). Magnetic: motion values x,y + springs sx,sy (stiffness 300, damping 18) + button ref.
**Hooks:** `useEffect`, `useRef`, `useState`, `useMotionValue`, `useSpring (framer-motion)`, `useNavigate`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion,useMotionValue,useSpring)`, `react-router-dom (useNavigate)`, `lucide-react (ArrowUpRight, ShieldCheck, Sparkles)`
**Data flow:** 100% mock/hardcoded. Nav links and FloatingChip stat values ('284K','0.913','0.65','0.17%') are literals. navigate() routes out to /overview,/risk,/studio,/nlp. No props from parent (default export takes none) and no API.
**Styling:** framer-motion + CSS-variable theme of the LEGACY aurora system: var(--signature), var(--surface), var(--border), var(--text), var(--text-muted), var(--violet), var(--cyan), var(--font-display/body/mono), plus .glass and .gradient-text utility classes (backdrop-blur glassmorphism). Pill buttons borderRadius 999. The design language DESIGN.md marks as legacy.

**Details:** Magnetic (7-51): motion.button whose x/y motion values are set on mousemove to (cursorOffset*0.35 horizontal, *0.4 vertical) and spring-eased; reset to 0 on mouseLeave; whileHover scale 1.04, whileTap 0.97. Uses ref.current! non-null assertion in onMouseMove (type hole). FloatingChip (54-91): glass chip, initial opacity0/scale0.8 -> animate with an INFINITE y:[0,-14,0] float whose duration is 6+delay s (each chip floats at a slightly different period); opacity/scale have one-shot delays. Typewriter (94-115): respects prefers-reduced-motion (immediately sets n=text.length). Otherwise after a 900ms setTimeout it starts setInterval(24ms) incrementing n by 2 chars/tick until full, then clearInterval. BUG: cleanup only clears the outer `start` timeout — the inner setInterval id is local and not returned, so unmounting after 900ms but before typing finishes leaks the interval and calls setN on an unmounted component. Injects a <style> with @keyframes blink for the caret. fadeUp (117-125): variants object, custom index drives staggered delay 0.15*i, ease cubic [0.22,1,0.36,1], blur(8px)->0. Hero (127): fixed glass nav at top:20 with logo + 4 links + a primary Magnetic 'Request Access'. Four FloatingChips at corners. Headline block uses motion variants stagger (custom 0..3). Subtitle wraps Typewriter, minHeight 3.2em to prevent layout shift while typing. Scroll cue: motion.span bouncing arrow with infinite y:[0,7,0] 1.6s, entrance delay 2.2s. Trust marquee (286-310): duplicated array ([...Array(2)].flatMap) of compliance terms animated x:['0%','-50%'] linear 22s infinite, masked with linear-gradient maskImage for edge fade.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 98-107): Typewriter setInterval leak: the inner interval id is only cleared from inside its own callback when typing completes; the useEffect cleanup return only clears the outer `start` timeout. Unmounting mid-typing keeps the interval firing setN on an unmounted component (React state-update-on-unmounted warning + leak). Should capture and clear the interval in cleanup.
- _low_ (line 39): Non-null assertion ref.current! in onMouseMove — type hole; fine at runtime but unsafe pattern.
- _low_ (line 111): var(--cyan, #22d3ee) fallback hardcoded inline in two spots (caret + scroll arrow) rather than a guaranteed token.
- _nit_ (line 1): Dead legacy module retained per DESIGN.md 'maintain, don't extend' — candidate for deletion if the aurora system is fully retired.

**Dead code / TODOs:** ENTIRE FILE is dead code — grep confirms no import of Hero anywhere in src/. Superseded by the inline HeroSection in Landing.tsx.

### `frontend/src/sections/LedgerSections.tsx`  _(373 lines)_

**Purpose:** Legacy long-form 'Forensic Ledger' scroll story built with framer-motion: a scroll-driven gold/green chain thread, slamming rubber stamps, verifying ledger entries, redaction reveals, a live SHA-256 hash ticker, a self-playing investigator paper-terminal, hover-loupe exhibit photos, a fingerprint watermark, pipeline grid, numbers, and a wax-seal CTA. DEAD CODE — not imported anywhere in src/. The framer-motion ancestor of the GSAP rebuild now living inside Landing.tsx.
**Exports:** `default LedgerSections`
**Props/inputs:** LedgerSections: none. Stamp(internal):{children,green?,style?}. Entry:{no:string,children}. Redacted:{children:string}. ExhibitPhoto:{ex:EXHIBITS[number], i:number}.
**State/refs:** LedgerSections: uv:boolean (UV lamp toggle). HashTicker: hex:string. Rehearsal: n:number (chars typed) + inView. ExhibitPhoto: lens:{x,y}|null (loupe position). Refs: wrapRef + per-component inView refs. useScroll/useSpring for the chain thread.
**Hooks:** `useEffect`, `useRef`, `useState`, `useInView`, `useScroll`, `useSpring (framer-motion)`, `useNavigate`
**Dependencies:** `react`, `framer-motion (motion,useInView,useScroll,useSpring)`, `react-router-dom (useNavigate)`, `lucide-react (ArrowUpRight, Github, Lamp)`
**Data flow:** 100% mock/hardcoded. EXHIBITS array (5 items) references real screenshot paths under /shots/*.png (02-studio-dashboard, 09-investigator, 07-risk-engine, 06-sql, 08-nlp). PIPELINE (6 steps), NUMBERS (6 stats incl. '82 tests','0 fabricated metrics'), REHEARSAL (hardcoded SQL terminal transcript), MICROTEXT (repeated 14x). CASE NO. 'WK-2026-0612', EXAMINER 'R. MODI', GitHub link github.com/rajmodi262/verita are literals. No API.
**Styling:** framer-motion + the CANONICAL paper/ink design system via CSS classes: .ledger,.torn-edge,.uv,.guilloche,.stamp,.stamp--green,.chain-node(.sealed),.ledger-label,.redact(.lifted),.hash-ticker,.paper-terminal,.exhibit-frame,.tape,.loupe,.microtext,.coffee-ring,.barcode,.wax-seal,.uv-ink,.fingerprint and CSS vars var(--ink/ink-soft/paper/foil-gold/seal-green/ledger-line/font-serif/body). data-cursor attributes hook the custom cursor.

**Details:** Stamp (61-76): useInView(once,margin -60px) drives a spring slam — initial scale 2.6 + rotate ±(14/16), settles to scale1 rotate ±(3/4); green vs red rotate sign differs. Entry (79-93): useInView(once,-80px); opacity/y reveal; renders a .chain-node that gets .sealed when inView and a 'LEDGER ENTRY {no} · VERIFIED ✓ / PENDING' label that flips on inView. Redacted (95-99): adds .lifted when scrolled into view (-120px) to lift the redaction bar. HashTicker (102-115): respects prefers-reduced-motion (sets a fixed hex and returns). Otherwise setInterval(90ms) rolls a new random 64-char hex; cleanup clears interval (good). Rehearsal (132-158): types only when inView; respects reduced-motion (sets n=9999); setInterval(28ms) +2 chars, and when n>=total+30 RESETS n to 0 — LOOPS FOREVER re-typing the transcript; cleanup clears interval. ExhibitPhoto (161-192): magnifying-loupe effect — onMouseMove computes lens % from getBoundingClientRect; renders a loupe div with backgroundImage of the same photo at backgroundSize 260% offset by lens %; onMouseLeave clears. whileHover rotate->0 scale 1.015; resting rotate alternates ±1.2deg by index parity; tape corners + 'HOVER TO INSPECT'. Uses imgWrap.current! non-null assertion (type hole). Fingerprint (195-206): SVG of 13 concentric rotated dashed ellipses (rx 20+i*13, ry 26+i*12, rotate i*7) — decorative. LedgerSections (208): UV-lamp button toggles the `uv` class on root, revealing .uv-ink hidden security text (banknote metaphor); aria-pressed set (good a11y). useScroll(target wrapRef, offset ['start 0.8','end end']) + useSpring(stiffness 90,damping 26) drives the chain thread's scaleY (motion.div absolute left 50%, gradient gold->green). Sections: §1 Problem (Entry 0001 with two <Redacted> words), microtext divider, §2 Manifesto (0002, fingerprint + glass-box quote + two stamps + UV 'AUTHENTIC' ink + embedded Rehearsal), §3 Exhibits (maps EXHIBITS to alternating row/row-reverse Entry blocks, no built as `000${i+3}`), §4 Pipeline (0008, coffee-ring + 6-cell auto-fit grid), §5 Numbers (0009, auto-fit grid of 6), §6 CTA (0010, wax-seal whileInView spring, Studio button + GitHub link target=_blank rel=noreferrer, closing UV-ink easter egg). HashTicker + guilloche bookend top and bottom.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 289): Exhibit Entry numbering `000${i+3}` is fragile — only correct because EXHIBITS has exactly 5 items (yields 0003-0007). A 7th+ exhibit produces malformed numbers like '00010'. Should zero-pad with padStart, not string interpolation.
- _low_ (line 174): Non-null assertion imgWrap.current! in ExhibitPhoto onMouseMove — type hole; relies on the handler only firing while mounted.
- _low_ (line 109, 140): Math.random()-based hash/typing is cosmetic but HashTicker rolls a new 64-char string every 90ms indefinitely while mounted (Rehearsal every 28ms) — continuous re-render churn. Both correctly gated on prefers-reduced-motion and cleaned up; noted as constant background work.
- _low_ (line 178): img has loading='lazy' and alt={ex.title} (good), but the loupe magnifier is mouse-only with no keyboard/touch affordance — 'HOVER TO INSPECT' is inaccessible to keyboard/touch users (AAA bar).
- _nit_ (line 1): Dead legacy module duplicating the canonical ledger story now inlined in Landing.tsx. Candidate for deletion.

**Dead code / TODOs:** ENTIRE FILE is dead code — grep confirms no import of LedgerSections anywhere in src/. Re-implemented (in GSAP, paper/ink) inside Landing.tsx's HowItWorks + LedgerCTA. References /shots/*.png assets that may be orphaned if this is their only consumer.

### `frontend/src/components/landing/FraudNetwork.tsx`  _(147 lines)_

**Purpose:** The only LIVE 3D module — a react-three-fiber scene rendering a hand-authored money-laundering graph: 20 nodes (fraud nodes red+larger, normal amber), faint normal edges, and 6 animated dashed 'fraud path' edges that march along the laundering route (0->3->5->7->8->10->11). The whole group slowly auto-rotates. Mounted lazily by Landing.tsx's FraudNetSection inside a Canvas.
**Exports:** `default FraudNetwork`
**Props/inputs:** FraudNetwork: none. Node(internal):{pos:[number,number,number], fraud:boolean, index:number}. FraudEdge:{a:[3], b:[3]}.
**State/refs:** No React state. Refs: groupRef (THREE.Group), per-Node meshRef+glowRef, per-FraudEdge ref. All animation via useFrame (no re-renders).
**Hooks:** `useRef`, `useFrame (@react-three/fiber)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useRef)`, `@react-three/fiber (useFrame)`, `@react-three/drei (Line)`, `three (THREE)`
**Data flow:** 100% hardcoded topology. NODES (20 hand-placed [x,y,z] tuples), FRAUD_EDGES (6), FRAUD_NODES Set([0,3,5,7,8,10,11]), NORMAL_EDGES (24). No props from Landing (rendered as <FraudNetwork/> with no inputs). No API. V3 helper wraps tuples into THREE.Vector3.
**Styling:** Pure WebGL/three materials — no CSS. Colors: fraud #ff4d5e, normal #ffb700 (note: #ff4d5e differs from Landing's RED #c2331f — the 3D scene uses a brighter red). meshBasicMaterial (unlit), glow halos with depthWrite false. Normal edges drei <Line> lineWidth 0.5 opacity 0.20; fraud edges lineWidth 2.2 dashed dashSize 0.18 gapSize 0.09.

**Details:** Node (55-87): useFrame pulses core mesh scale = 0.85 + 0.15*sin(t*1.8 + index*0.7) and glow scale = 1.5 + 0.5*sin(t*1.4 + index); glow opacity oscillates higher for fraud (0.18+0.12*sin) vs normal (0.06+0.04*sin). Core sphere radius 0.13 (fraud)/0.07 (normal), geometry segments 14x14 core, 10x10 glow at radius*2.8. Uses useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null!) and guards meshRef.current/glowRef.current before mutating (safe). FraudEdge (91-109): useFrame sets material.dashOffset = -clock.elapsedTime*0.5 each frame to march the dashes; ref typed as `any` (type hole) and optional-chained. Scene FraudNetwork (113-146): groupRef.rotation.y = t*0.10 (continuous spin), rotation.x = sin(t*0.06)*0.12 (gentle wobble) — reads groupRef.current.rotation WITHOUT a null guard, relying on R3F guaranteeing the ref before useFrame runs. Renders NORMAL_EDGES, then FRAUD_EDGES (as FraudEdge), then all NODES. Money-laundering narrative encoded in the comment at line 39. Parent Canvas supplies camera fov50/z12, ambientLight 0.4, dpr [1,2], alpha — but meshBasicMaterial is unlit so ambientLight has no visual effect.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 117-119): groupRef.current.rotation accessed in useFrame without a null check (the ref is typed null! and never guarded, unlike the Node refs). Safe under R3F mount ordering but a latent null-deref if the scene is ever used outside that guarantee.
- _low_ (line 92): FraudEdge ref typed as useRef<any>(null) — type hole; loses type safety on .material.dashOffset. Should type as the drei Line/Line2 ref.
- _low_ (line 70): Color inconsistency: fraud red here is #ff4d5e but Landing.tsx's brand RED is #c2331f, and the FraudNetSection legend swatch uses Landing's RED — so the legend's 'Fraud path' swatch (#c2331f) does not match the actual rendered edges (#ff4d5e).
- _nit_ (line 59): Per-node useFrame closures (20 nodes) each run trig every frame; fine at 20 nodes but one-useFrame-per-mesh doesn't scale — larger graphs would need an instanced approach.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. The ambientLight added by the parent Canvas is inert because all materials are meshBasicMaterial (unlit) — effectively dead lighting. No dead code within the file itself.

### `frontend/src/components/landing/ParticleField.tsx`  _(119 lines)_

**Purpose:** Legacy GLSL particle background for the aurora/glass system — a points cloud of 7200 amber/warm-white particles with organic sine-wave drift, mouse repulsion, per-particle brightness pulse, and additive blending. DEAD CODE — not imported anywhere in src/ (the live FraudNetSection uses FraudNetwork, not this).
**Exports:** `default ParticleField`
**Props/inputs:** None.
**State/refs:** No React state. uniforms object memoized via useMemo. mouse from useThree. Geometry data computed once at module load (POSITIONS, RANDOMS) outside the component.
**Hooks:** `useMemo`, `useFrame`, `useThree (@react-three/fiber)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useMemo)`, `@react-three/fiber (useFrame,useThree)`, `three (THREE)`
**Data flow:** Self-contained / deterministic. buildGeometryData() generates 7200 particle positions (spread 40x24x10) and per-particle randoms using a sin-hash (Math.sin(i*k)*43758.5453 % 1) at module-eval time — deterministic, no seed input. No props, no API. Mouse comes from R3F useThree().mouse, lerped into uMouse uniform.
**Styling:** Pure GLSL/WebGL. Vertex shader: uTime-driven two-field sine drift (p.x,p.y,p.z), mouse repulsion via exponential falloff exp(-d*0.30)*3.2 with mouse mapped to world via *vec2(19.5,12.0), gl_PointSize = (0.5 + r*2.6)*(215.0/-mv.z) for perspective sizing, top ~13% of particles (step(0.87,r)) flagged white. Fragment shader: circular point with soft glow (core + halo), discards outside d>0.5, mixes uColorA #ffb700 / uColorB #fff8e8 by vWhite, alpha*0.90. shaderMaterial transparent, depthWrite false, AdditiveBlending.

**Details:** Module-level constant N=7200. buildGeometryData (68-82) runs ONCE at import (POSITIONS/RANDOMS frozen as module singletons), so all instances would share identical geometry — deliberate perf choice but the field can't be re-seeded. uniforms (91-96) memoized: uTime, uMouse(Vector2), uColorA amber, uColorB warm white. useFrame (98-101): updates uTime = clock.elapsedTime and lerps uMouse toward mouse at factor 0.055 (slow smoothing). Renders <points> with bufferGeometry (attributes-position [POSITIONS,3], attributes-aRandom [RANDOMS,1]) and the shaderMaterial. Magic numbers throughout the shader (0.36 time scale, 0.41/0.29/0.22 spatial freqs, 0.58/0.44/0.30 amplitudes, 0.30 repulsion falloff, 3.2 repulsion strength, 19.5/12.0 mouse-world scale, 215.0 point-size base, 0.87 white threshold). No prefers-reduced-motion handling (moot while unused).

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 98-101): useFrame runs an unconditional 7200-particle GPU update every frame with no prefers-reduced-motion / visibility gating. Moot while unused, but if revived it animates for reduced-motion users and when offscreen.
- _low_ (line 68-84): POSITIONS/RANDOMS are module-singletons computed at import time, so all ParticleField instances share one immutable geometry — cannot vary or re-seed per instance. Intentional but a hidden coupling.
- _nit_ (line 1): Dead legacy module; aurora background superseded by the dark-paper Forensic Ledger. Candidate for deletion. Shader magic numbers undocumented.

**Dead code / TODOs:** ENTIRE FILE is dead code — grep confirms no import of ParticleField anywhere in src/. Belongs to the retired aurora/glass background. No TODOs.

## Reusable primitives (CustomCursor, EChart, ErrorBoundary, SignalField)

> Four standalone, self-contained presentational/primitive components used across the Verita frontend. CustomCursor renders a global decorative cursor (instant dot + spring-lagged ring) driven by framer-motion motion values. EChart is a thin imperative wrapper around the `echarts` library that mounts a chart into a div, syncs `option`, and handles resize/dispose. ErrorBoundary is the app's only class component — a render-error catcher with a "Try again" reset. SignalField is a react-three-fiber decorative background: a ~2600-point additively-blended particle cloud that rotates, breathes, and parallaxes toward the cursor. None of these consume API data; all data is either generated procedurally (SignalField), passed in via props (EChart, ErrorBoundary), or sourced from browser pointer events (CustomCursor).

### `frontend/src/components/CustomCursor.tsx`  _(88 lines)_

**Purpose:** Global decorative custom cursor: an instant-tracking cyan dot plus a blue ring that follows with spring lag (the 'trail'). The ring expands and brightens when hovering interactive elements. Self-disables on touch/coarse pointers and when prefers-reduced-motion is set.
**Exports:** `default CustomCursor (React.FC, no props)`
**Props/inputs:** None — takes no props.
**State/refs:** useState `hovering` (boolean, drives ring scale/opacity, line 16). useState `enabled` (boolean, gates rendering, line 17). Motion values: `x`/`y` (useMotionValue, init -100 to start off-screen, lines 11-12) for the instant dot; `ringX`/`ringY` (useSpring derived from x/y, stiffness 350 / damping 28 / mass 0.6, lines 13-14) for the lagging ring.
**Hooks:** `useState`, `useEffect`, `useMotionValue (framer-motion)`, `useSpring (framer-motion)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useState)`, `framer-motion (motion, useMotionValue, useSpring)`
**Data flow:** No props/API. Pure browser input: a `mousemove` listener (line 31) reads e.clientX/Y into the motion values and computes hover via e.target.closest('a, button, [data-cursor]'). Capability detection reads window.matchMedia for '(pointer: fine)' and '(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'.
**Styling:** All inline styles on motion.div elements. Hardcoded colors: ring border #4d7cff with boxShadow rgba(77,124,255,0.5); dot background #22d3ee with boxShadow rgba(34,211,238,0.8). zIndex 9999, position fixed, pointerEvents none on both. Ring uses mixBlendMode 'screen'. Ring 40x40 with -20 margin offset to center; dot 6x6 with -3 margin offset.

**Details:** Mount effect (lines 19-33): synchronously reads matchMedia for fine pointer + reduced motion; if NOT fine OR reduced, returns early WITHOUT setting enabled (stays false, component renders null at line 35). Otherwise setEnabled(true) and attaches a window 'mousemove' handler. The handler (lines 25-30) sets x/y motion values and toggles `hovering` based on whether the event target's closest ancestor matches the interactive selector. Cleanup removes the listener. The two animated layers: (1) the trailing ring — translateX/Y bound to the SPRING values ringX/ringY so it physically lags behind; an `animate` prop springs scale 1->1.8 and opacity 0.6->0.9 on hover (transition spring stiffness 300/damping 20, lines 53-54). (2) the instant dot — translateX/Y bound to the RAW motion values x/y so it tracks the pointer with zero lag. The initial off-screen position (-100,-100) prevents a flash at origin (0,0) before first mouse move. Magic numbers: spring params (350/28/0.6 and 300/20), sizes (40/6), offsets (-20/-3), zIndex 9999, glow radii (24px/12px).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 19-33): matchMedia is evaluated only once on mount; there is no MediaQueryList 'change' listener. If the user toggles prefers-reduced-motion at the OS level, or switches input modality (e.g. attaches a mouse to a tablet / Windows tablet-mode flip), `enabled` never updates without a remount. a11y/responsiveness gap.
- _low_ (line 29): Per-mousemove DOM traversal: every mousemove fires el.closest(...) on the event target. On deep DOM trees this runs at full pointer-event frequency with no throttle/rAF batching — minor perf cost, and setHovering will re-render the component on every hover-state transition.
- _low_ (line 31): Listener attached to `window` without `{ passive: true }`. Not a scroll/touch listener so impact is negligible, but passive is the safer default for high-frequency pointer listeners.
- _nit_ (line 28): `e.target as HTMLElement` is an unchecked cast. If the event target is a non-Element node (e.g. a text node or the document in edge cases), `.closest` would throw; in practice mousemove targets are Elements so this is low-risk, but it's an unguarded type assertion.
- _nit_ (line 8,22): Comment says it is 'Disabled on coarse pointers (touch)' but the logic keys off '(pointer: fine)' rather than '(pointer: coarse)'. Functionally equivalent for typical devices, but a hybrid device reporting fine would still enable the cursor — minor doc/intent mismatch.
