- _nit_ (line 169): makeBlueprintTex texture is created in useMemo with [] deps so it is stable per mount, but because FloorScene unmounts/remounts floors on phase change the canvas texture is regenerated every entry (no shared cache across floors).

**Dead code / TODOs:** Line 1: useRef imported, never used. Line 2: useFrame imported, never used. Lines 179-184: floor border array values are unused placeholders (only the loop index drives position); duplicated [0,RW,0.14] entries are dead data.

### `frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RiskLabFloor.tsx`  _(303 lines)_

**Purpose:** Themed 'forensic risk lab' room: a UV-reveal interactive floor (mouse exposes hidden evidence via a custom shader), near-black walls, ceiling UV tube strips, an animated UV lamp rig, examination tables with evidence-grid surfaces and pulsing under-table glow, two SHAP feature-importance readout screens, and UV-reactive wall text strips. The only floor with live interactivity.
**Exports:** `RISK_FY (const = 14.5)`, `default RiskLabFloor (component)`
**Props/inputs:** RiskLabFloor: none. Subcomponents: UVLampRig({y,z}), ExaminationTable({position}), SHAPScreen({position}), UVStrip({position,label}), UVFloor() (no props).
**State/refs:** No React state. Refs: UVLampRig armRef (THREE.Group, line 55) animated; ExaminationTable glowRef (THREE.Mesh, line 104) animated. useRiskLabUVReveal internally holds a mouseRef + shader material.
**Hooks:** `useMemo (evidence tex, SHAP tex, UV strip tex)`, `useRef (armRef, glowRef)`, `useFrame (UVLampRig line 56; ExaminationTable line 106)`, `useRiskLabUVReveal (custom, line 213) — only floor consuming live context`
**Dependencies:** `react (useMemo, useRef)`, `@react-three/fiber (useFrame)`, `three`, `../../hooks/useUVReveal (useRiskLabUVReveal)`
**Data flow:** Mostly mock textures, but UVFloor (lines 212-220) is genuinely interactive: useRiskLabUVReveal (useUVReveal.ts) builds a ShaderMaterial via createUVRevealMaterial and each frame copies BuildingContext.mouseNDC into the uMouse uniform and clock.elapsedTime into uTime — so cursor position reveals hidden UV ink on the floor. SHAP bar values [0.85,0.62,0.44,0.38,0.29,0.21,0.15,0.09] and labels velocity/frequency/amount/location/device/time/merchant/network (lines 163-164) are HARDCODED — not real model output. UV strip labels are hardcoded fictional values ('CHAIN HEAD: 0xA8F2C3D1', 'RISK SCORE: 0.847 / FLAGGED', line 282-283).
**Styling:** Cyan/UV palette #7df9ff with emissive materials, near-black surfaces #0d0b18/#060410/#080612, violet ambient #1a0a30. Heavy use of emissive + emissiveMap on plane screens.

**Details:** makeUVStripTex(label): 256x32 dark canvas, cyan JetBrains Mono text. makeEvidenceTex: 512x256 grid + 5 evidence markers (E-001..E-005, lines 35-44, coords cast via Number()/String() because the tuple mixes number+string) + concentric 'fingerprint smudge' circles r=4..20 at (260,130). UVLampRig: armRef rotates z by sin(t*0.4)*0.06 (gentle sway, line 58), emissive UV tube + pointLight 2.0 distance 4.5. ExaminationTable: glowRef under-table panel pulses emissiveIntensity 0.18 + sin(t*1.2)*0.06 (line 109); legs generated by nested map over [-1.1,1.1]x[-0.6,0.6]; under-table pointLight 0.6. SHAPScreen: useMemo builds 256x160 bar chart, bar width v*150, row pitch 17px starting y=18, color alpha 0.25+v*0.5; rendered as emissive plane (emissiveMap=tex, intensity 0.3). UVFloor uses <primitive object={mat}> with the shader material from the hook (plane segments 1x1). RiskLabFloor body: UVFloor + 7 emissive grid lines, three near-black walls + ceiling, three UV ceiling tube strips at x=[-2.5,0,2.5] each with pointLight (0.5 distance 14), one UVLampRig over table at z RZ+3, two ExaminationTables (RZ+3, RZ-3), two SHAPScreens on right wall (RZ+2, RZ-5), three UVStrips on left wall, a reagent cabinet with 3 shelves, and three ambient/point lights (violet ambient 0.4, #3a1a70 0.8, cyan 0.5). Animation cleanup: useFrame callbacks are auto-managed by r3f and torn down on unmount, fine.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 152-177): SHAPScreen draws a 'SHAP FEATURE IMPORTANCE' chart with fully hardcoded bars/labels. The project actually ships a real SHAP-capable risk engine (per repo commit history), so this immersive readout is mock data masquerading as model output — a correctness/authenticity gap if it is ever read as real.
- _low_ (line 39-44): makeEvidenceTex markers array mixes number and string element types in a single tuple, forcing Number(x)/Number(y)/String(lbl) coercions. A typed tuple ([number,number,string]) would avoid the runtime casts and the implicit any[] inference.
- _low_ (line 152): SHAPScreen's texture useMemo has [] deps and ignores `position`; fine, but two SHAPScreens each build their own identical 256x160 canvas instead of sharing one texture — minor redundant canvas allocation.
- _low_ (line 180): SHAPScreen <mesh position={position}> passes the [number,number,number] tuple directly as the position prop (works in r3f) while sibling components destructure into a <group> — inconsistent style, harmless.
- _nit_ (line 229-232): Floor UV grid lines use 7 thin emissive planes laid ON the shader floor at y+0.005; combined with the shader-revealed floor this is double-drawing grid lines (the shader baseMap already draws a grid per useUVReveal.ts), a slight visual/perf redundancy.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. Hardcoded fictional forensic values throughout (SHAP scores, risk 0.847, chain hash 0xA8F2C3D1) are intentional set-dressing, not wired to the backend risk engine.

PendantLamp: bulbRef emissiveIntensity oscillates 2.8 + sin(t*2.3)*0.15 (line 184) — subtle flicker; bulb mesh + cone shade (BackSide) + castShadow pointLight (1.5, distance 6). DetectiveDesk: body+top+leather pad, 4 case files stacked with i*0.028 x-offset and i*0.024 y-offset, a tilted photo plane, a magnifying glass (torus ring + translucent circular lens opacity 0.35), and a desk-lamp group with cone shade + warm pointLight (1.2). FilingCabinet: body + 3 drawer fronts via i*0.46 vertical pitch, gold handles, label slots. Lighting: ambient #4a2e10 0.45 + central warm pointLight 0.9 (distance 18, decay 1.5). Magic numbers: card layout coords, pair indices, plank count 13 divisor for 12 seams.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 261-273): Cork-board frame loop is broken/incomplete: the offset ternaries `(fi===0 ? 0 : 0)` are always 0 (dead arithmetic) and the mapped array values are never used, so both 'frame' pieces are centered identically and only two of the four expected frame edges render. Either dead code or an unfinished frame.
- _low_ (line 261): The mapped array is typed `as number[][]` and its element contents are entirely ignored — dead data plus a loose cast that hides the no-op.
- _low_ (line 201): PendantLamp pointLight has castShadow plus a second castShadow pointLight per lamp (two PendantLamps) on top of the central light — multiple shadow-casting point lights are expensive on the GPU; combined with other floors' shadow lights this can be a perf trap on weaker devices.
- _nit_ (line 12): ctx.getContext('2d')! uses non-null assertion (every canvas tex factory does this across all five floors); if a browser returns null (rare, e.g. context-lost) this throws. No guard/fallback anywhere in the subsystem.

**Dead code / TODOs:** Lines 261-273: cork board 'frame' loop maps over `[[-1.9,0.08,3.0],[3.8,0.08,-0.04]]` but the per-element ternaries `(fi===0 ? 0 : 0)` always evaluate to 0 — the position offsets are no-ops and the tuple values are never read. The frame geometry only varies via `fi===0 ? [0.08,3.0,0.1] : [3.8,0.08,0.1]`. Both frame pieces therefore render at the same center, so only a vertical-left bar and a horizontal bar appear (no right/top frame) — likely an incomplete frame implementation.

VaultFloor body: stone floor + nested 5x3 tile-joint grid, three walls + ceiling, 5 arch ribs, four ArchiveShelves along back, one VaultDoor on left wall, a central exam table with open box + tilted paper sheet, two CeilingChains (z RZ, RZ+6), four TorchSconces (corners, alternating flipX), a SEALED banner, and three lights (ambient 0.3, warm 0.8, gold table 0.6).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 232): `<cylinderGeometry args={[...]} rotation-z={Math.PI/2} />` — rotation-z is applied to the geometry element, which has no transform; it is silently ignored by r3f. The intent was to lay the connecting chain bar horizontally (it should be on the <mesh>). Result: the bar's orientation is wrong/ineffective. Concrete latent bug.
- _low_ (line 39-43): Each ArchiveShelf builds 20 CanvasTextures in useMemo, and there are four shelves => 80 offscreen 128x64 canvases generated on every mount of the Vault floor. With FloorScene unmount/remount on phase change these are recreated each visit (no caching/dispose), a memory/GC churn trap.
- _low_ (line 202-206): linksRef.current is a plain array populated via ref callbacks but never reset/cleared on unmount; combined with remounts this is mostly harmless (GC'd with component) but the stale-index pattern is fragile if LINKS ever changes between renders.
- _low_ (line 159-165, 195): Four TorchSconces each run a per-frame useFrame mutating a pointLight position+intensity (4 dynamic lights) plus other floor point lights — several simultaneous dynamic point lights with decay is a known r3f/three perf cost, notable on a deliberately dark scene.
- _nit_ (line 23): Label text embeds a literal '✓' (U+2713) drawn via canvas fillText — depends on the default canvas font having the glyph; on a fallback font it may render as tofu. Minor cross-platform risk.

**Dead code / TODOs:** Line 232: CeilingChain's connecting bar uses a JSX prop `rotation-z={Math.PI/2}` placed on the <cylinderGeometry> element rather than on the parent <mesh> — geometry elements do not have a rotation-z transform, so this prop has no effect (the bar is not actually rotated to horizontal). Likely a latent bug: the chain bar renders vertical/unintended instead of lying along the link row.

### `frontend/src/immersive/canvas/floors/RooftopFloor.tsx`  _(398 lines)_

**Purpose:** Themed night-rooftop finale: a star-field sky dome + extra GPU star points, distant glowing city buildings below, dark tarmac deck, an art-deco battlement parapet with finials, an illuminated pulsing 'VERITA' sign, a water tower, an observation telescope, a gold chain-seal floor medallion, a satellite dish, and HVAC units. The brand 'summit' room.
**Exports:** `ROOFTOP_FY (const = 50.5, // lobby -9.5, step 12, index 5)`, `default RooftopFloor (component)`
**Props/inputs:** RooftopFloor: none. Subcomponents: SkyDome(), StarPoints(), VeritaSign({position}), WaterTower({position}), Parapet(), Telescope({position}), CityLightsBelow().
**State/refs:** No React state. Ref: VeritaSign glowRef (THREE.PointLight, line 104) animated. SkyDome/StarPoints use useMemo for geometry/texture.
**Hooks:** `useMemo (makeSkyTex line 45; StarPoints BufferGeometry line 56; makeSignTex line 103)`, `useRef (glowRef)`, `useFrame (VeritaSign line 105 — sign light pulse)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useMemo, useRef)`, `@react-three/fiber (useFrame)`, `three (CanvasTexture, BufferGeometry, BufferAttribute, Color, RepeatWrapping, BackSide, FrontSide)`
**Data flow:** Pure mock/procedural. makeSkyTex draws a gradient night sky with 700 random stars + aurora band + city horizon glow. StarPoints builds 500 randomized 3D points. CityLightsBelow uses a hardcoded 9-building array and Math.random() per window. VERITA sign text hardcoded. No API/props.
**Styling:** Cold night palette: sky gradient #010308->#12243a, tarmac #111008, gold #a8842c brand, warm sign glow #ffd080, distant city orange #ff6818, cold blue ambient #0a0c1a.

**Details:** makeSkyTex: 1024x512, vertical gradient + 700 stars (y<380 only) + violet aurora band (y330-430) + warm city-horizon glow gradient (y420-512); wrapS=RepeatWrapping. SkyDome: sphere r=195, BackSide, meshBasicMaterial, fog={false}. StarPoints: 500 points placed on a hemisphere (phi up to PI*0.48) at r=100..180 offset around (0, ROOFTOP_FY+8, RZ); pointsMaterial size 0.28 sizeAttenuation, fog={false}. VeritaSign: makeSignTex draws a gold radial halo then a blurred (shadowBlur 28) text pass then a sharp pass for 'VERITA' in Georgia serif; glowRef pointLight intensity pulses 1.8 + sin(t*1.1)*0.25 (line 107); backing panel + emissive sign face + gold trim bars. WaterTower: 4 legs, cross brace (PI/4), tank body cylinder, 3 stave bands (torus), conical roof. Parapet: builds merlons/battlements along three sides (back z + two x sides) via a `sides` config array; alternating merlon/crenel heights (PARAPET_H 1.8 vs *0.6), merlons get gold cap blocks; plus 4 corner finials (cone + cylinder base). Telescope: 3-leg tripod arranged at angles [0, 2PI/3, 4PI/3] with tilt, mount sphere, tilted tube, eyepiece. CityLightsBelow: 9 hardcoded buildings [x,h,z,w,d]; for each, a dark box + a grid of glowing window planes where `Math.random() > 0.4` (≈60% lit) and emissiveIntensity 1.2 + Math.random()*0.8 — windows are randomized AT RENDER TIME, not memoized; plus a strong distant city pointLight (2.5, distance 100). RooftopFloor body: SkyDome + StarPoints + CityLightsBelow + tarmac deck + 5 deck seams + Parapet + VeritaSign on back parapet + WaterTower + Telescope + gold medallion (circle + ring) + satellite dish + 3 HVAC units + 4 lights (cold ambient 0.55, blue 0.4, warm sign spill 1.0, orange city bounce 0.35).

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 293-312): CityLightsBelow builds the window grid with Math.random() inline in render (line 295 visibility, line 308 emissiveIntensity) and is NOT memoized. Any re-render of RooftopFloor re-randomizes which windows are lit and their brightness, causing a visible flicker / non-deterministic scene. Should be precomputed in useMemo (as StarPoints correctly does).
- _medium_ (line 294-300): Window grid uses Math.floor(h/3) rows and Math.floor(w/2.5) cols, then divides by those same floored counts for spacing (e.g. w/Math.floor(w/2.5)). If a building's w<2.5 or h<3, Math.floor yields 0 -> division by zero (Infinity/NaN positions) and Array.from({length:0}) — the current hardcoded data avoids 0 (min w=5,h=18), but it is an unguarded edge case.
- _low_ (line 47, 195, 137): SkyDome sphere radius 195 vs StarPoints r up to 180 vs CityLightsBelow z down to -100 and distant light distance 100 — several large hardcoded world-scale magic numbers that must stay mutually consistent with the building's 12-unit floor stacking; no shared constant documents this relationship.
- _low_ (line 39-41): makeSkyTex sets wrapS=RepeatWrapping but never sets repeat or wrapT, and the texture is applied to a sphere with default UVs — the RepeatWrapping has no visible effect (dead configuration).
- _low_ (line 1): Like other floors, useRef is used but the file also follows the shared pattern; here it IS used (glowRef), so fine — but note SkyDome/StarPoints/CityLightsBelow each allocate large geometry/textures regenerated on every floor remount with no dispose().
- _nit_ (line 316): CityLightsBelow distant pointLight intensity 2.5 distance 100 decay 1 is a very large-radius light; combined with per-floor lights this is a notable but bounded fragment-shading cost.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. CityLightsBelow window visibility and brightness are driven by un-memoized Math.random() (lines 295, 308) so they recompute and flicker on any re-render of this subtree (see issue).

---

## Immersive DOM overlays (HTML layers above the react-three-fiber canvas)

> Eight DOM components rendered on top of the 3D building canvas, all driven by the central `BuildingContext` (phase state machine: preloading → stamp_intro → exterior_* → entering → lobby → floor_* / rooftop / elevator_traveling / teleporting). Animation is split across three libraries: framer-motion for declarative enter/exit/loop (DossierNav, ElevatorHUD, ExteriorHint, FloorContentOverlay, plus the AnimatePresence fade-out shells of Preloader/StampIntro), GSAP timelines for imperative canvas-coordinated sequences (PaperShredTransition, StampIntro), and a hand-rolled RAF lerp spring (UVCursor halo). Canvas-sync is achieved indirectly through the shared `phase` value: PaperShredTransition watches `phase === 'teleporting'` (a 600ms window set by `teleportTo`/`enterBuilding` in BuildingContext) to fly paper in/out while the camera snaps behind it; UVCursor feeds normalized device coords (NDC) into the context via `useMouseNDC` so the 3D shaders can read the cursor without React re-renders. Data flow is mixed: nav/HUD/intro use hardcoded floor manifests (mock/static), while FloorContentOverlay pulls live data from `useFloorData` (real `fetch` against `${API_BASE}/api/history/*` with graceful offline fallback). The visual language is the canonical "Forensic Ledger" paper/ink/stamp identity (cream #f3eee2, iron-gall #14120b, stamp-red #c2331f, gold #a8842c, seal-green #1c6e4a), styled almost entirely with inline style objects and hardcoded hex (no design tokens).

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/DossierNav.tsx`  _(421 lines)_

**Purpose:** Left-edge collapsible 'dossier folder' navigation drawer listing the four building exhibits (studio/risk/detective/vault). Lets the user expand a tab, read a classification detail card, and teleport into a floor.
**Exports:** `default DossierNav (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** DossierNav takes no props. Internal subcomponents: ExhibitTab({floor: FloorExhibit, isSelected: boolean, onClick: ()=>void}); ExhibitCard({floor: FloorExhibit, onEnter: ()=>void}).
**State/refs:** useState selected: FloorId|null (which tab is expanded, line 241); useState isOpen: boolean (drawer open/closed, line 242). No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState x2`, `useBuildingContext (phase, teleportTo, currentFloor)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useState)`, `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext, FloorId type)`
**Data flow:** 100% mock/static. FLOORS is a hardcoded const array of 4 FloorExhibit objects (lines 16-53). currentFloor comes from context for the 'CURRENT:' header label. teleportTo(floorId) is the only outbound effect (line 257). No API calls.
**Styling:** Inline style objects only, hardcoded hex. Ledger palette: cream rgba(243,238,226,*), iron #14120b, stamp-red #c2331f, gold #a8842c, green #1c6e4a. Fonts: Fraunces serif (titles, weight 900), JetBrains Mono (codes/tags). backdropFilter blur(8px) on the panel (line 321).

**Details:** Mounted only when phase is lobby or any floor_* (visible computed lines 245-251); returns null otherwise (line 252) — note rooftop and elevator_traveling are intentionally excluded so the dossier hides on the roof. Outer fixed wrapper at left:0, top:50% translateY(-50%), zIndex 120 (line 261). The inner motion.div slides in from x:-280 to 0 with delay 0.6s, duration 0.5s, custom cubic-bezier [0.22,1,0.36,1] (line 263-265), width 228px. A vertical 'DOSSIER' folder-tab button protrudes at right:-36 (lines 272-309) using writingMode vertical-rl + textOrientation mixed + rotate(180deg) to render text bottom-to-top; the arrow glyph toggles ◀/▶ on isOpen (line 307). The panel itself is wrapped in AnimatePresence and only mounts when isOpen (line 313), animating opacity+x:-20→0 over 0.22s. Each FLOOR maps to an ExhibitTab plus a nested AnimatePresence-wrapped ExhibitCard that mounts when selected===floor.id (lines 362-380). ExhibitTab statusColor is a ternary mapping ACTIVE→green, SEALED→gold, else→red (lines 65-70); whileHover nudges x:4 only when not selected (line 75). Clicking a tab toggles selection (selected===id ? null : id, line 368) so a second click collapses. ExhibitCard (lines 142-236) renders a classification banner, title/subtitle, divider, and an 'ENTER EXHIBIT →' button whose whileHover animates letterSpacing to 0.18em and whileTap scales 0.97. handleEnter (lines 254-258) closes drawer, clears selection, and calls teleportTo. Footer (lines 384-413) shows a dynamic case number CASE NO. V-{new Date().getFullYear()}-001 (line 401) and a static 'VERIFIED' green stamp. Magic numbers: slide delay 0.6, width 228 (must stay in sync with FloorContentOverlay's left:228 offset), tab protrusion -36, folder height 80.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 362-380): ExhibitTab is a motion.button but the expand/collapse uses click only; there is no aria-expanded/aria-controls relationship between tab and card, no focus trap, and no Escape-to-close — an a11y gap given the project's stated WCAG AAA bar.
- _low_ (line 239-252): The visible/return-null pattern unmounts the component entirely outside lobby/floor phases. Because the outer slide-in animation has delay:0.6 (line 265), every time the user returns to a qualifying phase the 280px slide replays with a 0.6s delay; on rapid floor switching the nav repeatedly re-slides in.
- _low_ (line 401): Case number 'V-{year}-001' is hardcoded to '-001' and recomputes new Date() on every render. Cosmetic, but the static suffix undercuts the 'alive/forensic' authenticity intent.
- _nit_ (line 12): FLOORS status union is 'ACTIVE'|'SEALED'|'RESTRICTED' yet only ACTIVE and SEALED are ever assigned in the manifest (no RESTRICTED entry), so the red statusColor branch (line 70) is dead in practice.
- _nit_ (line 89): borderLeft is set conditionally AND a CSS transition:'background 0.2s' (line 89) coexists with framer-motion's whileHover x animation; mixing CSS transitions and motion transforms on the same element is harmless but inconsistent with the rest of the file's pure-motion approach.

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/ElevatorHUD.tsx`  _(272 lines)_

**Purpose:** Right-edge always-visible 'elevator panel' showing all six floor stops (rooftop, vault, detective, risk lab, studio, lobby), highlighting the current floor and letting the user teleport to any other reachable stop.
**Exports:** `default ElevatorHUD (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** ElevatorHUD takes no props. Subcomponents: FloorButton({stop: FloorStop, isCurrent: boolean, isReachable: boolean, onClick: ()=>void}); TravelIndicator() (no props).
**State/refs:** No useState/useRef. Purely derived from context.
**Hooks:** `useBuildingContext (phase, currentFloor, teleportTo, isTransitioning)`
**Dependencies:** `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext, FloorId)`
**Data flow:** 100% mock/static. STOPS hardcoded array of 6 FloorStop objects (lines 12-19). Reads phase/currentFloor/isTransitioning from context; teleportTo(stop.id) is the only outbound effect (line 237).
**Styling:** Inline styles, hardcoded hex. Dark glass aesthetic (rgba(20,18,11,0.88) panel + backdropFilter blur(10px), lines 189-191) — this is the LEGACY dark/glass system per DESIGN.md, contrasting the cream paper of DossierNav. Accent gold #a8842c, active red #c2331f, cream text rgba(243,238,226,*).

**Details:** Visible across far more phases than DossierNav: lobby, all floor_*, rooftop, elevator_traveling, AND teleporting (lines 152-160). activeFlorId (note the typo in the identifier) is derived by a chained ternary (lines 162-167): lobby→'lobby', rooftop→'rooftop', traveling/teleporting→currentFloor ?? 'lobby', else phase.replace('floor_','') cast to FloorId. The whole rail is inside AnimatePresence; the motion.div enters from x:200 opacity:0 with delay 0.4s, exits back to x:200, easing [0.22,1,0.36,1] (lines 173-176). Crucially it uses transformTemplate (line 177) to PREPEND translateY(-50%) before framer's generated transform so the vertical centering survives the x animation — a deliberate fix for the 'transform clobbers translateY' problem. FloorButton (lines 22-123): disabled when !isReachable||isCurrent (line 36); whileHover x:-4 and whileTap scale:0.94 only when reachable+not-current (lines 37-38); a 28x28 number pill goes red bg when current; an active ◀ arrow (lines 107-120) mounts only on the current row with its own initial/animate (no AnimatePresence, so no exit). isReachable is computed at the call site as !isTransitioning && stop.id !== activeFlorId (line 236), so during a transition every button is disabled. TravelIndicator (lines 126-145) is a pulsing '▲ TRAVELING ▲' animating opacity:[0.4,1,0.4] over 0.9s repeat Infinity, shown only during elevator_traveling/teleporting (lines 243-245). Footer (lines 248-266) is a static '◈ CHAIN SEALED ◈' gold seal. Magic numbers: width 170 (FloorContentOverlay reserves right:175 to clear it — a 5px mismatch), enter delay 0.4, pill 28px, pulse 0.9s.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 162): Identifier typo: `activeFlorId` (should be activeFloorId). Cosmetic but propagates through lines 162, 235, 236.
- _low_ (line 167): phase.replace('floor_','') as FloorId is an unchecked cast — a type hole. If a new phase string slips through the visible guard without a matching ternary branch, this silently produces a bad FloorId.
- _low_ (line 165): During teleporting, all buttons are disabled by isTransitioning, but activeFlorId falls back to currentFloor ?? 'lobby' (lines 165-166), so mid-teleport the highlighted stop momentarily points at the OLD floor rather than the destination (currentFloor is only set after the 600ms timer in teleportTo).
- _low_ (line 184): Panel width 170 vs FloorContentOverlay's right:175 reservation (FloorContentOverlay line 181) — a 5px inconsistency in the two files' shared layout contract; a latent coupling that will drift.
- _nit_ (line 107): The active ◀ arrow uses initial/animate without AnimatePresence, so it never animates out when the current floor changes (it disappears with the row re-render).

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/ExteriorHint.tsx`  _(67 lines)_

**Purpose:** Bottom-center 'Click the doors to enter' affordance hint shown only while the user idles outside the building, with a hand-drawn door SVG and a pulsing up-arrow.
**Exports:** `default ExteriorHint (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** None.
**State/refs:** None (no useState/useRef).
**Hooks:** `useBuildingContext (phase only)`
**Dependencies:** `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext)`
**Data flow:** Pure presentational. Reads phase; renders only when phase === 'exterior_idle' (line 6). No outbound actions, no data.
**Styling:** Inline styles, hardcoded hex. Iron #14120b SVG strokes, gold rgba(168,132,44,0.65) arrow, JetBrains Mono label. pointerEvents:'none' on the whole hint (line 27) so it never intercepts the door clicks it advertises.

**Details:** AnimatePresence-wrapped motion.div keyed 'ext-hint' (line 12). Enters opacity:0 y:8 → opacity:1 y:0 with delay 0.9s, duration 0.55s, easeOut (line 16); exits to opacity:0 y:6. Fixed bottom:36 left:50% translateX(-50%) zIndex 100 (lower than nav/HUD at 120). Inline SVG (lines 31-37): two 11x32 door-leaf rects with a center seam, two handle circles at cx 12/16, and a baseline line. The pulse arrow (lines 51-62) is a separate motion.div animating opacity:[0.3,0.7,0.3] and y:[0,-3,0] over 1.8s repeat Infinity easeInOut. The delay:0.9 on entry means the hint waits ~0.9s after entering exterior_idle before fading in. Magic numbers: bottom 36, entry delay 0.9 / dur 0.55, pulse dur 1.8.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 52): Infinite pulse animation (repeat:Infinity) does not respect prefers-reduced-motion. Unlike UVCursor (which bails entirely on reduced-motion), this looping y/opacity animation runs unconditionally — an accessibility gap against the AAA bar.
- _nit_ (line 31): Decorative SVG has no aria-hidden; with pointerEvents:none and a text label present it is low-impact, but the empty <svg> may be announced by some screen readers.
- _nit_ (line 47): Copy 'Click the doors to enter' is mouse-centric; no keyboard/tap equivalent guidance. Minor UX/i18n consideration.

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/FloorContentOverlay.tsx`  _(300 lines)_

**Purpose:** Bottom 'dossier strip' panel that appears when standing on a floor (or rooftop), showing live data rows for that floor (datasets/cases/queries) pulled from the backend, plus a CTA that flashes the screen and routes into the real 2D app page.
**Exports:** `default FloorContentOverlay (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** FloorContentOverlay takes no props. Subcomponents: StatusBadge({status: FloorItem['status'], accent: string}); DataRow({item: FloorItem, accent: string}); ExitFlash({color: string, onDone: ()=>void}).
**State/refs:** useState exiting: boolean (line 135); useState exitColor: string default '#f3eee2' (line 136). No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState x2`, `useCallback x2 (handleEnter line 152, handleFlashDone line 158)`, `useNavigate (react-router)`, `useBuildingContext (phase, currentFloor, isTransitioning)`, `useFloorData(activeFloor)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useState, useCallback)`, `framer-motion (motion, AnimatePresence)`, `react-router-dom (useNavigate)`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext, FloorId)`, `../hooks/useFloorData (useFloorData, FloorItem)`
**Data flow:** REAL data via useFloorData. activeFloor derived from phase (lines 139-145), passed to useFloorData which fetches `${API_BASE}/api/history/{analyses|investigations|queries}` (studio/risk/detective single endpoint, vault+rooftop Promise.allSettled across all three) and maps into FloorItem[]. On fetch failure useFloorData returns {items:[], summary:'BACKEND OFFLINE — SHOWING OFFLINE STATE'}. FLOOR_META (lines 19-45) is static config (route, cta, accent, classification). On CTA, navigate(meta.route) routes into the real React-Router 2D app.
**Styling:** Inline styles, hardcoded hex. Cream paper panel rgba(243,238,226,0.96) + backdropFilter blur(6px), borderTop colored by per-floor meta.accent. Mono for data, Fraunces for the title. Score bars are absolutely-positioned divs whose `right` inset encodes score (line 98).

**Details:** activeFloor maps floor_studio/risk/detective/vault and rooftop to FloorId, else null (lines 139-145); if no meta the component returns null (line 163). visible = !!activeFloor && !isTransitioning && phase !== 'teleporting' (line 150) — isTransitioning already covers most of teleporting, the extra phase check is belt-and-suspenders. The panel motion.div (lines 171-189) enters y:120 opacity:0 → 0 with delay 0.35 dur 0.45 ease [0.22,1,0.36,1]; keyed by activeFloor so switching floors triggers a fresh enter/exit. It is hard-positioned bottom:0 left:228 right:175 (lines 180-181) with inline comments 'clear DossierNav' / 'clear ElevatorHUD'. zIndex 118 sits just under nav/HUD (120). DataRow (lines 70-112): score bar fill is a div with right:`${(1-item.score)*100}%` (line 98), i.e. score 0..1 maps to a left-anchored fill — score>1 or <0 would overflow/invert. data.items.slice(0,4) caps the list at 4 rows (line 243); when empty it shows data.summary (lines 235-241). StatusBadge (lines 48-67) is a lookup keyed by status (VERIFIED green/FLAGGED red/PENDING gold). The CTA (lines 263-292) whileHover animates letterSpacing 0.18em + background #14120b, whileTap scale 0.97, with a perpetually-animating → arrow (x:[0,4,0] repeat Infinity 1.5s). EXIT SEQUENCE: handleEnter (lines 152-156) sets exitColor (special-cases the cyan risk accent #7df9ff → near-black #050818 so the flash isn't neon, else uses meta.accent) and sets exiting=true; ExitFlash (lines 115-129) is a full-screen fixed zIndex 200 overlay animating opacity 0→1 scale 0.92→1 over 0.22s, firing onAnimationComplete→handleFlashDone→navigate(meta.route). Magic numbers: left 228, right 175, enter delay 0.35, slice(0,4), summary width 220, score-bar 48x4, flash dur 0.22.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 21): Route mismatch: FLOOR_META maps detective→'/nlp' but BOTH vault AND rooftop→'/overview' (studio→'/studio', risk→'/risk'). The 'CHAIN VAULT / audit trail' floor (cta 'OPEN AUDIT VAULT') has no dedicated destination — it lands on the same '/overview' as rooftop's 'ENTER PLATFORM', likely a content/route bug or placeholder.
- _medium_ (line 167): ExitFlash renders OUTSIDE the AnimatePresence (line 167) and relies SOLELY on onAnimationComplete to navigate, with no timeout fallback. If the framer-motion animation is interrupted (tab backgrounded, reduced-motion, or a re-render unmounting it) onAnimationComplete may not fire and the navigation silently never happens, stranding the user on the flash. No guard/cleanup exists.
- _medium_ (line 150): On floor change, useFloorData synchronously resets data to EMPTY (summary 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…') then fetches (useFloorData line 151-154). Because the panel is keyed by activeFloor and animates in with delay 0.35, the user can briefly see 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…' on every floor entry even with a fast backend — a perceived-latency flaw. No skeleton distinguishes 'connecting' from 'offline'.
- _low_ (line 98): Score bar right:`${(1-item.score)*100}%` has no clamp. FloorItem.score is documented 0–1 but useFloorData passes raw quality_score (useFloorData line 36) which could exceed 1; out-of-range score overflows or inverts the bar.
- _low_ (line 48): StatusBadge destructures `accent` (line 48) and DataRow passes it (line 109), but StatusBadge never uses accent — dead prop / vestigial threading.
- _low_ (line 181): Hardcoded left:228 / right:175 to 'clear' the sibling overlays is a fragile layout contract; right:175 does not even match ElevatorHUD's actual width 170. On narrow viewports the 228+175 reserved gutters can crush the panel — no responsive breakpoint.
- _low_ (line 154): exitColor special-case keys off the literal hex '#7df9ff' (risk's cyan). This couples FloorContentOverlay to an exact hex in FLOOR_META; changing risk's accent would silently break the flash-color override.

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/PaperShredTransition.tsx`  _(204 lines)_

**Purpose:** Full-screen canvas paper-confetti transition that flies 72 torn-paper polygons toward screen center (cover) while the 3D camera teleports behind it, then scatters them outward (reveal). Synchronized to the BuildingContext 'teleporting' phase.
**Exports:** `default PaperShredTransition (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** None.
**State/refs:** No useState. Refs: canvasRef (HTMLCanvasElement), piecesRef (Piece[]), rafRef (number), tlRef (gsap.core.Timeline|null), prevPhaseRef (BuildingPhase, init 'preloading'), activeRef (boolean re-entrancy lock).
**Hooks:** `useEffect (single, deps [phase])`, `useRef x6`, `useBuildingContext (phase only)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useRef)`, `gsap`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext, BuildingPhase type)`
**Data flow:** Pure presentational/canvas. No data, no props. Reads phase to trigger fly-in/fly-out. Coordinates with the canvas via shared phase: BuildingContext.teleportTo/enterBuilding set phase='teleporting' for 600ms (camera snaps during this window), and this component covers that snap.
**Styling:** Single <canvas> fixed inset:0 zIndex 115 (between FloorContentOverlay 118 and exterior overlays), display:'none' until activated, pointerEvents:'none'. Pieces drawn in Canvas2D with cream-family fills and a faint iron stroke rgba(20,18,11,0.08).

**Details:** PIECE_COUNT=72, 6 cream COLORS cycled by index (line 42). buildPieces (lines 20-48) seeds each piece on a ring at angle (i/72)*2π + random jitter, dist = cw*0.7 + random*cw*0.3 (off-screen), random w 40-120 / h 30-90, a torn polygon of 5-7 verts with per-vertex radius (0.4+rand*0.6)*(w*0.4+h*0.1), random rot, vrot (-3..3), alpha 1, stores startX/startY. drawPieces (lines 50-69) clears, then for each piece with alpha>0 saves ctx, sets globalAlpha, translate/rotate, strokes+fills the polygon. The single useEffect (lines 81-190) sizes the canvas to window inner size ONCE (lines 84-87), grabs ctx, defines flyIn/flyOut, then runs phase-transition logic. flyIn (lines 91-140): guarded by activeRef, rebuilds pieces, resets to start positions alpha 0, shows canvas, builds proxyArr of plain objects and GSAP-tweens them toward center (cw/2 ± rand 120 / ch/2 ± rand 80) over 0.32s stagger each:0.004 from:'random' ease power3.in, copying proxy values back into pieces onUpdate; then starts a self-perpetuating requestAnimationFrame draw loop (lines 131-139). flyOut (lines 143-175): tweens pieces back to startX*1.1 - cw*0.05 etc, alpha→0, 0.38s stagger 0.003 power2.out; onComplete cancels RAF, hides canvas, releases activeRef. Phase logic (lines 178-185): entering teleporting (prev != teleporting) → flyIn; leaving teleporting → flyOut. Cleanup (lines 187-189) ONLY cancels the RAF, does NOT kill tlRef. Magic numbers: 72 pieces, 0.7/0.3 spawn ring, 0.32/0.38 durations, 0.004/0.003 staggers, scatter 120/80, fly-out 1.1/0.05.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 187): Effect cleanup cancels rafRef but does NOT kill tlRef (the GSAP timeline). On unmount mid-transition the timeline keeps running; its onUpdate mutates pieces and its onComplete touches canvasRef.current — a use-after-unmount and a leaked timeline. Cleanup should call tlRef.current?.kill().
- _high_ (line 84): Canvas backing store is sized to window.innerWidth/innerHeight only inside this effect (deps [phase]) and there is NO window resize listener. A viewport resize that does not coincide with a phase change leaves the canvas stale, so paper pieces are mispositioned and may fail to cover the screen during the camera snap.
- _medium_ (line 92): flyIn early-returns if activeRef is already true. If a teleport is triggered again before flyOut completes (rapid floor switching), flyIn no-ops and the existing pieces/animation persist; combined with tlRef.current?.kill() living only inside flyIn (line 108), overlapping transitions can desync the visual from the actual phase and the 600ms camera window.
- _medium_ (line 179): Phase-edge detection fires flyIn whenever phase becomes 'teleporting'. BuildingContext.enterBuilding ALSO sets phase='teleporting' (BuildingContext line 68), so the shred plays on the very first lobby entry, not only on floor-to-floor teleports. If the entry transition is meant to differ, this conflates two journeys — worth confirming against design intent.
- _low_ (line 72): No prefers-reduced-motion handling: the full 72-piece confetti + RAF loop plays for all users, unlike UVCursor which bails. AAA accessibility gap for a large-area motion effect.
- _nit_ (line 146): flyIn builds proxyArr from p.startX/startY while flyOut builds it from current p.x/p.y; the asymmetry is intentional (fly-out resumes wherever pieces are) but undocumented and easy to break.

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/Preloader.tsx`  _(227 lines)_

**Purpose:** Full-screen ledger-styled boot/loading screen with a fake-but-gated progress bar, cycling forensic status messages, a live deterministic pseudo-hash readout, and a chain-integrity indicator. Stalls at 83% until the 3D scene reports ready, then completes.
**Exports:** `default Preloader (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** PreloaderProps { sceneReady: boolean; onComplete: () => void }.
**State/refs:** useState progress (0), msgIdx (0), visible (true). Refs: progressRef (0, source of truth to avoid stale closures), completedRef (false, idempotency guard), intervalRef (setInterval handle).
**Hooks:** `useState x3`, `useRef x3`, `useCallback (complete)`, `useEffect x2 (driver loop; sceneReady unblock)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback)`, `framer-motion (AnimatePresence, motion)`
**Data flow:** Props-driven. sceneReady comes from parent (ultimately BuildingContext.sceneReady set by the 3D scene). onComplete advances the phase. No API. buildHashDisplay is a deterministic function of progress (line 21).
**Styling:** Inline styles. Cream #f3eee2 full-bleed background, zIndex 10000 (above almost everything). Ledger authenticity: repeating-linear-gradient ruled lines every 28px (lines 97-99) and a red left-margin rule at left:72 (lines 104-114). Fraunces wordmark, JetBrains Mono throughout. Progress is a 1px hairline track with a leading tick.

**Details:** MESSAGES is a const 8-tuple of forensic phrases ending 'CLEARANCE GRANTED' (lines 9-18). buildHashDisplay(progress) (lines 21-29) builds 0x + 16 hex bytes from seed = floor(progress*997 + i*127) % 256 — explicitly avoids Math.random in render so the hash is stable per progress value (documented, line 20). complete() (lines 39-45) is idempotent via completedRef, clears the interval, sets visible=false, calls onComplete after 680ms (matching the 0.68s exit fade). Driver effect (lines 48-59): setInterval every 155ms; if !sceneReady && progress>=83 it returns early (the 83 STALL gate), else advances progress by random 3-12 (Math.random()*9+3), clamps to 100, updates msgIdx = min(floor(p/100*7),7), and if p>=100 schedules complete after 420ms. Unblock effect (lines 62-70): when sceneReady && progress>=83 && !completed, clears interval, jumps to 100, sets last message, completes after 420ms. Progress fill and leading tick are framer animated to width/left `${progress}%` over 0.22s (lines 142-165). Message span keyed by msgIdx cross-fades. Percent zero-padded to 3 digits (line 191). Chain integrity flips 'CHECKING…' gold → 'VERIFIED ✓' green at progress>=100 (lines 206-209). Hash readout updates every tick (line 218). AnimatePresence exit fades overlay opacity→0 over 0.68s ease [0.4,0,0.2,1]. Magic numbers: stall 83, tick 155ms, delta 3-12, complete delays 420/680ms, hash seed 997/127, ruled lines 28px, margin 72, wordmark 2.8rem, track 312px.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 50): No maximum-time failsafe: if sceneReady NEVER arrives, the driver early-returns at line 50 once progress hits 83, pinning the bar permanently and hanging the boot forever with no timeout/escape. A hard cap fallback would prevent a stuck preloader.
- _medium_ (line 56): Two paths can schedule setTimeout(complete,420): the driver when p>=100 (line 56) and the unblock effect (line 68). complete() is idempotent (completedRef), so it won't double-run, but the orphaned setTimeout from the losing path is never cleared — a dangling timer can fire after unmount, calling clearInterval/setVisible on an unmounted component (React warns).
- _medium_ (line 48): The driver setInterval lives in an effect with deps [sceneReady, complete]. On sceneReady false→true the cleanup clears the old interval and creates a NEW one, restarting the 155ms cadence; any in-flight setTimeout(complete) survives across the re-subscribe, so the two effects encode overlapping, order-dependent completion logic that is hard to reason about.
- _low_ (line 52): Math.random() in the progress delta makes load time non-deterministic and untestable; combined with the 83 stall and no failsafe, there is no reproducible boot timing.
- _nit_ (line 82): zIndex 10000 vs StampIntro 9998 vs UVCursor 9999 — three different magic z-indices in three files with no shared constant; easy to collide as overlays grow.

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/StampIntro.tsx`  _(268 lines)_

**Purpose:** Cinematic intro: a red 'VERITA' rubber stamp drops and impacts, then black ink diffuses outward across a cream canvas (revealing cream VERITA text against the spreading dark), a tagline fades in, and the whole overlay fades out. Plays after the Preloader.
**Exports:** `default StampIntro (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** StampIntroProps { onComplete: () => void }.
**State/refs:** useState visible(true), stampGone(false), inkProgress(0), taglineVisible(false). Refs: canvasRef, stampWrapRef, stampInnerRef, noisePointsRef ([number,number][]).
**Hooks:** `useState x4`, `useRef x4`, `useEffect x4 (precompute noise; size canvas; redraw on inkProgress; GSAP sequence)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useRef, useState)`, `framer-motion (AnimatePresence, motion)`, `gsap`
**Data flow:** Props-driven (onComplete). Pure presentational; no data, no context. Self-contained animation. The ink-circle edge geometry is precomputed locally.
**Styling:** Inline styles. Full-screen fixed zIndex 9998, cursor:'none' (line 163 — hides the OS cursor so UVCursor takes over). Canvas paints cream then a dark-ink polygon. VERITA in cream Fraunces clamp(5rem,12vw,10rem); the red stamp uses a 5px double border #c2331f. Tagline JetBrains Mono.

**Details:** Effect 1 (lines 21-38): precomputes 480 ink-edge points once — radius n = 1.0 plus six sine/cosine harmonics (freq 3.7/7.1/11.3/17.9/23.3/31.1, decreasing amplitudes 0.034→0.005) for an organic rubber-stamp edge; stored in noisePointsRef. Effect 2 (lines 41-49): sizes canvas to viewport once and paints it cream. Effect 3 (lines 52-79): on every inkProgress change repaints cream, then if inkProgress>0 draws the dark polygon at radius r = inkProgress * maxR where maxR = sqrt(cx²+cy²)*1.28 (reaches past the furthest corner). Effect 4 (lines 86-148) is the master GSAP sequence using a CLOSURE `killed` flag instead of a startedRef — explicitly documented (lines 82-85) as a React StrictMode fix so the double-invoked effect doesn't permanently kill the animation. Sequence: gsap.set stampInner y:-180 opacity:1; runSequence after 160ms: (1) stamp descends y→0 0.30s power3.in; (2) impact squash scaleX 1.06/scaleY 0.91 0.06s yoyo repeat 1; (3) stamp opacity→0 0.10s then setStampGone(true); (4) ink tween obj.p 0→1 over 1.30s power2.out driving setInkProgress each onUpdate; (5) +160ms setTaglineVisible(true); (6) +1850ms setVisible(false) then +720ms onComplete(). Cleanup sets killed=true, clears the 160ms timeout, gsap.killTweensOf(stampInner). Every nested callback re-checks killed. AnimatePresence exit fades overlay opacity→0 over 0.72s ease [0.4,0,0.2,1] (matches the 720ms before onComplete). Magic numbers: 480 noise pts, harmonic freqs/amps, maxR 1.28, stamp y -180, durations 0.30/0.06/0.10/1.30, delays 160/1850/720ms.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 148): Effect 4 has deps [onComplete]. If the parent passes a non-memoized onComplete (a fresh function each render), the entire GSAP sequence tears down (killed=true, killTweensOf) and restarts from y:-180 on every parent re-render — the intro visibly stutters/restarts. Correctness depends on the caller memoizing onComplete; nothing here guards against that.
- _medium_ (line 41): Canvas sized once with no resize handling (effect deps []). If the window resizes during the ~4s intro, the cream fill and ink polygon use stale dimensions and maxR (computed from old cx/cy), leaving uncovered cream edges as the ink expands.
- _low_ (line 150): No prefers-reduced-motion handling and no skip-on-click affordance: the full ~4-second cinematic plays for everyone. Against the AAA bar this should offer a reduced/skip path.
- _low_ (line 67): maxR multiplier 1.28 is tuned for typical aspect ratios; on extreme aspect ratios sqrt(cx²+cy²)*1.28 may under/over-shoot, and combined with the no-resize issue can leave uncovered corners.
- _nit_ (line 227): stampWrapRef is attached (line 227) but never read — dead ref (only stampInnerRef is animated).

### `frontend/src/immersive/dom/UVCursor.tsx`  _(109 lines)_

**Purpose:** Custom 'UV lamp' cursor: an exact inner cyan dot plus a spring-lagged outer halo ring (screen blend mode) that grows on hover and shrinks on click. Also feeds normalized device coordinates to the 3D context for shader uniforms.
**Exports:** `default UVCursor (React.FC)`
**Props/inputs:** None.
**State/refs:** useState pos {x,y} (exact cursor, init -200,-200 offscreen); hovering; clicking; laggedPos {x,y} (rendered halo position). Refs: laggedRef (lerp source of truth), targetRef (latest cursor target), rafRef (RAF handle).
**Hooks:** `useState x4`, `useRef x3`, `useEffect x2 (mouse listeners; RAF spring loop)`, `useBuildingContext (setMouseNDC, uvActive)`, `useMouseNDC(setMouseNDC)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect, useRef, useState)`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext)`, `../hooks/useMouseNDC (useMouseNDC)`
**Data flow:** Reads uvActive from context (controls cursor opacity). Writes mouse NDC into context via useMouseNDC(setMouseNDC) — this is the canvas-sync channel: BuildingContext stores NDC in a mutable ref (mouseNDCRef, BuildingContext lines 50-52) so useFrame/shaders read cursor position WITHOUT triggering React re-renders. No API.
**Styling:** Inline styles. Two fixed divs zIndex 9999, pointerEvents:none. Halo: radial cyan gradient rgba(125,249,255,*) + 1px border + glow box-shadow, mixBlendMode:'screen', willChange:transform, CSS transition on width/height (0.14s) and opacity (0.35s). Inner dot 8px solid #7df9ff with glow.

**Details:** Effect 1 (lines 18-35): registers passive mousemove (updates targetRef and pos state, and sets hovering via e.target.closest('a, button, [data-interactive]')), plus mousedown→clicking true / mouseup→false; all three removed on cleanup. Effect 2 (lines 38-52): RAF lerp spring — each tick lerps laggedRef toward targetRef at SPEED 0.09 (laggedRef += (target-lagged)*0.09) and calls setLaggedPos with a fresh object, then re-schedules; cancelled on unmount. useMouseNDC(setMouseNDC) (line 55) adds a SECOND passive mousemove listener converting clientX/Y to NDC and pushing into context. prefers-reduced-motion: if reduce, the component returns null (line 57) — fully disabling the custom cursor for reduced-motion users. haloSize is 68 when clicking, 116 when hovering, else 88 (line 59); opacity 1 when uvActive else 0.45 (line 60). The halo is transform-positioned at laggedPos - half (lagged center) while the inner dot is at exact pos - 4 (line 96). Magic numbers: offscreen init -200, SPEED 0.09, halo sizes 68/116/88, dot 8px, transitions 0.14s/0.35s.

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 38): The RAF spring calls setLaggedPos({...laggedRef.current}) on EVERY frame (~60fps) unconditionally — even when the cursor is idle and laggedRef has already converged on targetRef. This is a continuous per-frame React state update + re-render for the component's whole life, defeating the 'zero overhead' goal stated in the comment (line 12). It should stop the loop (or skip setState) once |target-lagged| is below a threshold.
- _medium_ (line 57): The prefers-reduced-motion check `window.matchMedia(...).matches` is read during render and returns null, but it is NOT a reactive subscription — if the user toggles reduced-motion at runtime, the cursor won't appear/disappear until an unrelated re-render. Also evaluating matchMedia in the render body conflates a side-effecting query with render. (Hook order is safe because the early return is after all hooks.)
- _medium_ (line 27): Two separate passive mousemove listeners are registered on window — one in Effect 1 (handleMove, which setPos's every move) and one inside useMouseNDC (line 55). Every mouse move runs both handlers and triggers a React re-render for the exact dot, on top of the per-frame halo re-render. The two could be unified.
- _low_ (line 22): hovering detection uses (e.target as HTMLElement).closest(...). If e.target is ever a non-Element, .closest is undefined and would throw. Practically mousemove targets are Elements, but the unchecked cast is a latent type/runtime hole.
- _low_ (line 57): When UVCursor returns null under reduced-motion, the OS cursor must be restored elsewhere; StampIntro and presumably the app shell set cursor:'none'. If any ancestor keeps cursor:none while UVCursor is null, reduced-motion users could be left with NO visible cursor — a cross-file coupling worth verifying.
- _nit_ (line 13): Both laggedRef AND laggedPos hold the halo position (ref as lerp source, state to trigger render). Standard pattern, but the dual source invites a stale-state bug if future edits read laggedPos instead of laggedRef in the tick.

---

## Immersive layer custom hooks (frontend/src/immersive/hooks)

> Five custom React hooks that drive the react-three-fiber immersive "Forensic Ledger building" experience. useCameraJourney owns the cinematic camera (GSAP tweens + per-frame parallax driven by phase state in BuildingContext). useMouseNDC is a tiny window mousemove listener that emits normalized device coordinates consumed by the camera and UV shader. useFloorData and useLiveLedger both fetch the same backend history endpoints (analyses / queries / investigations) — useFloorData returns per-floor summary cards on phase change, while useLiveLedger paints a scrolling forensic-ledger texture onto an offscreen canvas (RAF animation + 4s polling) wrapped as a THREE.CanvasTexture. useRiskLabUVReveal builds a custom shader material whose mouse/time uniforms are updated per frame for the UV-lamp reveal effect on the Risk floor. Data is real (FastAPI history API via API_BASE) with graceful offline fallbacks; useLiveLedger also ships a 14-row hardcoded placeholder deck.

### `frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useCameraJourney.ts`  _(129 lines)_

**Purpose:** Drives the r3f camera through the immersive building: GSAP-tweens the camera position/look target on BuildingPhase changes (exterior approach, lobby, each floor, rooftop) and applies continuous mouse-parallax in a useFrame loop. Returns void; mutates the shared three.js camera directly.
**Exports:** `useCameraJourney (named, returns void)`
**Props/inputs:** No params. Reads camera from useThree(); reads phase, mouseNDC, setPhase from useBuildingContext().
**State/refs:** No useState. Refs: activeTween (gsap.core.Tween | null) to track/kill the in-flight tween; lookTarget (THREE.Vector3, init clone of LOOK.exterior) the smoothed lookAt point; parallax ({x,y}) accumulator for eased mouse offset.
**Hooks:** `useThree`, `useBuildingContext (custom)`, `useRef x3`, `useEffect ([phase])`, `useFrame`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useRef)`, `@react-three/fiber (useThree,useFrame)`, `three`, `gsap`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext, BuildingPhase)`
**Data flow:** Pure props/context -> three.js camera mutation. No network. phase + mouseNDC come from BuildingContext; setPhase is called to advance exterior_approach -> exterior_idle on tween complete.
**Styling:** N/A (3D camera logic, no DOM/CSS).

**Details:** Floor Y layout: LOBBY_FLOOR_Y=-9.5, FLOOR_STEP=12; floorCamY(idx)=-9.5+idx*12+1.7 (eye height), floorLookY(idx)=...+1.6 (look 0.1 below eye). FLOOR_INDEX maps phases lobby0/floor_studio1/floor_risk2/floor_detective3/floor_vault4/rooftop5 (Partial<Record>). Named anchors: approach_start (0,5,42), exterior_idle (0,2,34), LOOK.exterior (0,1,-22). buildFloorCam(idx) -> pos (0,camY,-19) look (0,lookY,-34). useEffect on [phase] (exhaustive-deps disabled at L101): (1) if phase==='exterior_approach' snap camera to approach_start, copy lookTarget=LOOK.exterior, kill() prior tween, then gsap.to camera.position -> exterior_idle over 2.0s power2.out, onComplete setPhase('exterior_idle'). (2) Floor phases: kill(), compute target; BIG-JUMP guard L79 if |camera.z - target.z|>18 -> hard-copy pos+look and return (lets the paper-shred transition mask the cut, no fly-through); SNAP-Y guard L86 if |camera.y-target.y|>8 -> instantly set y for pos+look to avoid visible vertical travel; then two parallel 0.7s power2.out tweens for lookTarget and camera.position. useFrame loop: isExterior branch eases parallax toward mouseNDC.x*1.4 / y*0.7 at 0.035, and only when notMoving (tween inactive) lerps camera x/y toward exterior_idle+parallax at 0.04. Interior branch: when floorIdx defined and tween inactive, eases parallax toward mouseNDC.x*0.5 / y*0.25 at 0.025 then lerps camera x/y toward base.pos+parallax at 0.03. Every frame ends with camera.lookAt(lookTarget.current). Magic numbers throughout (1.4,0.7,0.035,0.04,0.5,0.25,0.025,0.03,18,8,0.7s,2.0s). The parallax accumulator is shared between exterior and interior branches and is never reset on phase change, so it carries over.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 101-102): useEffect deps intentionally only [phase] with exhaustive-deps disabled. camera, setPhase, mouseNDC are referenced but omitted. camera/setPhase are stable in practice, but the suppression hides that buildFloorCam allocates new THREE.Vector3 objects on every phase change (L36-41, also called twice per frame in useFrame L122) — minor GC churn each frame.
- _medium_ (line 122): Perf: buildFloorCam(floorIdx) is called inside useFrame on every animation frame for interior phases, allocating two new THREE.Vector3 every frame (~60/sec). Should hoist a memoized base per phase instead of reallocating.
- _low_ (line 50-53): kill() nulls activeTween.current, but the GSAP tween created for lookTarget (L91) is never tracked in a ref and never killed on phase change — only the camera.position tween is killed. A rapid phase switch can leave a stale lookTarget tween running concurrently with the new one, briefly fighting over lookTarget.current.
- _low_ (line 43): Hook returns void and side-effects the shared camera; there is no unmount cleanup that kills the active tween (kill() is only called inside the effect body on the next phase change). If the component unmounts mid-tween, the GSAP tween keeps running against a possibly-disposed camera until it completes.
- _nit_ (line 79): Magic thresholds 18 (z-jump, L79) and 8 (y-jump, L86) are unexplained constants implicitly tied to FLOOR_STEP=12 geometry; brittle if floor spacing changes.
- _nit_ (line 118): FLOOR_INDEX[phase] returns 0 for 'lobby'; the interior parallax branch correctly uses `floorIdx !== undefined` (handles index 0) rather than a falsy check — deliberate and correct, worth noting.

### `frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useFloorData.ts`  _(167 lines)_

**Purpose:** Fetches per-floor summary data (items + summary line + total count) from the backend history API when the active floor changes, with per-floor fetcher functions and offline fallback. Returns a FloorData object for floor UI panels.
**Exports:** `FloorItem (interface)`, `FloorData (interface)`, `useFloorData (named hook, returns FloorData)`
**Props/inputs:** useFloorData(floorId: FloorId | null) -> FloorData.
**State/refs:** useState<FloorData> data, initialized to EMPTY ({items:[], summary:'CONNECTING TO SERVER…', totalCount:0}). No refs.
**Hooks:** `useState`, `useEffect ([floorId])`
**Dependencies:** `react (useState,useEffect)`, `../context/BuildingContext (FloorId)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`, `fetch (global)`
**Data flow:** REAL backend via API_BASE. GET /api/history/analyses, /investigations, /queries (limit varies per floor). studio/risk/detective hit one endpoint; vault/rooftop hit all three via Promise.allSettled. rooftop's 4 'system status' items are HARDCODED (Analysis Engine/Risk Model v2.1/NLP Investigation/Chain Integrity) — only the summary counts come from the API.
**Styling:** N/A (data hook). status enum ('VERIFIED'|'FLAGGED'|'PENDING') is consumed by UI for stamp styling.

**Details:** fetchers map keyed by floor short-id: studio/risk/detective/vault/rooftop (NOTE: no 'lobby' key). fetchStudio: analyses?limit=6, derives totalRows (sum row_count), avgQ (mean quality_score*100 rounded), label sliced to 32 chars, meta 'GRADE x', status VERIFIED if grade in [A,B] else PENDING, score=quality_score||0.5, summary 'N datasets ● X.XM rows ● Y% avg quality'. fetchRisk: investigations?limit=6, scoreMap {CRITICAL .95,HIGH .72,MEDIUM .45,LOW .22,MINIMAL .08}, FLAGGED if CRITICAL/HIGH else VERIFIED, score fallback 0.4, summary 'flagged ● clear ● cases total' — 'clear' counts only LOW/MINIMAL so MEDIUM is neither flagged nor clear. fetchDetective: queries?limit=6, ok=count(q.ok), status VERIFIED if q.ok else FLAGGED, score 0.85/0.2, meta '{row_count} rows'. fetchVault: Promise.allSettled of analyses/investigations/queries limit=4, builds items from first 2 analyses (hardcoded score 0.88) + first 2 invests (score 0.72) — queries fetched and counted into total but NOT rendered as items; summary 'total records sealed ● chain integrity: VERIFIED' (always literal VERIFIED). fetchRooftop: allSettled limit=2, 4 hardcoded system items, summary with live counts + 'ALL SYSTEMS NOMINAL'. Hook effect: if !floorId or no fetcher -> setData(EMPTY) and return; else setData(EMPTY) (loading flash), cancelled flag, call fetcher, on success setData if !cancelled, on catch set offline FloorData ('BACKEND OFFLINE — SHOWING OFFLINE STATE'); cleanup sets cancelled=true. EMPTY constant reused as both initial and loading state.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 21-27): No HTTP status check before r.json() (also L43-49, L65-71): a 404/500 returns a non-JSON or error body and r.json() may throw or yield unexpected shape. studio/risk/detective have no own try/catch and rely solely on the hook-level .catch — a single bad response replaces the whole floor with the offline state even if the endpoint exists.
- _medium_ (line 152): setData(EMPTY) is called synchronously on every floorId change before the fetch resolves, so the panel flashes 'CONNECTING TO SERVER…' on each navigation even when data returns in <100ms. No debounce / stale-while-revalidate; previous floor's data discarded immediately.
- _low_ (line 29): avgQ assumes quality_score is a 0–1 fraction (*100), but the per-item score (L36) uses quality_score directly as a 0–1 progress value (fallback 0.5). If backend ever returns 0–100 scores, summary shows e.g. 8800% — no clamping/validation.
- _low_ (line 49): fetchRisk summary 'clear' = LOW/MINIMAL only; MEDIUM cases counted in neither flagged nor clear, so flagged+clear can be < list.length, which may confuse displayed totals.
- _low_ (line 91): ar.value.analyses access after allSettled assumes fulfilled value is an object with .analyses; if endpoint returns valid JSON of a different shape (array/error) .analyses is undefined and silently coerces to [] — masks backend contract drift.
- _nit_ (line 35): Status mapping logic ('A'/'B' grade, CRITICAL/HIGH risk) is duplicated near-verbatim in useLiveLedger.ts (L54,78) — two sources of truth for the same VERIFIED/FLAGGED rules; should be shared.
- _nit_ (line 19): EMPTY is a module-level object reused as initial, loading, and (shape of) offline fallback; FloorData.items is an array so accidental mutation of EMPTY.items would leak across consumers (no mutation today, but it's a shared reference).

**Dead code / TODOs:** rooftop system-status items are hardcoded mock placeholders (Risk Model v2.1 string duplicates a version baked elsewhere). fetchVault fetches queries (qr) and counts them in total but never surfaces them as items — wasted request. 'lobby' floor has no fetcher so it always renders EMPTY/CONNECTING.

### `frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useLiveLedger.ts`  _(249 lines)_

**Purpose:** Generates a continuously-scrolling 'forensic audit ledger' as an offscreen 2D canvas and returns it wrapped in a THREE.CanvasTexture for mapping onto 3D geometry. Polls the backend history API every 4s for real entries; falls back to a hardcoded placeholder deck.
**Exports:** `LedgerEntry (interface)`, `useLiveLedger (named hook, returns THREE.CanvasTexture | null)`
**Props/inputs:** No params.
**State/refs:** useState<THREE.CanvasTexture|null> texture (null until effect creates canvas). Refs: entriesRef (LedgerEntry[]) live data buffer mutated by poll without re-render; scrollRef (number) accumulated scroll offset; rafRef (number) requestAnimationFrame id.
**Hooks:** `useState`, `useRef x3`, `useEffect ([] mount-once)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useEffect,useRef,useState)`, `three (CanvasTexture)`, `../../config (API_BASE)`, `fetch (global)`, `document.createElement('canvas')`, `CanvasRenderingContext2D`
**Data flow:** REAL backend via API_BASE: Promise.allSettled of analyses?limit=10, queries?limit=10, investigations?limit=8 in fetchEntries(); maps to LedgerEntry, sorts desc by timestamp string. On any throw or empty -> render() substitutes placeholder() (14 hardcoded fake rows). Data flows into entriesRef (not state) so the RAF render picks it up without React re-render.
**Styling:** Imperative Canvas 2D drawing — paper #f9f4e8, JetBrains Mono fonts at fractional px sizes, ruled lines, red left margin rule, green VERIFIED / red FLAGGED stamp rects, amber PENDING and hash text. The resulting CanvasTexture is the styling surface; no CSS/DOM.

**Details:** Constants: CW=720, CH=490, LH=21 (line height), ML=18 (margin-left). shortHash(seed): djb2-style (h=5381, (h<<5)+h ^ charCode) -> 0x + 8-hex uppercase. fmtTime(iso): Date->ISO, 'T'->space, slice(0,19)+'Z'; catch slices raw string. tickHash(): time-bucketed (Date.now()/1800 ms ≈ 1.8s buckets) murmur-ish mix -> 0x+8hex+'…', so displayed CHAIN HEAD changes ~every 1.8s — purely cosmetic, not a real hash. fetchEntries: builds ANALYSIS/QUERY/INVESTIGATION entries; ids padded to 4 (A-0001); analysis hash=shortHash(`A{id}{dataset_id}`); investigation prefers real inv.chain_head (first 8 chars uppercased) else shortHash; status mapping mirrors useFloorData; final sort by b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp) (string compare, relies on ISO lexical order). placeholder(): 14 rows from LABELS[i%9] (only 9 labels), TYPES[i%3], STATUSES[i%4] (weighted VERIFIED,VERIFIED,FLAGGED,PENDING), timestamps stepping back 2,700,000ms (45min) each. render(): paints background, ruled horizontal lines every LH from y=LH to CH-26, vertical red rule at x=46, header text, column header string, draws up to 20 row slots with vertical scroll: offsetY=scroll%LH, startIdx=floor(scroll/LH)%len, each slot y=36+slot*LH-offsetY, break when y>CH-28; per-row draws number (right-aligned, resets textAlign), id, timestamp, type, status stamp box, hash; footer bar at CH-26 with live tickHash() and '{n} RECORDS SEALED'. useEffect (mount-once): creates canvas CW×CH, THREE.CanvasTexture with anisotropy=4, setTexture, gets 2d ctx (non-null assertion!), animate() RAF increments scrollRef by 0.35/frame, renders, sets tex.needsUpdate, schedules next frame; poll() awaits fetchEntries and writes entriesRef; initial poll() + setInterval(poll,4000). Cleanup: cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef), clearInterval, tex.dispose(). Canvas element not explicitly removed (never appended to DOM, GC'd with closure).

**Issues:**
- _high_ (line 223): const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')! uses a non-null assertion. If the 2d context is unavailable (rare, but possible under canvas exhaustion / headless), ctx is null and the first render() throws inside the RAF loop, breaking the texture silently. No guard.
- _medium_ (line 49): Type holes: ar.value.analyses (also L61,73) iterated as any — a.quality_grade, q.ok, inv.risk_level, a.dataset_id, *.created_at accessed untyped/unguarded. a.dataset_id undefined produces hash seed like 'A5undefined'; created_at missing -> fmtTime('') -> new Date('') Invalid Date -> toISOString throws -> caught -> ''.slice(0,19)+'Z' = 'Z' as the timestamp, then sorted by 'Z'. Silent data corruption rather than error.
- _medium_ (line 89): Sort uses b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp) on formatted 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SSZ' strings. Works only because the format is lexically monotonic; any entry whose timestamp degraded to 'Z' (see above) or a differing format sorts unpredictably to top/bottom.
- _medium_ (line 225-231): RAF render loop runs unconditionally for the hook's whole lifetime (~60fps redraw of a 720x490 canvas + texture upload) regardless of whether the ledger is on-screen/visible. No visibility/pause gating; constant GPU texture re-upload (needsUpdate every frame) and CPU repaint even when the floor isn't in view.
- _low_ (line 31): tickHash buckets on Date.now()/1800 — 1800 is milliseconds, not a clean second boundary; intent unclear and the trailing '…' implies truncation that isn't real. Cosmetic but misleading as an audit artifact in a compliance product.
- _low_ (line 96-100): LABELS has 9 entries indexed by i%9 while placeholder builds 14 rows — labels repeat (rows 9–13 reuse labels 0–4) but TYPES (i%3) and STATUSES (i%4) cycle on different periods, so label/type pairing is arbitrary (e.g. a 'csv' label can get type QUERY).
- _low_ (line 42-46): fetchEntries wraps Promise.allSettled in try/catch; allSettled never rejects, so the catch only guards parse errors before settling and rarely fires. A bad single endpoint just yields fewer results (acceptable), but the try/catch gives a false sense of comprehensive handling.
- _nit_ (line 112): Layout magic numbers (column x-offsets 36,54,132,342,452,516; stamp widths 58/52; font sizes 7/7.5/8/8.5/10px) are scattered literals in render() with no named constants — fragile to tweak.

**Dead code / TODOs:** placeholder() is intentional mock data (14 financial-crime-themed rows) used as offline fallback AND as the render source whenever rows.length===0. tickHash 'CHAIN HEAD' is a cosmetic fake-hash, not derived from real chain data.

### `frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useMouseNDC.ts`  _(17 lines)_

**Purpose:** Attaches a passive window mousemove listener and reports the pointer as normalized device coordinates (x,y in [-1,1], y flipped) via a callback. Feeds camera parallax and the UV-reveal shader.
**Exports:** `useMouseNDC (named hook, returns void)`
**Props/inputs:** useMouseNDC(onMove: (x:number, y:number) => void) -> void.
**State/refs:** No state/refs. handler memoized via useCallback([onMove]).
**Hooks:** `useCallback ([onMove])`, `useEffect ([handler])`
**Dependencies:** `react (useCallback,useEffect)`, `window (addEventListener/removeEventListener, innerWidth/innerHeight)`
**Data flow:** DOM event -> callback. x=(clientX/innerWidth)*2-1, y=-(clientY/innerHeight)*2+1 (standard NDC with inverted Y). No network/state.
**Styling:** N/A.

**Details:** handler is recreated whenever onMove identity changes (useCallback dep [onMove]); the effect re-binds the listener on every handler change (add on mount/handler-change, remove on cleanup) — correct add/remove pairing. Listener registered { passive: true } so it cannot preventDefault (fine, read-only). Reads window.innerWidth/innerHeight at event time, so it stays correct across resize without a resize listener. Returns void; purely a side-effecting subscription. No throttling/RAF — fires on every native mousemove, but per-event work is trivial (two divides) and downstream consumers throttle via their own lerp/useFrame.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 4-11): If the caller passes an inline arrow as onMove (not memoized), useCallback's [onMove] dep changes every render, recreating handler and tearing down/re-adding the window listener on every parent render. Correctness is fine but it puts a memoization burden on every caller; an internal ref-to-latest-callback pattern would make it bind-once.
- _nit_ (line 5): Typed against MouseEvent only; touch/pointer input is not handled, so parallax is mouse-only (no touchmove/pointermove). Acceptable for a desktop immersive scene but a coverage gap on touch devices.
- _nit_ (line 3): Returns void with no SSR guard on window; fine in a Vite client-only app but would throw if ever imported in an SSR/test environment without a window.

### `frontend/src/immersive/hooks/useUVReveal.ts`  _(43 lines)_

**Purpose:** Builds (memoized) the custom UV-reveal ShaderMaterial for the Risk Lab floor — a dark base texture plus hidden 'UV ink' texture revealed under a mouse-driven spotlight — and updates its uMouse/uTime uniforms every frame.
**Exports:** `useRiskLabUVReveal (named hook, returns the ShaderMaterial from createUVRevealMaterial)`
**Props/inputs:** No params. Reads mouseNDC from useBuildingContext().
**State/refs:** No useState. Ref: mouseRef (THREE.Vector2 init (0,0)) scratch vector to push NDC into the uniform without per-frame allocation. material is created via useMemo.
**Hooks:** `useMemo ([] once)`, `useRef`, `useFrame`, `useBuildingContext (custom)`
**Dependencies:** `react (useMemo,useRef)`, `@react-three/fiber (useFrame)`, `three`, `../context/BuildingContext (useBuildingContext)`, `../canvas/materials/uvReveal (createUVRevealMaterial, makeUVInkTex, drawRiskLabUVInk)`
**Data flow:** No network. Context mouseNDC -> material.uniforms.uMouse each frame; clock.elapsedTime -> material.uniforms.uTime. Procedurally generated textures (no asset/API).
**Styling:** Imperative Canvas 2D inside makeUVInkTex builds the base map: fill #0a0812, cyan grid rgba(125,249,255,0.18) lineWidth 0.5 every 32px (matches RiskLabFloor floor color per comment). uvMap from drawRiskLabUVInk with repeat.set(2,4). Actual reveal blending lives in the shader in canvas/materials/uvReveal (not in this file).

**Details:** useMemo([]) builds material once: baseMap=makeUVInkTex(512,512, gridDrawCallback) — draws solid dark fill then cyan grid lines (both horizontal and vertical) in a single loop `for i 0..w step 32` using w for both axes (assumes square 512). uvMap=makeUVInkTex(512,512, drawRiskLabUVInk) then uvMap.repeat.set(2,4) (tiles hidden ink 2x horizontal, 4x vertical). createUVRevealMaterial(baseMap, uvMap) returns the ShaderMaterial. useFrame: mouseRef.set(mouseNDC.x, mouseNDC.y); material.uniforms.uMouse.value.copy(mouseRef.current); material.uniforms.uTime.value=clock.elapsedTime. uMouse fed raw NDC ([-1,1]), so the shader is responsible for NDC->UV/world mapping. Magic numbers: 512 texture size, 32px grid, repeat (2,4), grid alpha 0.18, color #0a0812.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 15-33): The material and its two 512x512 CanvasTextures created in useMemo are never disposed on unmount. No useEffect cleanup calling material.dispose()/baseMap.dispose()/uvMap.dispose(). When the Risk floor remounts (phase changes in/out) this leaks GPU texture + program memory each cycle.
- _low_ (line 35-40): useFrame runs for the hook's entire lifetime updating uMouse/uTime even when the Risk floor isn't the active phase/visible; no gating on phase. Minor, but it keeps the shader animating off-screen.
- _low_ (line 37-38): mouseRef.set then copy is an extra indirection; material.uniforms.uMouse.value.set(mouseNDC.x, mouseNDC.y) would be simpler and equally allocation-free. Minor dead-ish indirection.
- _low_ (line 16-25): Grid loop uses w for both axes with `i <= w` step 32 on a hardcoded 512 texture; if makeUVInkTex were ever called non-square this draws an incorrect grid. Tightly coupled to the 512x512 literals passed two lines above.
- _nit_ (line 11): Return type is inferred from createUVRevealMaterial; no explicit return annotation, so consumers depend on cross-module inference — slight type-stability risk if uvReveal's signature changes.

**Dead code / TODOs:** None in this file. mouseRef is slightly redundant — set from mouseNDC then copied into the uniform; could copy mouseNDC straight into uMouse.value, but the scratch ref avoids allocating a Vector2 per frame.

---

## Frontend chart/option utilities, global design-system CSS, and the Vitest test harness + build/deploy config

> This subsystem covers three concerns. (1) Data-to-view transform: lib/chartOptions.ts maps backend-pre-aggregated chart specs (line/bar/pie/histogram/heatmap) and forecast payloads (actual + dashed forecast + a stacked CI band) into ECharts options, with its unit test asserting option shape and null-padding alignment. (2) The design system: index.css (728 lines) is the token source of truth for the 'Dual World' system — Forensic Ledger (paper/ink/stamp) plus a legacy Machine World (CRT/amber) — declaring all CSS custom properties, four named-only web-font stacks, a radius scale, an html.light Porcelain theme, ledger/glass surfaces, an extensive landing animation library, print/react-grid-layout overrides, and a (partial) reduced-motion block. (3) The harness: vitest.config.ts (happy-dom, globals, setup.ts polyfills for matchMedia/ResizeObserver), plus the ErrorBoundary and InsightsPanel component tests. Build/deploy: Vite with manual vendor chunking (three/echarts/motion/grid/vendor) and chunkSizeWarningLimit 900; build script runs `tsc && vite build`; test script `vitest run`; a multi-stage node:20-alpine → nginx:1.27-alpine Dockerfile; nginx.conf proxies /api/ to the backend Docker service, does SPA fallback, gzips text/js/json/svg (min 256B), and caches js/css/svg/woff(2) immutably for 1y. tsconfig is strict with noFallthroughCasesInSwitch but noUnusedLocals/Parameters disabled (which is why the unused fireEvent imports and unused spec.title don't error).

### `frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.ts`  _(151 lines)_

**Purpose:** Pure shape-transform layer that maps backend chart specs (from the recommendation engine) and forecast payloads into ECharts option objects. The backend pre-aggregates all data, so this module does zero numeric computation — it only restructures already-aggregated arrays into ECharts series/axis/visualMap config.
**Exports:** `interface ChartSpec`, `function specToOption(spec, muted, grid): EChartsOption`, `interface ForecastData`, `function forecastOption(fc, muted, grid): EChartsOption`
**Props/inputs:** specToOption(spec: ChartSpec, muted: string, grid: string). forecastOption(fc: ForecastData, muted: string, grid: string). muted = axis-label color, grid = axis-line/splitLine color, both passed in from the caller (theme tokens) rather than read from CSS here.
**State/refs:** None — stateless pure functions.
**Dependencies:** `import type { EChartsOption } from "echarts" (type-only import)`
**Data flow:** Input is a ChartSpec/ForecastData object that originates from the backend recommendation/forecast engine (API data), passed through as the `spec`/`fc` argument. Output is a plain ECharts option consumed by a chart wrapper component. No mock data, no API calls inside this file. Colors (muted/grid) flow in as props from the theme.
**Styling:** Hardcoded color literals and a 7-color PALETTE constant (line 18: #4d7cff,#a855f7,#22d3ee,#16c784,#f5a524,#ff4d5e,#6366f1). Fonts hardcoded as "JetBrains Mono". Tooltip background hardcoded rgba(11,14,26,0.92) and text #f2f5ff. These duplicate the CSS design tokens rather than reading them.

**Details:** axisStyle(muted, grid) (lines 20-26) is a shared helper returning axisLine/axisLabel/splitLine with dashed splitLine (`type: "dashed" as const`), label fontFamily JetBrains Mono fontSize 10. `base` (lines 29-33) sets grid margins {left:48,right:18,top:18,bottom:32}, item-trigger tooltip, and color PALETTE. switch on spec.chart_type: line (36-62) overrides tooltip to trigger:axis, xAxis category from d.map(p=>p.x) boundaryGap:false, single smooth line symbol:none width 2.5 color #4d7cff with vertical linear-gradient areaStyle from rgba(77,124,255,0.35) to 0.02. bar (63-72) axis tooltip, xAxis labels rotate 30deg only when d.length>6 (magic 6), itemStyle borderRadius [4,4,0,0] barMaxWidth 36 color #4d7cff. pie (73-89) donut radius [48%,72%] center [50%,44%] avoidLabelOverlap labels hidden bottom circle-icon legend data mapped to {name,value}. histogram (90-99) bars from d.map(p=>p.count) x labels hidden (axisLabel.show:false) color #a855f7 barCategoryGap 8%. heatmap (100-115) does NOT spread base (custom grid {left:70,right:18,top:10,bottom:60}), x and y both category from labels, xAxis rotate 45, visualMap min:-1 max:1 (assumes correlation domain) diverging ramp [#ff4d5e,#0b0e1a,#4d7cff], series data via labels.indexOf(c.x)/labels.indexOf(c.y) — O(n) indexOf per cell. default (116-117) returns bare base. forecastOption (130-150): xs = history x's then forecast x's; nHist=history length; pad(arr,before,after) prepends before nulls and appends after nulls to align on the shared category axis. Series: actual line padded (0,points.length); a two-series CI band trick — ci-lo invisible line stacked on key ci at p.lo, then ci-band stacked at (p.hi-p.lo) with areaStyle rgba(168,85,247,0.16) so fill sits between lo/hi; both CI series silent:true tooltip.show:false lineStyle.opacity 0; forecast dashed violet line padded (nHist,0). Legend lists only actual+forecast.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 13-15): ChartSpec.data is typed any[] and labels string[]?, so all p.x/p.y/p.label/p.value/p.bin/p.count/c.x/c.y/c.value accesses are unchecked any member accesses — type holes that defeat the strict TS config. A discriminated union per chart_type would catch shape mismatches at compile time.
