- _low_ (line 113): Heatmap series uses labels.indexOf(c.x)/labels.indexOf(c.y) inside a .map, O(n) per cell → O(n^3) for an n×n matrix; a precomputed index Map would make it O(n^2). If a cell's x/y is not in labels, indexOf returns -1 and ECharts silently misplaces/drops it (no validation).
- _low_ (line 18, 49, 70, 97, 104, 110, 146): Colors and tooltip bg/text are hardcoded hex/rgba duplicating index.css tokens (#4d7cff==--blue, #a855f7==--violet, #ff4d5e==--danger, rgba(11,14,26,..) is a legacy dark value). They will NOT follow html.light theme — charts stay dark-themed in light mode, and any token change must be edited in two places. Only muted/grid are themable via props.
- _nit_ (line 31, 104, 138): Tooltip backgroundColor rgba(11,14,26,0.92) is a legacy machine-world dark value with no token; in Porcelain (light) mode this tooltip is near-black — minor contrast/visual inconsistency.
- _nit_ (line 95): Histogram hides x-axis labels entirely (axisLabel.show:false) so bins are unlabeled on the axis; readers rely solely on the tooltip to identify a bin — a legibility/a11y tradeoff.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. `spec.title` is part of ChartSpec but never used inside specToOption (titles must be rendered by the wrapper). The unknown-type default returns bare base (no series).

### `frontend/src/lib/chartOptions.test.ts`  _(59 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vitest unit suite for chartOptions.ts. Verifies the option shape produced for line/pie/heatmap/unknown specs and for forecast payloads, including the null-padding alignment trick.
**Props/inputs:** N/A
**State/refs:** Module-level fixtures: muted=#888, grid=#222 (lines 4-5); fc ForecastData fixture (37-42) method holt, backtest_mape 12.3, 2 history points, 1 forecast point.
**Dependencies:** `vitest (describe,it,expect)`, `./chartOptions (specToOption, forecastOption, ChartSpec, ForecastData types)`
**Data flow:** All inputs are inline mock fixtures; outputs are cast to any and asserted with expect. No DOM, rendering, or API.
**Styling:** N/A

**Details:** specToOption block (7-34): (1) line test asserts series[0].type==='line', data===[10,20], xAxis.data===the x strings; (2) pie test asserts series[0].type==='pie' and data mapped to {name,value}; (3) heatmap test (data cast as any) asserts series[0].type==='heatmap' and visualMap defined; (4) unknown type 'mystery' asserts the fallback still has .color (base PALETTE). forecastOption block (36-58): (1) asserts series names include actual/forecast/ci-band and xAxis.data spans history+forecast x's; (2) asserts the forecast series' first history.length entries are all null (padding-before correctness). Every assertion casts the option to any to bypass the EChartsOption type.

**Issues:**
- _nit_ (line 10): Heavy use of : any casts on option objects forfeits type-level regression safety; a structural typo in the produced option would not fail the test as long as the asserted leaf still exists.
- _nit_ (line 44-52): Coverage gaps: bar/histogram branches are untested, and the ci-lo stacking / (hi-lo) math is not numerically verified — only the presence of the ci-band series name is checked.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. Bar and histogram switch branches of specToOption are never directly asserted (only line/pie/heatmap/default).

### `frontend/src/index.css`  _(728 lines)_

**Purpose:** Global stylesheet and design-token source of truth for the 'Dual World' design system v3 ('The Machine World × The Forensic Ledger'). Defines all CSS custom properties (color primitives, semantic colors, font stacks, radius scale), the reset, scrollbar, canonical ledger/paper components, legacy glass surfaces, machine-world CRT effects, the landing-page animation library, print styles, react-grid-layout overrides, and a reduced-motion block.
**Exports:** `:root custom properties act as the exported design tokens`
**Props/inputs:** N/A
**State/refs:** N/A
**Dependencies:** `Implicit external web fonts referenced by name but NOT @font-face/@import'd here: Fraunces, Space Grotesk, Inter, JetBrains Mono (must be loaded elsewhere, e.g. index.html). Falls back to Georgia/system-ui/ui-monospace if absent.`
**Data flow:** Pure styling. Tokens consumed via var(--x) by components and by chart wrappers (which pass colors in JS). html.light toggles light theme; .ledger / .ledger.uv toggle paper/UV worlds.
**Styling:** This IS the styling layer. Approach: CSS custom properties on :root, a light-mode override scoped to html.light, BEM-ish utility classes, and @keyframes-driven animations.

**Details:** CUSTOM PROPERTIES (:root, 7-57): Forensic Ledger — --paper #f3eee2, --paper-2 #ece5d3, --ink #14120b, --ink-soft #4a4434, --ledger-rule rgba(20,18,11,0.12), --stamp-red #c2331f, --seal-green #1c6e4a, --foil-gold #a8842c, --archival-blue #2b3a8c, --uv-cyan #7df9ff, --uv-night #191238. Machine World — --amber #ffb700, --amber-dim #7a5900, --machine-ground #050507, --machine-ground2 #07070b. App workspace (Dark Ledger default) — --bg #131110, --bg-2 #1b1916, --surface rgba(232,224,204,0.055), --surface-2 rgba(232,224,204,0.095), --border rgba(232,224,204,0.10), --text #e8dfc8, --text-muted #756b58. Semantic (chart/alert compat) — --danger #ff4d5e, --success #16c784, --blue #4d7cff, --violet #a855f7, --cyan #22d3ee, --indigo #6366f1, --signature linear-gradient(120deg,#4d7cff,#a855f7 55%,#22d3ee). FONT STACKS — --font-serif "Fraunces",Georgia,serif; --font-display "Space Grotesk",system-ui,sans-serif; --font-body "Inter",system-ui,sans-serif; --font-mono "JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace. RADIUS — none 0, sm 4px, md 8px, lg 12px, xl 18px. LIGHT MODE (html.light, 60-70) overrides bg/surface/border/text and remaps --danger→#c2331f and --success→#1c6e4a (ledger-toned) but NOT --blue/--violet/--cyan. RESET (72-82) universal margin/padding 0 box-sizing border-box; html scroll-behavior smooth; body uses tokens, antialiased, overflow-x hidden. CUSTOM CURSOR (84-87) @media(pointer:fine){body/a/button/[data-cursor] cursor:none} — hides native cursor, expects a JS custom cursor. SCROLLBAR (89-95) webkit-only 8px pill. LEDGER PANEL (97-116) .ledger-panel + legacy .glass share surface/border + Hard-Shadow 4px 4px 0 (no blur); html.light tones shadow; .glass keeps radius-lg. MACHINE WORLD (118-171) .crt-lines amber scanlines @0.022, .crt-vignette radial darken, @keyframes blink 1.1s step-end → .cursor-blink::after '▮', stream-down marquee, scan-pass + .scan-beam 4s amber sweep. FORENSIC LEDGER (173-475) .ledger ruled paper via repeating-linear-gradient 31/32px + ::selection foil-gold; .guilloche security band; .stamp distressed rubber stamp (double border, rotate -4deg, SVG feTurbulence mask, mask-size 120px) + green/gold variants; .redact ink bar ::after 0.9s cubic-bezier wipe, .lifted reverses origin; .ledger-label mono micro-label; .torn-edge polygon clip-path margin-top -18px; .microtext banknote strip; .chain-node 14px dot + .sealed gold glow + --dark variant; .wax-seal 92px radial seal; .paper-terminal/.exhibit-frame off-white hard-shadow panels; .hash-ticker mono green; .flip-wrap/.flip-inner/.flip-front/.flip-back 3D flip (perspective 1400px, preserve-3d, 0.8s, backface hidden); .handwriting italic serif rotate -1.2deg. UV MODE (371-409) .ledger.uv night gradient, .uv-ink transparent until revealed as glowing #7df9ff, .uv-toggle sticky bottom-right (top:calc(100vh-72px), margin-left:calc(100%-158px)). .loupe 150px magnifier; case props .tape (tl/tr rotated), .coffee-ring, .barcode bars; .sig-pad signature canvas + .sig-btn. PRINT (478-487) hides all, shows only #verita-report on white. RGL (489-508) placeholder stamp-red @0.12 square corners, custom resize-handle ::after chevron gold on hover. LANDING ANIMS (510-714) .glitch-text::before/::after via attr(data-text) danger/cyan two-window glitch keyframes over 5s; marquee-x 22s; m-stream vertical hex; float-y/pulse-ring/shimmer-gold; .foil-shimmer gold gradient clipped to text 3s; .chain-connector + .active::after scaleX fill; .evidence-card initial hidden/translated (JS reveals); .stat-number mono clamp(2.4rem,5vw,4rem); .impact-word overflow-clip reveal will-change; stat-rise clip-path; .fly-card/.landed transform reset; .land-cursor fixed mix-blend-mode difference (the custom cursor); ring-expand; char-land per-letter scatter using --cy/--cr with blur. REDUCED MOTION (716-728) kills animation/transition on * and named landing effects, forces .evidence-card visible.

**Issues:**
- _medium_ (line 84-87): cursor:none on body/links/buttons for all fine-pointer devices hides the native cursor globally, relying on a JS custom cursor (.land-cursor). If that JS fails to mount, errors, or isn't present on non-landing app routes, the user has NO visible cursor — a serious usability/a11y regression. The reduced-motion block does not restore the cursor either.
- _medium_ (line 716-723): Reduced-motion override is incomplete relative to the project's WCAG AAA bar. The universal *{animation:none;transition:none} catches element animations/transitions, but pseudo-element transitions are only re-stated for .redact::after — .chain-connector::after's scaleX transition is not explicitly listed, and the explicit selector list omits scan-pass/.scan-beam, blink/.cursor-blink, ring-expand/.ring, char-land/.char-anim, stat-rise/.stat-reveal. Worth auditing that every motion path is genuinely suppressed.
- _low_ (line 46-49): Four web fonts (Fraunces, Space Grotesk, Inter, JetBrains Mono) are referenced only by family name; no @font-face/@import here and no font-display strategy. If index.html doesn't load them the UI silently falls back to Georgia/system-ui/monospace, changing the whole forensic-ledger identity, with no FOUT/FOIT control expressed in CSS.
- _low_ (line 90-95): Scrollbar styling is webkit-only (::-webkit-scrollbar); Firefox gets the default — no scrollbar-width/scrollbar-color fallback, a cross-browser inconsistency.
- _low_ (line 36-43, 60-70): Comment says semantics are 'kept for chart/alert compat', but html.light only remaps --danger and --success, leaving --blue/--violet/--cyan/--indigo/--signature at dark-world values. Combined with chartOptions.ts hardcoding the same hexes, theming is split-brain: some semantics adapt to light mode, others (and all chart colors) do not.
- _low_ (line 480-487): Print rule uses body *{visibility:hidden} then reveals #verita-report; visibility:hidden still occupies layout so hidden ancestors can leave blank space and any position:fixed element outside the report tree can affect pagination. Also depends on an element with id verita-report existing — silent no-op if the report view isn't mounted.
- _nit_ (line 393-396): .uv-toggle anchors with top:calc(100vh-72px) and margin-left:calc(100%-158px) — magic offsets assuming a 158×72px button region; changing padding/text breaks the bottom-right anchoring. A position:fixed right/bottom would be more robust.
- _nit_ (line 119-139): Machine-world overlays (.crt-lines/.crt-vignette/.scan-beam) use position:absolute inset:0 and rely on a positioned ancestor; dropped into a static container they escape to the nearest positioned ancestor or viewport — a latent containing-block footgun.

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. DESIGN.md notes the dark aurora/glass system is legacy ('maintain, don't extend'); .glass here is the legacy compat surface re-skinned to the ledger aesthetic.

### `frontend/src/test/setup.ts`  _(14 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vitest global setup file (referenced by vitest.config.ts setupFiles). Extends expect with jest-dom matchers and polyfills two browser APIs the test environment may lack so components depending on them can render under test.
**Props/inputs:** N/A
**State/refs:** N/A
**Dependencies:** `@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest (registers toBeInTheDocument etc.)`
**Data flow:** Pure test-environment mutation: assigns window.matchMedia and globalThis.ResizeObserver.
**Styling:** N/A

**Details:** Line 1 imports vitest-flavored jest-dom matchers. Lines 4-8 stub window.matchMedia only if falsy — returns a fake MediaQueryList {matches:false, media:query, onchange:null, add/removeListener no-ops, add/removeEventListener no-ops, dispatchEvent:()=>false} cast as any; comment notes cursor/typewriter components read matchMedia. Lines 11-13 polyfill ResizeObserver (used by the ECharts wrapper) as a no-op class observe/unobserve/disconnect, only if not already defined. vitest.config.ts sets environment to happy-dom (not jsdom) which already provides both APIs, so the ||-guards short-circuit; the stubs keep the suite green only if the environment is switched to jsdom (jsdom is installed as a devDependency).

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 3-13): Comments reference jsdom ('jsdom doesn't implement matchMedia'), but vitest.config.ts uses environment:'happy-dom', which already implements both matchMedia and ResizeObserver — so the ||-guarded stubs never execute in the current config (dead/aspirational code or stale comments). jsdom is installed but unused by the vitest config; reconcile the comment vs. the actual environment.
- _nit_ (line 4): matchMedia stub always returns matches:false, so tests cannot exercise the matches:true branch (reduced-motion / dark-mode). A coverage ceiling for any media-query-dependent logic.

**Dead code / TODOs:** The matchMedia and ResizeObserver stubs are guarded with `|| existing` and the active environment (happy-dom) already provides both — so the stub bodies are effectively dead in the current config (aspirational/jsdom-oriented). Comments still reference jsdom.

### `frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.test.tsx`  _(26 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vitest + React Testing Library suite for the ErrorBoundary class component. Verifies it passes children through when healthy and renders the labeled fallback (with the error message and a 'Try again' button) when a child throws during render.
**Props/inputs:** N/A — exercises ErrorBoundary's children/label props.
**State/refs:** Module-local `spy` holding the console.error mock.
**Dependencies:** `vitest (describe,it,expect,vi,beforeAll,afterAll)`, `@testing-library/react (render,screen,fireEvent)`, `./ErrorBoundary (component under test)`
**Data flow:** All inputs are inline JSX; a Boom() component (lines 5-7) unconditionally throws new Error('kaboom') to trigger the boundary. console.error is spied/silenced so React's error logging doesn't pollute test output.
**Styling:** N/A

**Details:** beforeAll (line 11) mocks console.error to a no-op (vi.spyOn) and afterAll (12) restores it — needed because React logs caught render errors and the boundary's componentDidCatch also calls console.error. Test 1 (14-17): renders <ErrorBoundary><div>safe content</div></ErrorBoundary>, asserts 'safe content' present (healthy passthrough). Test 2 (19-24): renders with label='Studio' wrapping <Boom/>, asserts /Something went wrong/, the label+message /Studio: kaboom/, and the literal 'Try again' button. The 'Try again' reset behavior (clicking to clear state and re-render children) is NOT exercised.

**Issues:**
- _low_ (line 2): fireEvent imported but never used — dead import. The 'Try again' recovery branch (onClick → setState({error:null}) re-rendering children) is left uncovered, which is the most behaviorally interesting part of the boundary.
- _nit_ (line 19-24): Does not assert componentDidCatch logged with the '[Verita] error in Studio:' prefix, nor that re-rendering after a successful 'Try again' restores children — both untested.

**Dead code / TODOs:** fireEvent is imported (line 2) but never used — the 'Try again' recovery path appears intended to be tested but the assertion was never written.

### `frontend/src/components/studio/InsightsPanel.test.tsx`  _(25 lines)_

**Purpose:** Vitest + RTL suite for the InsightsPanel 'Key Findings' component. Verifies the executive summary and all finding texts render, and that each finding's evidence trail ('how was this computed?') is hidden by default and revealed on click — the auditability feature.
**Props/inputs:** N/A — exercises InsightsPanel's insights[] and summary props.
**State/refs:** Module-level `insights` fixture (lines 5-8): a 'comparison'/info insight with a scipy ttest evidence string and a 'quality'/warning insight with a nulls-count evidence string.
**Dependencies:** `vitest (describe,it,expect)`, `@testing-library/react (render,screen,fireEvent)`, `./InsightsPanel (default export + named type Insight)`
**Data flow:** Inline mock Insight[] fixtures + summary string passed as props; no API, no store. fireEvent.click drives the disclosure interaction.
**Styling:** N/A

**Details:** Test 1 (11-16): renders summary='A summary sentence.' + the two insights; asserts the summary and both finding texts (regex /HNW is significantly higher/, /country is 30% empty/) are present. Test 2 (18-23): asserts /ttest_ind/ initially NOT in document (queryByText → null), then clicks the FIRST 'how was this computed?' via screen.getAllByText('how was this computed?')[0] (getAllByText because each insight renders its own button), then asserts /ttest_ind/ becomes present — validating the per-row disclosure toggle (open===i?null:i) and the conditional {open===i && <motion.code>} render. The framer-motion height:'auto' animation is not asserted (only presence).

**Issues:**
- _nit_ (line 18-23): Only the open/reveal direction is tested; the toggle-closed path (clicking the same button again → open===i?null) and the single-open invariant (opening row B closes row A) are not asserted, leaving part of the accordion state machine uncovered.
- _nit_ (line 6): Fixture's info insight (severity:'info') silently exercises an undefined-token fallback in the component: InsightsPanel.tsx borderLeft for non-warning uses var(--accent,#12b5a3), but --accent is NOT defined in index.css :root, so info findings always render the hardcoded #12b5a3 fallback. (Reported against the component but surfaced via this test's fixture.)

**Dead code / TODOs:** No TODOs. The toggle-closed direction and single-open invariant of the accordion are not asserted.
